Function AfterSave()
	'******** makrodan sonra sil ***********
	'If Doc.DataEntryMode<>"GUI" Then Exit Function
	'****************************************
	Set BOME=Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
	Mamulkod=Trim(BOME.MAMULCODE)
	If Left(MamulKod,2)="40" Then
		Set doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK00","")
		Set S00=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")
		nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(MamulKod)
		Doc1.RunCommand("RUN_MODULE_PROC_NOCALC('CALC_OPTION1S')")
		nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
		If nret=0 Then
			'MsgBox Mamulkod &" kodlu �r�n�n stok kart� F/O listesi otomatik olarak g�ncellendi."
			nrr=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		Else
			'MsgBox Mamulkod &" kodlu �r�n�n stok kart� F/O listesi otomatik olarak g�ncellenemedi.L�tfen kontrol edin."
		End If
	End If
End Function


Function BeforeSave()
	'******** makrodan sonra sil ***********
	If Doc.DataEntryMode<>"GUI" Then Exit Function
	'****************************************
	Set BOMT=Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
	Set BOME=Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
	UAEM=BOME.MAMULMIKTAR	
	For i= 1 To BOMT.GetRecCount
		If Trim(BOMT.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i))="I" Then
			BOMT.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(i)=UAEM
		End If
	Next

End Function

Function OzelHakVarMi(HakKodu)

Public ustmargin
Function OnLogin()
	Dim Kullanici
	Kullanici = trim(ucase(Doc.Getd7UserName()))
	If Kullanici="STLM1" or Kullanici ="STLM2" or Kullanici ="URTMD" then 
		Call Geciken_Satinalmalar(Sk)
	End If	
End Function

Sub Geciken_Satinalmalar(Sk)
	Bugun = Doc.Bugun()
	Set S46T = Doc.Select1("STN","STOK46T","ACIKKAPALI='' & REV_RTESTARIH < '"+Bugun+"' ","CH_KODU",-1)
	Dim pe1,S46T 
	If S46T.GetRecCount>0 Then
		S46T.SORT("CH_KODU+KOD+<REV_RTESTARIH")
		Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
 		SatirNo = 0
		res = pe1.Open(0,"Stok Kontrol",297,210,"LANDSCAPE")
		Call SatinalmaSayfasi(pe1)
		For i = 1 to S46T.GetRowCount()
			Kodu = Trim(S46T.KOD(i)) 
			StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD",Kodu, "")
			NStok=Trim(S46T.NONSTOK_AD(i))
			SipMiktar = S46T.SF_MIKTAR(i) 
			KapananMiktar = S46T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i) 
			Bakiye = SipMiktar - KapananMiktar 
			SipTarih = S46T.TARIH(i) 
			TesTarih = S46T.REV_RTESTARIH(i) 
			FirmaKodu = S46T.CH_KODU(i) 
			Birim=Trim(S46T.SF_SF_UNIT(i))			
			FirmaAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD",FirmaKodu, "")
			SipNo=Trim(S46T.EVRAKNO(i))
			GunFarki = Doc.CDateToInt(Bugun) - Doc.CDateToInt(TesTarih)
			If SatirNo = 37 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
				Call SatinalmaSayfasi(pe1)
				SatirNo = 0
				End If		
				res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial",8)
				SatirNo = SatirNo + 1
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,6,5,30,i)
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,16,5,60,FirmaAdi)
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,76,5,55,SipNo)
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,87,5,20,Doc.StrToNorDate(SipTarih))
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,103,5,20,Doc.StrToNorDate(TesTarih))
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,119,5,55,StokAdi)
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,176,5,60,NStok&Trim(S46T.NOTE0(i)))
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,238,5,30,Doc.NumFormat(SipMiktar,12,0))
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,251,5,30,Doc.NumFormat(KapananMiktar,12,0))
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,264,5,30,Doc.NumFormat(Bakiye,12,0))
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,276,5,30,Birim)
				res = pe1.write(15+SatirNo*5,287,5,30,Gunfarki)

		Next
		res = pe1.Show()
		res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
	End If
End Sub

Sub Satinalma()

	YTarih = Doc.Date_NumToStr(Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.Bugun) + 5)	
	Tarih=Doc.StrToDate(YTarih)
	Dim pe1,RS_STOK46T 
	Set RS_STOK46T = Doc.Select1("VXXCVXCV","STOK46T","ACIKKAPALI<>'K'&REV_RTESTARIH<'"&Tarih&"' ","",-1)
	If RS_STOK46T.GetRecCount>0 Then
		Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
		Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add	
		Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets.Add
		Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value="Sipari� No"
		Sh1.Cells(1,2).Value="Malzeme Kodu"
		Sh1.Cells(1,3).Value="Malzeme Ad�"
		Sh1.Cells(1,4).Value="Sipari� Miktar�"
		Sh1.Cells(1,5).Value="Gelen Miktar"
		Sh1.Cells(1,6).Value="Bakiye Miktar"
		Sh1.Cells(1,7).Value="Teslim Tarihi"
'		Sh1.Cells(1,8).Value="Gecikme"
		Sh1.Cells(1,8).Value="Tedarik�i"
		For i = 1 to RS_STOK46T.GetRowCount()
			ENo=Trim(RS_STOK46T.EVRAKNO(i))
			Kodu = RS_STOK46T.KOD(i) 
			StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD",Kodu, "")
			SipMiktar = RS_STOK46T.SF_MIKTAR(i) 
			KapananMiktar = RS_STOK46T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i) 
			Bakiye = SipMiktar - KapananMiktar 
			TesTarih = RS_STOK46T.REV_RTESTARIH(i) 
			FirmaKodu = RS_STOK46T.CH_KODU(i) 
			FirmaAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD",FirmaKodu, "")
			Tel = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "TELEFONNO_1",FirmaKodu, "")
			GunFarki = Doc.CDateToInt(Bugun) - Doc.CDateToInt(TesTarih)
			Sh1.Cells(i+1,1).Value=ENo
			Sh1.Cells(i+1,2).Value=Kodu
			Sh1.Cells(i+1,3).Value=StokAdi
			Sh1.Cells(i+1,4).Value=SipMiktar
			Sh1.Cells(i+1,5).Value=KapananMiktar
			Sh1.Cells(i+1,6).Value=Bakiye
			Sh1.Cells(i+1,7).Value=tesTarih
'			Sh1.Cells(i+1,8).Value=GunFarki
			Sh1.Cells(i+1,8).Value=firmaAdi
		Next
		Excel.Visible=True
	End If
End Sub

Sub GuvenlikStok()

	Set S00=Doc.Select1("_TEMP","STOK00","","",-1)
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add	
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value="Malzeme Kodu"
	Sh1.Cells(1,2).Value="Malzeme Ad�"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Value="G�venlik Sto�u"
	Sh1.Cells(1,4).Value="Hm Depo Mevcudu"
	Sh1.Cells(1,5).Value="Eksik Miktar"
	Sh1.Cells(1,6).Value="Birimi"
	s=1
	for i= 1 To S00.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S00.KOD(i))
		GStok=S00.B_GUVENLIKSTOGU(i)
		HmSerbestMiktar=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(Skod,"HM","")
		If GStok<>0 Then
			Set S01=Doc.Select1("_TEMSS","STOK01","KOD='"&Skod&"'&AMBCODE='HM'&ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='I2'","",-1)
			S01.GROUPBY("KOD")
			RMiktar=S01.SF_MIKTAR
			Fark=(HmSerbestMiktar+RMiktar)-GStok
			If Fark<0 Then
				s=s+1
				Sh1.Cells(s,1).Value=Skod
				Sh1.Cells(s,2).Value=Trim(S00.AD(i))
				Sh1.Cells(s,3).Value=GStok
				Sh1.Cells(s,4).Value=HmserbestMiktar
				Sh1.Cells(s,5).Value=Fark
				Sh1.Cells(s,6).Value=S00.IUNIT(i)
			End IF
		End If
	Next
	Excel.Visible=True
End Sub

Function BeforeSave()

	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Function

	Set MPSTM=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	For i= 1 To MPSTM.GetRecCount
		MamulKodu=Trim(MPSTM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i))
		If Left(MamulKodu,2)<>"40" Then
			If Trim(MPSTM.VRI_NUM1(I))="" Then
				Set BOM=Doc.Select1("xcvcgb","BOMU01E","MAMULCODE='"&MamulKodu&"'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'","",-1)
				If BOM.GetRecCount>0 and Trim(BOM.GK_1)<>"" Then
					MPSTM.VRI_NUM1(I)=Doc.RightJustify("REV-"&Trim(BOM.GK_1),10)
				End If 
			End If
		End If
	Next
	
	Set MPSE=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MPST=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
'	For i= 1 To MPST.GetRecCount
'		If MPST.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i)="I" Then
'			TesTarih=	MPST.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(i)
'			Exit For		
'		End If
'	Next
'	If TesTarih<>"" Then
'		MPSE.REFTARIH1=TesTarih
'    09/05/2018 tarihli toplant�da Mehmet bey ve M�cahit Bey'in talebi �zerine a��lan g�ne�ekildi.
		If MPSE.REFTARIH1="" Then
			MPSE.REFTARIH1=Doc.Bugun
		End If
'	End If
If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
	For i= 1 To MPST.GetRecCount
		KaynakKodu=Trim(MPST.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i))
		If KaynakKodu="I" and Trim(MPST.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(i))="" then
			say=say+1
		End If
	Next
	If say>0 Then
		MsgBox "Biti� tarihi belirtilmemi� proses sat�r(lar)� var. L�tfen kontrol ediniz."
'		BeforeSave=-1
	End If
End If
	MamulKod=Trim(MPSE.MAMULSTOKKODU)
	Miktar=MPSE.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
	Paket=MPSE.SF_PAKETSAYISI
	If Left(MamulKod,5)="30 18" or Left(MamulKod,5)="46 18" Then
		If MPSTM.GetRecCount=0 Then
			satir=MPSTM.AddRow
			MPSTM.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			MPSTM.MAMULSTOKKODU(satir)=MamulKod
			MPSTM.SF_PAKETSAYISI(satir)=Paket
			MPSTM.SF_PAKETICERIGI(satir)=Miktar

		End If

Sub AlanBilgileriniDoldur()

	Set QVAL2T=Doc.GetTableObject("QVAL02T")
	For i= 1 To QVAL2T.GetRecCount
		vKod=Trim(QVAL2T.QS_VARCODE(i))
		Alantipi=Trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("QVALSC","AD_L2",vKod,-1))
		If AlanTipi="N" Then
			QVAL2T.QS_VARTYPE(i)="N"
			QVAL2T.QS_VARLEN(i)="12"
			QVAL2T.QS_VARSIG(i)="3"
		ElseIF AlanTipi="T" Then
			QVAL2T.QS_VARTYPE(i)="T"
			QVAL2T.QS_VARLEN(i)="10"
		ElseIF AlanTipi="D" Then
			QVAL2T.QS_VARTYPE(i)="D"
			QVAL2T.QS_VARLEN(i)="10"
		End If
		QVAL2T.REFTEXT01(i)="UYGUN"
	Next

End Sub


Sub AritmetikOrtalamaBul()
	Set QVAL2T=Doc.GetTableObject("QVAL02T")
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "QVAL02T")
	For i= 1 To QVAL2T.GetRecCount
		For j= 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			Olcum = QVAL2T.GetFieldValue("QS_DVALUE_00"&j, i)
			If Olcum>0 Then
				sayi=sayi+1
			End If
			Toplam=Toplam+Olcum
			Ortalama=Doc.Div(Toplam,Sayi)
			If Ortalama>0 and Olcum>0 Then	
				QVAL2T.SetFieldValue "ARITMETIC_MEAN",i,Doc.NumFormat(Ortalama,12,3)
			End If
		Next
		sayi=0
		Toplam=0
		Ortalama=0
	Next
End sub

'-------------------------------------
Function BeforeCommand(cmd)
	If cmd = "RUN_MODULE_PROC_NOCALC('CALC_STM_DETAILS')" Then
		Set  FRMSTACKKRTHESAPLAMA = Doc.GetTableObject("FRM_STACK_KRT_HESAPLAMA")
		
		If FRMSTACKKRTHESAPLAMA.BAZFIYAT = "E" Then
			Doc.d7Msgbox "[ Operasyonlar i�in Baz Fiyat �ncelikli Olsun ] Kutusu Se�ilmi�. " & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Stok Kart�ndaki ( Fiyat-1) Alan� Dolu Olan Stok Kodlar� i�in Operasyon Maliyet De�eri Bu Alandaki Fiyat Dikkate Al�nacakt�r.", "Uyar�", MB_OK
		End If
	End If
End Function
'-------------------------------------
Function AfterCommand(cmd)

	If cmd = "RUN_MODULE_PROC_NOCALC('CALC_STM_DETAILS')" Then
		Set  FRMSTACKKRTHESAPLAMA = Doc.GetTableObject("FRM_STACK_KRT_HESAPLAMA")
		
		If FRMSTACKKRTHESAPLAMA.BAZFIYAT = "E" Then	
			Set  SCHKTP = Doc.GetTableObject("SCCHECKTP")

			For i = 1 To SCHKTP.GetRecCount
				StokKoduVal = SCHKTP.KOD(i)
				StkFiyat1Val = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_OR_FIYAT_1", StokKoduVal, "")
				HMMaliyetVal = SCHKTP.STM_TB_H(i)
				EnvanterMiktarVal = SCHKTP.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				ToplamOperasyonMaliyetVal = EnvanterMiktarVal * StkFiyat1Val

				If StkFiyat1Val > 0 Then
					SCHKTP.STM_TB_I(i) = ToplamOperasyonMaliyetVal
					SCHKTP.STM(i) = ToplamOperasyonMaliyetVal + HMMaliyetVal
					SCHKTP.ZU_SCCHECKTP_BAZFIYAT(i) = StkFiyat1Val
				End If
			Next
		End If
	End If
End Function

	If KayitSayisi > 0 Then
		For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
			Set rs_S00OC = Doc.Select1("__3", "STOK00OC", "KOD='" & StkKodVal & "' & FEATURENO = '"& Trim(T.FEATURENO) & "' & OPTION1='"& rs_BOMU01T.BOMREC_FO_OPTION(i) &"' ", "", -1)
			If rs_S00OC.GetRecCount > 0 Then
				T.OPTION1 = rs_BOMU01T.BOMREC_FO_OPTION(i)

				Exit For
			End If
			Set rs_S00OC = Nothing
		Next
	End If

	Set rs_BOMU01T = Nothing
	Set T40T = Nothing

	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Table "SCFOEN","OPTION1"
	'Doc.CallFieldCalc_Table "SCFOEN","FEATURENO"
End Sub

Function CIHAZKARTNO_AfterValidate
	Set Srv=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVS00")
	KartNo=Trim(Srv.CIHAZKARTNO)
	If KartNo<>"" Then
		GarantiBitisTarihi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("SRVSC0","GARANTIBITISTARIH",KartNo,"")
		Srv.REFTARIH1=GarantiBitisTarihi
	End If
End Function

Function BeforeSave()

	Set SRV=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVS00")
	Call FaturaKontrol()
	If Trim(SRV.GK_4)="E" Then
		Call ClaimOlustur()
	End If
	CihazNo=Trim(SRV.CIHAZKARTNO)
	Set CHZ=Doc.Select1("dfssdf","SRVSC0","KOD='"&CihazNo&"'","",-1)
	StokKodu=Trim(CHZ.STOKKODU)
	IF Trim(SRV.STOKKODU)="" Then
		SRV.STOKKODU=Stokkodu
		SRV.STOKADI=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","AD",Stokkodu,"")
		SRV.SERINO=CihazNo
	End IF
	'If Trim(SRV.STOKKODU)="" Then
	'	MsgBox "Stok kodu bo� olamaz."
	'	BeforeSave=-1
	'End If
	CariKod=Trim(SRV.FIRMA)
	CariGrup=Trim(doc.getDbFieldValue("CARI00","GK_9",CariKod,""))
	If CariGrup="TYI" and Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" and SRV.ACIKKAPALI<>"K" Then	
		BorcBakiyesi = Doc.CariHesapToplamlariBA(CariKod, "BB")
		If BorcBakiyesi>0 Then
			k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Bu m��teriye ait bor� bakiyesi :" & BorcBakiyesi & " Fi� kaydedilsin mi?"  , "Dinamo ERP", MB_YESNO)
			If k=7 then				
				BeforeSave =-1
				Exit Function
			End If
		End If
	End If

End Function

Sub FaturaKontrol()
	Set SRV0=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVS00")
	Set SRVI=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVS00I")
	Set SRVM=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVS00M")
	If Trim(SRV0.REFTEXT02)<>"" Then
		For i= 1 To SRVI.GetRecCount
			SRVI.REFTEXT02(i)="E"
		Next
		For i= 1 To SRVM.GetRecCount
			SRVM.REFTEXT02(i)="E"
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub ClaimOlustur()
	Set SRV0=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVS00")
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("SRVS0C","")
	Set S0C=Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVS0C")
	nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(SRV0.KOD)
	S0C.AD=SRV0.UNVAN1	
	nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	If nret=0 Then
		nre=Doc1.Loadempty_Voucher()
	end If
End Sub

Sub AFTERNOTIFICATION(ByVal Notification_Type)
	If Notification_Type = "AFTER_DOCUMENT_INIT_S" Then 
		Set LSE = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTEE")
		LSE.Addfield2 "SERINO1", "CHAR", 20, 0, "L", "", ""
		LSE.Addfield2 "SERINO2", "CHAR", 20, 0, "L", "", ""
		LSE.Addfield2 "STKKOD", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "F2=DO_KBRWD('STOK00')", ""
		LSE.Addfield2 "STKAD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
		LSE.Addfield2 "STKGK3", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L", "", "STKGK3"
		LSE.Addfield2 "FOKOD", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L",  "F2=DO_BRW('FOSYSFT')", ""
		LSE.Addfield2 "GARANTI", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L",  "", "!GARANTI"
		LSE.Addfield2 "SERVISGK2", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L",  "", "SRVSGK2"
		LSE.Addfield2 "MUSTERI", "CHAR", 16, 0, "L", "F2=DO_BRW_1('CARI00')", ""
		LSE.Addfield2 "FATURABAGLI", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""

		LSE.GARANTI = "E"

		Call CreateSQLTable_Detay
		Call CreateSQLTable_Ozet
		Call CreateSQLTable_Ozet_STKGK3
		Call CreateSQLTable_Ozet_STOK_AD
	End If
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub CreateSQLTable_Detay
	SQL = "SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    s.KOD AS SERVISFISNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    s.TARIH AS SERVISTARIHI," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    s.FIRMA AS SERVISYAPILANFIRMA," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    c1.AD AS SATISFATCARIAD," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    s.CIHAZKARTNO AS SERVISCIHAZNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    s.GARANTILIMI," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    sc.SERINO AS SERVISSERINO," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    sc.STOKKODU AS TANIMLIPARCAKODU," & vbCrLf

	SQL = SQL & "    a.EVRAKNO AS SATISFAT_EVRAKNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.CH_KODU AS SATISFAT_CARIKOD," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.KOD AS SATISFAT_STKKODU," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.STOK_AD AS SATISFAT_STKADI," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.STKGK3 AS SATISFAT_STKGK3," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.SERINO AS SATISFAT_SERINO," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD AS SATISFAT_FOKOD, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD1 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD1, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD2 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD2, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD3 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD3, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD4 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD4, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD5 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD5, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD6 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD6, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD7 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD7, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD8 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD8, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD9 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD9, " & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    a.FOKOD_AD10 AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD10 " & vbCrLf

	SQL = SQL & "FROM dbo.SRVS00 AS s" & vbCrLf

	SQL = SQL & "LEFT JOIN dbo.CARI00 AS c1 ON c1.KOD = s.FIRMA" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "LEFT JOIN dbo.SRVSC0 AS sc ON sc.KOD = s.CIHAZKARTNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "LEFT JOIN dbo.STOK70T_ALL AS a" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    ON RTRIM(a.SERINO) = RTRIM(sc.SERINO)" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "   AND RTRIM(a.CH_KODU) = RTRIM(s.FIRMA)" & vbCrLf

	SQL = SQL & "        WHERE 1=0 " & vbCrLf
	
	Set SVS00SQLDETAY = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SVS00_SQL_DETAY", SQL)
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------
Sub CreateSQLTable_Ozet

	SQL = "SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    q.gK AS SATISFAT_STKKODU," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    q.gS AS SATISFAT_STKADI," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    q.gF AS SATISFAT_FOKOD," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    q.gT AS SATISFAT_STKGK3," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.EVRAKNO) AS SATISFAT_EVRAKNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.SERINO) AS SATISFAT_SERINO," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD1) AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD1," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD2) AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD2," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD3) AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD3," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD4) AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD4," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD5) AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD5," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD6) AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD6," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD7) AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD7," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD8) AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD8," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD9)AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD9," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.FOKOD_AD10) AS SATISFAT_FOKOD_AD10," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    CAST(SUM(ISNULL(q.SF, 0)) AS decimal(18, 0)) AS TOPLAM_SF_MIKTAR," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    CAST(MAX(ISNULL(q.fis_cnt, 0)) AS int) AS SERVIS_FIS_SAYISI" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "FROM (" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        l.gK, l.gS, l.gF, l.gT," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        l.KaynakDb, l.CH_KODU, l.KOD, l.SERINO, l.FOKOD, l.EVRAKNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        l.SF," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        l.FOKOD_AD1, l.FOKOD_AD2, l.FOKOD_AD3, l.FOKOD_AD4, l.FOKOD_AD5," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        l.FOKOD_AD6, l.FOKOD_AD7, l.FOKOD_AD8, l.FOKOD_AD9, l.FOKOD_AD10," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        ISNULL(f.fis_cnt, 0) AS fis_cnt" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    FROM (" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'') AS gK," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'') AS gS," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'') AS gF," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'') AS gT," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            a.KaynakDb, a.CH_KODU, a.KOD, a.SERINO, a.FOKOD, a.EVRAKNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(ISNULL(a.SF_MIKTAR, 0)) AS SF," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD1) AS FOKOD_AD1," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD2) AS FOKOD_AD2," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD3) AS FOKOD_AD3," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD4) AS FOKOD_AD4," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD5) AS FOKOD_AD5," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD6) AS FOKOD_AD6," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD7) AS FOKOD_AD7," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD8) AS FOKOD_AD8," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "MAX(a.FOKOD_AD9) AS FOKOD_AD9," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD10) AS FOKOD_AD10" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        FROM dbo.SRVS00 AS s" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.SRVSC0 AS sc ON sc.KOD = s.CIHAZKARTNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.STOK70T_ALL AS a" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            ON RTRIM(a.SERINO) = RTRIM(sc.SERINO)" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "           AND RTRIM(a.CH_KODU) = RTRIM(s.FIRMA)" & vbCrLf

	SQL = SQL & "        WHERE 1=0 " & vbCrLf

	SQL = SQL & "        GROUP BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            a.KaynakDb, a.CH_KODU, a.KOD, a.SERINO, a.FOKOD, a.EVRAKNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    ) AS l" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    LEFT JOIN (" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'') AS gK2," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'') AS gS2," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'') AS gF2," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'') AS gT2," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COUNT(DISTINCT s2.KOD) AS fis_cnt" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        FROM dbo.SRVS00 AS s2" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.SRVSC0 AS sc2 ON sc2.KOD = s2.CIHAZKARTNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.STOK70T_ALL AS a2" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            ON RTRIM(a2.SERINO) = RTRIM(sc2.SERINO)" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "           AND RTRIM(a2.CH_KODU) = RTRIM(s2.FIRMA)" & vbCrLf

	SQL = SQL & "        WHERE 1=0 " & vbCrLf

	SQL = SQL & "        GROUP BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'')" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    ) AS f" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        ON f.gK2 = l.gK AND f.gS2 = l.gS AND f.gF2 = l.gF AND f.gT2 = l.gT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & ") AS q" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "GROUP BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    q.gK, q.gS, q.gF, q.gT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "ORDER BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(q.fis_cnt) DESC, q.gK, q.gS, q.gF, q.gT"

	Set SVS00SQLOZET = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SVS00_SQL_OZET", SQL)
End Sub

Sub CreateSQLTable_Ozet_STKGK3
	SQL_Q = "    SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.gK, l.gS, l.gF, l.gT," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.KaynakDb, l.CH_KODU, l.KOD, l.SERINO, l.FOKOD, l.EVRAKNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.SF," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.FOKOD_AD1, l.FOKOD_AD2, l.FOKOD_AD3, l.FOKOD_AD4, l.FOKOD_AD5," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.FOKOD_AD6, l.FOKOD_AD7, l.FOKOD_AD8, l.FOKOD_AD9, l.FOKOD_AD10," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        ISNULL(f.fis_cnt, 0) AS fis_cnt" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "    FROM (" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'') AS gK," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'') AS gS," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'') AS gF," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'') AS gT," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            a.KaynakDb, a.CH_KODU, a.KOD, a.SERINO, a.FOKOD, a.EVRAKNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(ISNULL(a.SF_MIKTAR, 0)) AS SF," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD1) AS FOKOD_AD1," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD2) AS FOKOD_AD2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD3) AS FOKOD_AD3," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD4) AS FOKOD_AD4," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD5) AS FOKOD_AD5," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD6) AS FOKOD_AD6," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD7) AS FOKOD_AD7," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD8) AS FOKOD_AD8," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD9) AS FOKOD_AD9," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD10) AS FOKOD_AD10" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        FROM dbo.SRVS00 AS s" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.SRVSC0 AS sc ON sc.KOD = s.CIHAZKARTNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "LEFT JOIN dbo.STOK70T_ALL AS a" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            ON RTRIM(a.SERINO) = RTRIM(sc.SERINO)" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "           AND RTRIM(a.CH_KODU) = RTRIM(s.FIRMA)" & vbCrLf

	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        WHERE 1=0 " & vbCrLf

	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        GROUP BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            a.KaynakDb, a.CH_KODU, a.KOD, a.SERINO, a.FOKOD, a.EVRAKNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "    ) AS l" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "    LEFT JOIN (" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'') AS gK2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'') AS gS2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'') AS gF2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'') AS gT2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COUNT(DISTINCT s2.KOD) AS fis_cnt" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        FROM dbo.SRVS00 AS s2" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.SRVSC0 AS sc2 ON sc2.KOD = s2.CIHAZKARTNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.STOK70T_ALL AS a2" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            ON RTRIM(a2.SERINO) = RTRIM(sc2.SERINO)" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "           AND RTRIM(a2.CH_KODU) = RTRIM(s2.FIRMA)" & vbCrLf

	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        WHERE 1=0 " & vbCrLf

	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        GROUP BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'')" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "    ) AS f" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        ON f.gK2 = l.gK AND f.gS2 = l.gS AND f.gF2 = l.gF AND f.gT2 = l.gT" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & ") AS q" & vbCrLf

	SQL = "SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    lin.gT AS SATISFAT_STKGK3," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    CAST(SUM(lin.SF_SUM) AS decimal(18, 0)) AS TOPLAM_SF_MIKTAR," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    CAST(SUM(lin.FIS_MAX) AS int) AS SERVIS_FIS_SAYISI" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "FROM (" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        q.gT," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        q.gK," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        q.gS," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        q.gF," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        CAST(SUM(ISNULL(q.SF, 0)) AS decimal(18, 0)) AS SF_SUM,"& vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        CAST(MAX(ISNULL(q.fis_cnt, 0)) AS int) AS FIS_MAX" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    FROM (" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & SQL_Q
	SQL = SQL & "    GROUP BY q.gT, q.gK, q.gS, q.gF" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & ") AS lin" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "GROUP BY lin.gT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "ORDER BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    SERVIS_FIS_SAYISI DESC, SATISFAT_STKGK3"

	Set SVS00SQLOZETSTKGK3 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SVS00_SQL_OZET_STKGK3", SQL)

End Sub

Sub CreateSQLTable_Ozet_STOK_AD
	SQL_Q = "    SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.gK, l.gS, l.gF, l.gT," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.KaynakDb, l.CH_KODU, l.KOD, l.SERINO, l.FOKOD, l.EVRAKNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.SF," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.FOKOD_AD1, l.FOKOD_AD2, l.FOKOD_AD3, l.FOKOD_AD4, l.FOKOD_AD5," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        l.FOKOD_AD6, l.FOKOD_AD7, l.FOKOD_AD8, l.FOKOD_AD9, l.FOKOD_AD10," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "ISNULL(f.fis_cnt, 0) AS fis_cnt" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "    FROM (" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'') AS gK," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'') AS gS," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'') AS gF," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'') AS gT," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            a.KaynakDb, a.CH_KODU, a.KOD, a.SERINO, a.FOKOD, a.EVRAKNO," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(ISNULL(a.SF_MIKTAR, 0)) AS SF," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD1) AS FOKOD_AD1," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD2) AS FOKOD_AD2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD3) AS FOKOD_AD3," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD4) AS FOKOD_AD4," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD5) AS FOKOD_AD5," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD6) AS FOKOD_AD6," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD7) AS FOKOD_AD7," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD8) AS FOKOD_AD8," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD9) AS FOKOD_AD9," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            MAX(a.FOKOD_AD10) AS FOKOD_AD10" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        FROM dbo.SRVS00 AS s" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.SRVSC0 AS sc ON sc.KOD = s.CIHAZKARTNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.STOK70T_ALL AS a" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            ON RTRIM(a.SERINO) = RTRIM(sc.SERINO)" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "           AND RTRIM(a.CH_KODU) = RTRIM(s.FIRMA)" & vbCrLf

	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        WHERE 1=0 " & vbCrLf

	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        GROUP BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            a.KaynakDb, a.CH_KODU, a.KOD, a.SERINO, a.FOKOD, a.EVRAKNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "    ) AS l" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "    LEFT JOIN (" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'') AS gK2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'') AS gS2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'') AS gF2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'') AS gT2," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COUNT(DISTINCT s2.KOD) AS fis_cnt" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        FROM dbo.SRVS00 AS s2" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.SRVSC0 AS sc2 ON sc2.KOD = s2.CIHAZKARTNO" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        LEFT JOIN dbo.STOK70T_ALL AS a2" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            ON RTRIM(a2.SERINO) = RTRIM(sc2.SERINO)" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "           AND RTRIM(a2.CH_KODU) = RTRIM(s2.FIRMA)" & vbCrLf

	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        WHERE 1=0 " & vbCrLf

	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        GROUP BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.KOD AS nvarchar(100))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STOK_AD AS nvarchar(200))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.FOKOD AS nvarchar(30))), N'')," & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "            COALESCE(RTRIM(CAST(a2.STKGK3 AS nvarchar(20))), N'')" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "    ) AS f" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & "        ON f.gK2 = l.gK AND f.gS2 = l.gS AND f.gF2 = l.gF AND f.gT2 = l.gT" & vbCrLf
	SQL_Q = SQL_Q & ") AS q" & vbCrLf

	SQL = "SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    lin.gS AS SATISFAT_STKADI," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    CAST(SUM(lin.SF_SUM) AS decimal(18, 0)) AS TOPLAM_SF_MIKTAR," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    CAST(SUM(lin.FIS_MAX) AS int) AS SERVIS_FIS_SAYISI" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "FROM (" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    SELECT" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        q.gT," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        q.gK," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        q.gS," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        q.gF," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        CAST(SUM(ISNULL(q.SF, 0)) AS decimal(18, 0)) AS SF_SUM," & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "        CAST(MAX(ISNULL(q.fis_cnt, 0)) AS int) AS FIS_MAX" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    FROM (" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & SQL_Q
	SQL = SQL & "    GROUP BY q.gT, q.gK, q.gS, q.gF" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & ") AS lin" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "GROUP BY lin.gS" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "ORDER BY" & vbCrLf
	SQL = SQL & "    SERVIS_FIS_SAYISI DESC, SATISFAT_STKADI"

	Set SVS00SQLOZETSTOKADI = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SVS00_SQL_OZET_STOKADI", SQL)
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : MAKR8S
Makro Kodu : SRVS00-1
Hangi Tablo icin : SRVS00M

Function STOKKODU_AfterValidate()

	Set SRV=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVS00")
	Set SRM=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVS00M")

	FiyatList=Trim(SRV.REFTEXT01)
	If Trim(SRV.REFTEXT01)="" Then
		MsgBox "Fiyat listesi numaras� girilmemi�.Fiyat hesaplanmayacak."
		Exit Function
	End If
	If FiyatList<>"" Then
		For i= 1 To SRM.GetRecCount
			Skod=Trim(SRM.STOKKODU(i))
			Set S48T=Doc.Select1("11sfsdf","STOK48T","KOD='"&Skod&"'&EVRAKNO='"&FiyatList&"'","",-1)
			If S48T.GetRecCount>0 then
				SRM.OR_FIYAT(i)=S48T.PRICE
				SRM.PARABIRIMI(i)=S48T.PRICEUNIT
			End If
		Next
	End If
End Function

Function REFNUM01_AfterValidate()
	Set SRM=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVS00M")
	For i= 1 To SRM.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(SRM.STOKKODU(i))
		If Skod<>"" and  SRM.OR_FIYAT(i)>0 then
			Oran=Doc.Div(SRM.REFNUM01(i),100)
			IskTutar=SRM.OR_TUTAR(i)*Oran
			SRM.REFNUM02(i)=SRM.OR_TUTAR(i)-Isktutar			
		End If
	Next
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Sub AfterSave
	Set S00=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	StokKoduVal = S00.KOD
	EkrandakiGK7Val = Trim(S00.GK_7)
	GK1Val = Trim(S00.GK_1)

	If S00.CREA_TARIH >= "2026/04/14" Then 
		'********************* kalite b�l�m� i�in ***************************

		If GK1Val = "D��ER" Or  GK1Val = "TICARI" Or GK1Val = "�� G�V." or Left(StokKoduVal, 2) = "58" Then
		Else
			MailDurumu = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKoduVal, "MAIL_SEND2", 0)
	
			If Trim(MailDurumu) <> "OK" Then
				If EkrandakiGK7Val = "KK" and S00.KART_TIPI <> "U"  Then
					Call MailAt("KKTanimiOnay", Trim(StokKoduVal))
				End If
			End If
		End If
	
		If EkrandakiGK7Val = "KKYOK" or  EkrandakiGK7Val = "" Then
			KayitliGK7Degeri = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKoduVal, "OLDGK7",0))
		
			If KayitliGK7Degeri = "KK" Then
	
				nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "STOK00", StokKoduVal)   
				Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")  
	
				K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("OLDGK7", -1, EkrandakiGK7Val)   
	
				If EkrandakiGK7Val = "" Then
					K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("KKYOKWHY", -1, "")  
				End If
	
				T_U1.Exec "SAVE"
	
				Call MailAt("KKYOKTanimli", Trim(StokKoduVal))
			End If
		Else
			nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "STOK00", StokKoduVal)   
			Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")  
	
			K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("KKYOKWHY", -1, "")  
	
			T_U1.Exec "SAVE"
		End If
	End If

	'********************* sat�� b�l�m� i�in ***************************
	If Left(StokKoduVal, 1) = "7" Then
		MailDurumu = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKoduVal, "MAIL_SEND", 0)

		If Trim(MailDurumu) <> "OK" Then
			Call MailAt("YediliKodAcildi", Trim(S00.KOD))
		End If

	ElseIf Left(StokKoduVal, 2) = "40" Then
		MailDurumu = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKoduVal, "MAIL_SEND", 0)

		If Trim(MailDurumu) <> "OK" Then
			Call MailAt("KirkliKodAcildi", Trim(S00.KOD))
		End If
	End If

End Sub
'-----------------------------------
Function BeforeSave()
	SetS00=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	StokKoduVal = S00.KOD
	EkrandakiGK7Val = Trim(S00.GK_7)
	GK1Val = Trim(S00.GK_1)
	KayitliGK7Degeri = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKoduVal, "OLDGK7",0))

	If GK1Val = "D��ER" Or  GK1Val = "TICARI" Or GK1Val = "�� G�V." Then
	Else
		If EkrandakiGK7Val = ""  or EkrandakiGK7Val = "KK" Then
			If EkrandakiGK7Val = "" Then msgbox "GK-7 Alan� Bo� B�rak�lm��. Kontrol Ediniz."

			nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "STOK00", StokKoduVal)   
			Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")  

			K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("OLDGK7", -1, EkrandakiGK7Val)   

			T_U1.Exec "SAVE"

		ElseIf EkrandakiGK7Val = "KKYOK" AND KayitliGK7Degeri = "KK" Then
		
			KKYOKSebebi = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKoduVal, "KKYOKWHY", 0))

			If KKYOKSebebi = "" Then
				msg= "[ KKYOK ] Se�ilmi�. KTA B�l�m�nde KKYOK Se�me Sebebi Girilmemi�." 
				strwhy = inputbox(msg,"Uyar�","")

				If Trim(strwhy) <> "" Then
					nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "STOK00", StokKoduVal)   
					Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")  
			
					K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("KKYOKWHY", -1, strwhy)  
			
					T_U1.Exec "SAVE"
				End If
			End If
	
		End If
		
		Set S00DB = Nothing
	End If


exit Function
	Set S00=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	If Trim(S00.DEFAULT_LOCATION1)="" and Trim(S00.DEFAULT_LOCATION1)="" Then
		MsgBox "Stok Kart� Lokasyonlar� belirtilmeden kaydedilemez."
		BeforeSave=-1
	End If

	If S00.KART_TIPI = "S" Then
		If Trim(S00.GK_1) = "MAMUL" OR Trim(S00.KART_TIPI) = "YARIMAMUL"  Then
			msgbox "Kart T�r� [Sat�nal�nan] Se�ildi�inde GK-1 De�eri [MAMUL / YARIMAMUL] Se�ilemez."
			
			BeforeSave=-1
		End If
	End If

End Function
'-----------------------------------
Sub AfterLoad
	Set ZUSTOK00=Doc.GetTableObject("ZU_STOK00")
	
	ZUSTOK00.SafePack

	If ZUSTOK00.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	For i = 1 to  ZUSTOK00.GetRecCount
		If Trim(ZUSTOK00.HMSTKKOD(i)) <> "" Then
			ZUSTOK00.HMSTKAD(i)  = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ZUSTOK00.HMSTKKOD(i), "")
			ZUSTOK00.HMSTKBIRIM(i)  = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", ZUSTOK00.HMSTKKOD(i), "")
		End If
	Next
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------
Sub MailAt(Nereden, StokKoduVal)
	Set S00=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")

	KKYOKSebebi = ""

	StokAd1Val = Trim(S00.AD)
	StokAd2Val = Trim(S00.NAME2)
	StokGK7Val = Trim(S00.GK_7)

	colspanval = 4

	If Nereden = "YediliKodAcildi" Then
		KTAdaTanimliKirkliKod = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKoduVal, "URUN", 0)
	Else
		KKYOKSebebi = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKoduVal, "KKYOKWHY", 0))
	End If

	Mesaj = "<html><body>"
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<table style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family:Arial, sans-serif; font-size:13px; color:#000; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>"
	
	' Ba�l�k (3 s�tunu birle�tir)
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<tr style='background:#f9e0cc; font-weight:bold;'>"

	If Nereden <> "KKYOKTanimli" Then 
		Mesaj =Mesaj & "<td colspan='" & colspanval & "' style='padding:6px 8px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>Yeni A��lan Stok Koduna ait Bilgiler</td>"
	Else
		Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td colspan='" & colspanval & "' style='padding:6px 8px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>Stok Koduna ait GK-7 Alan� KK / KKYOK Durumu</td>"
	End If

	Mesaj = Mesaj & "</tr>"
	
	' Kolon ba�l�klar�
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<tr style='background:#f9e0cc;'>"
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>Stok Kodu</td>"
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>Stok Ad 1</td>"
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>Stok Ad 2</td>"
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>Stok GK-7</td>"

	If Trim(KTAdaTanimliKirkliKod) <> "" Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>KTA' da Tan�ml� �r�n Kodu </td>"
	End If

	Mesaj = Mesaj & "</tr>"
	
	' Veri sat�r� (�ekilen de�erler)
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<tr>"
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>" & StokKoduVal & "</td>"
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>" & StokAd1Val & "</td>"
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>" & StokAd2Val & "</td>"

	If Trim(KTAdaTanimliKirkliKod) <> "" Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>" & KTAdaTanimliKirkliKod & "</td>"
	End If

	If Nereden = "KKYOKTanimli" or Nereden = "KKTanimiOnay" Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & "<td style='padding:4px 6px; border:1px solid #b0a090;'>" & StokGK7Val & "</td>"
	End If

	Mesaj = Mesaj & "</tr>"
	
	Mesaj = Mesaj & "</table>"

	If Nereden = "KKYOKTanimli" Then 
		Mesaj = Mesaj & "<p style='font-family:Arial, sans-serif; font-size:13px; color:#000; margin-top:12px;'>"
		Mesaj = Mesaj & "<strong>KKYOK SEBEB� :</strong> " & KKYOKSebebi
		Mesaj = Mesaj & "</p>"
	End If

	Mesaj = Mesaj & "</body></html>"

	msgval = ""

	If Nereden = "KKTanimiOnay" Then 

		Mailler = "kalite@ozak-t.com" 

		If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "BT" Then
			msgbox "sadece recep.deniz@ozak-t.com mailine"
			Mailler = "recep.deniz@ozak-t.com"
		End If

		Konu = "[ " & StokKoduVal & " ] Stok Kodu A��lm��t�r. GK-7 Alan�nda Kalite Kontrol Tan�m� Bekleniyor."
		'msgval = "GK-7 Alan�nda Kalite Kontrol Tan�m� i�in Bilgilendirme Maili G�nderildi."

	ElseIf Nereden = "KKYOKTanimli" Then 
		Mailler = "kalite@ozak-t.com;planlama@ozak-t.com;depo@ozak-t.com;cihan.donmez@ozak-t.com" 

		If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "BT" Then
			msgbox "sadece recep.deniz@ozak-t.com mailine"
			Mailler = "recep.deniz@ozak-t.com"
		End If

		Konu = "[ " & StokKoduVal & " ] Stok Kodu GK-7  [KK] Durumu De�i�ti."
		'msgval = "GK-7 [KK] Durumu De�i�ti ile �lgili Bilgilendirme Maili G�nderildi."

	Else
		Mailler = "sales@ozak-t.com;onay.yis@ozak-t.com;planlama@ozak-t.com;recep.deniz@ozak-t.com"

		If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "BT" Then
			msgbox "sadece recep.deniz@ozak-t.com mailine"
			Mailler = "recep.deniz@ozak-t.com"
		End If

		Konu = "[ " & StokKoduVal & " ] Stok Kodu A��lm��t�r."
		msgval = "7' li Stok Kodu A��ld� Bilgisi Mail ile �letildi."
	End If

	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) Then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) Then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.TEXTTOHTML="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Konu
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=Mailler
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=Mesaj
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"

	If EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.EMAILGONDERILDI = "1"  Then
		'Msgbox msgval

		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "STOK00", StokKoduVal)   
		Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")  

		If Nereden = "YediliKodAcildi" Then
			K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("MAIL_SEND", -1, "OK")   
			
		ElseIf Nereden = "KKTanimiOnay" Then
			K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("MAIL_SEND2", -1, "OK")   
		End If

		T_U1.Exec "SAVE"
	End If
End Sub

Function AfterNew()
	If Doc.DataEntryMOde="GUI" Then
		STOK20E.TES_DEPOKODU="�KD"
		STOK20E.RED_DEPOKODU="��D"
	End If

End Function
Function BeforeSave()
If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then	
	Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	For i= 1 TO S20T.GetRecCount
		FOKodu=Trim(S20T.FOKOD(i))
		SipArtNo=Trim(S20T.SIPARTNO(i))
		Set S40T=Doc.Select1("sdfsdf","STOK40T","ARTNO='"&SipArtNo&"'","",-1)
		If S40T.GetRecCount>0 Then
			If Trim(S40T.FOKOD)<>FOkodu then
				MSgBox Trim(S20T.SRNUM(i))&". sat�rdaki Sat�� Sipari�i F/O'su ile �� Emrindeki F/O aras�nda uyu�mazl�k var.��lem iptal."
				BeforeSave=-1
				Exit Function
			End If
		End IF
	Next
	For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
		SSeriNo = Trim(S20T.SERINO(i))
		SLotNo = trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))
		Gk1=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_1",Skod,""))
		If Gk1="MAMUL" and Left(Skod,2)="40" Then
			SSonuc = trim(Doc.GetQValue(SKod,SLotNo,SSeriNo,"","UYGUN",0))
			If SSonuc<>"" then
				S20T.QCONAY(i)="E"
			Else
				S20T.QCONAY(i)="H"
			End If
		S20T.MPSKAPATMAENGEL(i)="E"
		End If
	Next
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	If S20E.TARIH>"2014/04/20" Then
		Call BosLotlariDoldur()
	End IF
	Call BosLotDoldur()
	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		lotNo = Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))
		Kod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
		If lotNo<>"" and left(kod,5)="30 02" Then
			S20T.SERINO(i)=lotNo
		End If
	Next
	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		SSeriNo = Trim(S20T.SERINO(i))
		Kod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
		If SSeriNo<>"" and left(kod,2)<>"40" Then
			S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=SSeriNo
		End If
	Next

	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		LotNo = Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))
		Kod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
		If LotNo<>"" and left(kod,2)="40" Then
			S20T.SERINO(i)=LotNo
		End If
	Next

	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	MamulDepo=Trim(S20E.AMBCODE)
	FasonaGidecekmi=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("GDEF00","GK_10",MamulDepo,""))
	If FasonaGidecekmi<>"" Then
		For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
			LotNo = Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))
			Kod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
			If LotNo="" Then
				S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
				S20T.SERINO(i)=""
			End If
		Next
	End If
	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		AsamaKodu=Trim(S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
		Mps=Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
		If Mps<>"" and AsamaKodu<>"" Then
			Set MPST=Doc.Select1("fsdf","MMPS10T","EVRAKNO='"&Mps&"'&VRI_RECETE_A='"&AsamaKodu&"'","",-1)
			If MPST.GetRecCount=0 Then
				S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i)=""
			End IF
		End If
	Next
	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		AsamaKodu=Trim(S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
		If AsamaKodu<>"" Then
			Set MPST=Doc.Select1("fsdf","MMPS10T","EVRAKNO='"&Mps&"'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'","",-1)
			If MPST.GetRecCount>0 Then
				For j=1 To MPST.GetRecCount
					Tezgah=Trim(MPST.R_KAYNAKKODU(j))
					FasonVarmi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("IMLT00","GK_6",Tezgah,"")
					If Trim(FasonVarmi)<>"" Then
						S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
					End If
				Next
			End IF
		End If
	Next
	Doc.RunCommand("CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003")
End If
End Function

Function BeforeCommand(cmd)
If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then	
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Eno=Trim(S20E.EVRAKNO)
	Set S20EE=Doc.Select1("sasad","STOK20E","EVRAKNO='"&Eno&"'","",-1)
	If S20EE.GetRecCount=0 then
		If Trim(cmd)="USR_SENT_FORMENT_USE1" Then
			MsgBox "Kaydedilmemi� �retim fi�ten ��kt� al�namaz.�nce fi�i kaydedin.��lem iptal."
			BeforeCommand=-1
		End If
	End If
End If
End Function

Sub BosLotlariDoldur()

	LastLotIndex = GetLastLotIndex()
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	TersYil=StrReverse(Yil)
	Ay=Mid(STOK20E.TARIH,6,2)
	Gun=Mid(STOK20E.TARIH,9,2)
	YilAyGun=TersYil&Ay&Gun
	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		If Left(STOK20T.KOD(i),2)="40" Then
			if trim(STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
				sirano=sirano+1
				STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i) = YilAyGun & "-" & Right("0000" & (sirano+LastLotIndex),4)
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Function GetLastLotIndex()

	QStr = "LOTNUMBER<>''&TARIH>'2014/04/21'"
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1("asdad111", "STOK20T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		If LEft(S20T.KOD(i),2)="40" then
			Tire = InStr(1, S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
			if Tire > 0  Then
				GetLastLotIndex = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
				Exit Function
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Function


Sub BosLotDoldur()

	LastLotIndex = GetLastIndex()
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		If Left(STOK20T.KOD(i),5)="30 02" Then
			if trim(STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
				sirano=sirano+1
				STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i) = "M"&Yil & "-" & Right("0000" & (sirano+LastLotIndex),4)
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Function GetLastIndex()

	QStr = "LOTNUMBER<>''"
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1("asdad111", "STOK20T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		If LEft(S20T.KOD(i),5)="30 02" then
			Tire =InStr(1, S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
			if Tire > 0  Then
				GetLastIndex = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
				Exit Function
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Function

Sub YMLotlama()
		Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
		Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
		Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
			MPSNo = Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
			Job=Trim(S20T.REFTEXT01(i))
			If Left(S20T.KOD(i),5)="30 02" Then
				If SonOperasyonmu(Job,MPSNo) then
					If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
						YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("YMSAYAC")
						Sayac="M"&yil&"-"&YMSayac
						S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
					End If
				End If
			End If
		Next
End Sub

Sub Siparise_Bagli_YMleri_Lotla()
	Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
		SipNo = Trim(S20T.SIPNO(i))
		Asama=Trim(S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
		If SipNo<>"" then
			If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
				YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("YMLOT")
				Sayac=yil&"-"&YMSayac
				S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
				S20T.SERINO(i)=Sayac
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Sub


Sub MamulLotlama()
		Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
		Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
		Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
		TersYil=StrReverse(Yil)
		Ay=Mid(S20E.TARIH,6,2)
		Gun=Mid(S20E.TARIH,9,2)
		YilAyGun=TersYil&Ay&Gun
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
			If Left(S20T.KOD(i),2)="40" Then
				If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
					MMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("MMSAYAC")
					Sayac=YilAyGun&"-"&MMSayac
					S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
				End If
			End If
		Next
End Sub

Sub BosLotDoldur()

	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()

Function SonOperasyonMu(Job,MPSNo)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dde5r5hj", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo &"'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&JOBNO='"&Job&"'", "<SRNUM",1)
	If Trim(M10T.VRI_RECETE_A)="" Then
		SonOperasyonmu=True
	Else
		SonOperasyonmu=False
	EndIf
End Function

Function AfterNew()
	If Doc.DataEntryMOde="GUI" Then
		STOK20E.TES_DEPOKODU="�KD"
		STOK20E.RED_DEPOKODU="IADE"
	End If
End Function
Function BeforeSave()
	Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then	
		For i= 1 TO S20T.GetRecCount
			FOKodu=Trim(S20T.FOKOD(i))
			SipArtNo=Trim(S20T.SIPARTNO(i))
			If SipArtNo<>"" and FOKodu<>"" Then
				Set S40T=Doc.Select1("_TEMP","STOK40T","ARTNO='"&SipArtNo&"'","",1)
				If S40T.GetRecCount>0 Then
					If Trim(S40T.FOKOD)<>FOkodu then
						MSgBox Trim(S20T.SRNUM(i))&". sat�rdaki Sat�� Sipari�i F/O'su ile �� Emrindeki F/O aras�nda uyu�mazl�k var.��lem iptal."
						BeforeSave=-1
						Exit Function
					End If
				End If
			End If
		Next
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
			Skod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
			SSeriNo = Trim(S20T.SERINO(i))
			SLotNo = trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))
			Gk1=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_1",Skod,""))
			If Gk1="MAMUL" and Left(Skod,2)="40" Then
				SSonuc = trim(Doc.GetQValue(SKod,SLotNo,SSeriNo,"","UYGUN",0))
				If SSonuc<>"" then
					S20T.QCONAY(i)="E"
				Else
					S20T.QCONAY(i)="H"
				End If
				S20T.MPSKAPATMAENGEL(i)="E"
			End If
		Next
		Doc.RunCommand("CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003")
	End If
'	For i= 1 TO S20T.GetRecCount
'		SKod=Left(Trim(S20T.KOD(i)),5)
'		If Skod="30 18" Then
'			Doc.RunCommand("RECETE_HESAPLA_MPSTRANSFERININ_TUMUNU_KULLAN")
'		end If
'	Next
	Set S20TI=Doc.Select2("dsdf","STOK20TI","KOD<>''","",-1)
	For i= 1 To S20TI.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S20TI.STOK01DEBULUNDUMU(i))="2" Then
			BulunamayanSatirSayisi=BulunamayanSatirSayisi+1
		End If	
	Next
	If BulunamayanSatirSayisi=0 Then
		Call MamulLotlama()
		Call YMLotlama()
		Call AGS_YMLotlama()
		Call Siparise_Bagli_YMleri_Lotla()
	End If

	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		lotNo = Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))
		Kod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
		If lotNo<>"" and left(kod,5)="30 02" Then
			S20T.SERINO(i)=lotNo
		End If
	Next
	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		SSeriNo = Trim(S20T.SERINO(i))
		Kod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
		If SSeriNo<>"" and left(kod,2)<>"40" Then
			S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=SSeriNo
		End If
	Next
	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		LotNo = Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))
		Kod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
		If LotNo<>"" and left(kod,2)="40" Then
			S20T.SERINO(i)=LotNo
		End If
	Next
	MamulDepo=Trim(S20E.AMBCODE)
	FasonaGidecekmi=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("GDEF00","GK_10",MamulDepo,""))
	If FasonaGidecekmi<>"" Then
		For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
			LotNo = Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))
			Kod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
			If LotNo="" Then
				S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
				S20T.SERINO(i)=""
			End If
		Next
	End If
	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		AsamaKoduu=Trim(S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
		Mps=Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
		If Mps<>"" and AsamaKoduu<>"" Then
			Set MPSTT=Doc.Select1("fs333df","MMPS10T","EVRAKNO='"&Mps&"'&VRI_RECETE_A='"&AsamaKoduu&"'","",-1)
			If MPSTT.GetRecCount=0 Then
				S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i)=""
			End IF
		End If
	Next
	For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
		AsamaKodu=Trim(S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
		Mps=Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
		If AsamaKodu<>"" Then
			Set MPST=Doc.Select1("___fsdfVVV","MMPS10T","EVRAKNO='"&Mps&"'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'","",-1)
			If MPST.GetRecCount>0 Then
				For j=1 To MPST.GetRecCount
					Tezgah=Trim(MPST.R_KAYNAKKODU(j))
					FasonVarmi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("IMLT00","GK_6",Tezgah,"")
					If Trim(FasonVarmi)<>"" Then
						S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
					End If
				Next
			End IF
		End If
	Next
' 19/08/2020 -AGS DEPOYA KES�LEN �RET�M F��LER�N�N S�PAR��E OTOMAT�K REZERV�N� SA�LAR
	If Trim(S20E.AMBCODE)="ARA�GE" Then
		For i= 1 To S20T.GetRecCount
			Kod=Trim(S20T.KOD(i))
			QOnay=S20T.QCONAY(i)	
			If QOnay="E" and left(kod,2)="40" Then
				If Trim(S20T.SIPNO(i))<>"" Then
					SipariseUretim=SipariseUretim+1
				End If
			End If
		Next
		If SipariseUretim>0 Then
			S20E.MPSSREZ1="S"
		End If
	End If
End Function

Function BeforeCommand(cmd)
If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then	
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Eno=Trim(S20E.EVRAKNO)
	Set S20EE=Doc.Select1("sasad","STOK20E","EVRAKNO='"&Eno&"'","",-1)
	If S20EE.GetRecCount=0 then
		If Trim(cmd)="USR_SENT_FORMENT_USE1" Then
			MsgBox "Kaydedilmemi� �retim fi�ten ��kt� al�namaz.�nce fi�i kaydedin.��lem iptal."
			BeforeCommand=-1
		End If
	End If
End If
End Function

Sub YMLotlama()
		Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
		Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
		Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
			MPSNo = Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
			Job=Trim(S20T.REFTEXT01(i))
			If Left(S20T.KOD(i),5)="30 02" Then
				If SonOperasyonmu(Job,MPSNo) then
					If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
						YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("YMSAYAC")
						Sayac="M"&yil&"-"&YMSayac
						S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
					End If
				End If
			End If
		Next
End Sub

Sub AGS_YMLotlama()
		Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
		Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
		Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
			MPSNo = Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
			Job=Trim(S20T.REFTEXT01(i))
			If Left(S20T.KOD(i),8)="30 01 25" Then
				If SonOperasyonmu(Job,MPSNo) then
					If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
						AGS_YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("AGSSAYAC")
						Sayac="HPU"&yil&"-"&AGS_YMSayac
						S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
						S20T.SERINO(i)=Sayac
					End If
				End If
			End If
		Next
End Sub

Sub Siparise_Bagli_YMleri_Lotla()
	Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
		SipNo = Trim(S20T.SIPNO(i))
		Asama=Trim(S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
		If SipNo<>"" then
			If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
				YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("YMLOT")
				Sayac=yil&"-"&YMSayac
				S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
				S20T.SERINO(i)=Sayac
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Sub


Sub MamulLotlama()
		Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
		Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
		Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
		TersYil=StrReverse(Yil)
		Ay=Mid(S20E.TARIH,6,2)
		Gun=Mid(S20E.TARIH,9,2)
		YilAyGun=TersYil&Ay&Gun
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
			If Left(S20T.KOD(i),2)="40" Then
				If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
					MMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("MMSAYAC")
					Sayac=YilAyGun&"-"&MMSayac
					S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
				End If
			End If
		Next
End Sub


Sub BosLotlariDoldur()

	LastLotIndex = GetLastLotIndex()
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	TersYil=StrReverse(Yil)
	Ay=Mid(S20E.TARIH,6,2)
	Gun=Mid(S20E.TARIH,9,2)
	YilAyGun=TersYil&Ay&Gun
	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		If Left(STOK20T.KOD(i),2)="40" Then
			if trim(STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
				sirano=sirano+1
				STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i) = YilAyGun & "-" & Right("0000" & (sirano+LastLotIndex),4)
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Function GetLastLotIndex()

	QStr = "LOTNUMBER<>''&TARIH>'2014/04/21'"
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1("asdad111", "STOK20T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		If LEft(S20T.KOD(i),2)="40" then
			Tire = InStr(1, S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
			if Tire > 0  Then
				GetLastLotIndex = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
				Exit Function
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Function


Sub BosLotDoldur()

	LastLotIndex = GetLastIndex()
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		MPSNo = Trim(STOK20T.MPSNO(i))
		Job=Trim(STOK20T.REFTEXT01(i))
			If Left(STOK20T.KOD(i),5)="30 02" Then
				If SonOperasyonmu(Job,MPSNo) then
					if trim(STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
					sirano=sirano+1
					STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i) = "M"&Yil & "-" & Right("0000" & (sirano+LastLotIndex),4)
				End if
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Function GetLastIndex()

	QStr = "LOTNUMBER<>''"
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1("asdad111", "STOK20T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		If LEft(S20T.KOD(i),5)="30 02" then
			Tire = InStr(1, S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
			if Tire > 0  Then
				GetLastIndex = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
				Exit Function
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Function


Function SonOperasyonMu(Job,MPSNo)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dde5r5hj", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo &"'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&JOBNO='"&Job&"'", "<SRNUM",1)
	If Trim(M10T.VRI_RECETE_A)="" Then
		SonOperasyonmu=True
	Else
		SonOperasyonmu=False
	End If
End Function

Sub MOTORLotlama()

End Sub

Sub Siparise_Bagli_YMleri_Lotla()
	Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
		SipNo = Trim(S20T.SIPNO(i))
		Asama=Trim(S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
		If SipNo<>"" then
			If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
				YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("YMLOT")
				Sayac=yil&"-"&YMSayac
				S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
				S20T.SERINO(i)=Sayac
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Sub MamulLotlama()
		Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
		Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
		Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
		TersYil=StrReverse(Yil)
		Ay=Mid(S20E.TARIH,6,2)
		Gun=Mid(S20E.TARIH,9,2)
		YilAyGun=TersYil&Ay&Gun
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
			If Left(S20T.KOD(i),2)="40" AND Left(S20T.KOD(i),5)<>"40 50" Then
				If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
					MMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("MMSAYAC")
					Sayac=YilAyGun&"-"&MMSayac
					S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
				End If
			End If
		Next
End Sub

Sub BosLotlariDoldur()

	LastLotIndex = GetLastLotIndex()
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	TersYil=StrReverse(Yil)
	Ay=Mid(S20E.TARIH,6,2)
	Gun=Mid(S20E.TARIH,9,2)
	YilAyGun=TersYil&Ay&Gun
	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		If Left(STOK20T.KOD(i),2)="40" Then
			if trim(STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
				sirano=sirano+1
				STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i) = YilAyGun & "-" & Right("0000" & (sirano+LastLotIndex),4)
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Function GetLastLotIndex()

	QStr = "LOTNUMBER<>''&TARIH>'2014/04/21'"
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1("asdad111", "STOK20T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		If LEft(S20T.KOD(i),2)="40" then
			Tire = InStr(1, S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
			if Tire > 0  Then
				GetLastLotIndex = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
				Exit Function
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Function

Sub BosLotDoldur()

	LastLotIndex = GetLastIndex()
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		MPSNo = Trim(STOK20T.MPSNO(i))
		Job=Trim(STOK20T.REFTEXT01(i))
			If Left(STOK20T.KOD(i),5)="30 02" Then
				If SonOperasyonmu(Job,MPSNo) then
					if trim(STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
					sirano=sirano+1
					STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i) = "M"&Yil & "-" & Right("0000" & (sirano+LastLotIndex),4)
				End if
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Function GetLastIndex()

	QStr = "LOTNUMBER<>''"
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1("asdad111", "STOK20T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		If LEft(S20T.KOD(i),5)="30 02" then
			Tire = InStr(1, S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
			if Tire > 0  Then
				GetLastIndex = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
				Exit Function
			End if
		End If
	Next
End Function


Function SonOperasyonMu(Job,MPSNo)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dde5r5hj", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo &"'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&JOBNO='"&Job&"'", "<SRNUM",1)
	If Trim(M10T.VRI_RECETE_A)="" Then
		SonOperasyonmu=True
	Else
		SonOperasyonmu=False
	End If
End Function

Function BeforeSave()

'If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "D7KSUPER" Then  Exit Function

	Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")

		For i= 1 TO S20T.GetRecCount
			FOKodu=Trim(S20T.FOKOD(i))
			SipArtNo=Trim(S20T.SIPARTNO(i))
			If SipArtNo<>"" and FOKodu<>"" Then
				Set S40T=Doc.Select1("_TEMP","STOK40T","ARTNO='"&SipArtNo&"'","",1)
				If S40T.GetRecCount>0 Then
					If Trim(S40T.FOKOD)<>FOkodu then
						MSgBox Trim(S20T.SRNUM(i))&". satirdaki Satis Siparisi F/O'su ile Is Emrindeki F/O arasinda uyusmazlik var.Islem iptal."
						BeforeSave=-1
						Exit Function
					End If
				End If
			End If
		Next
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount

			SSeriNo = Trim(S20T.SERINO(i))

					If SSonuc<>"" then
						S20T.QCONAY(i)="E"
					Else
						S20T.QCONAY(i)="H"
					End If
					S20T.MPSKAPATMAENGEL(i)="E"
				End If
			End If
		Next
		Doc.RunCommand("CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003")
	End If

		' KARSITRNUM -> ilk g�r�len KOD e�lemesi
		Set dict = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
		
		' Sonu�lar� g�stermek i�in ge�ici tablo
		Set mism = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MISMATCH=STACK", "IO")
		mism.Addfield2 "KARSITRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
		mism.Addfield2 "KOD",        "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
		mism.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO",    "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
		mism.Addfield2 "TARIH",      "DATE", 10,  0, "L", "", ""
		
		For i = 1 To S20TStack.GetRecCount()
			key = Trim(S20TStack.KARSITRNUM(i))
			kod = Trim(S20TStack.KOD(i))
	
			If key <> "" Then
				If Not dict.Exists(key) Then
					dict.Add key, kod   ' ilk kodu sakla
				ElseIf dict(key) <> kod Then
					' Fark yakaland�, sat�r� sonu�lara ekle
					row = mism.AddRow
					mism.SetCurrentRow row
	
					mism.KARSITRNUM = key
					mism.KOD        = kod
					mism.EVRAKNO    = Trim(S20TStack.EVRAKNO(i))
					mism.TARIH      = S20TStack.TARIH(i)
				End If
			End If
		Next
		
		Set S20TStack = Nothing
		Set dict = Nothing
		
		If mism.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
			MsgBox "Farkl� KOD i�eren M�kerrer KARSITRNUM De�eri Bulundu: " & mism.GetRecCount() & " sat�r." & vbcrlf & "Kay�t ��lemi �ptal Edilmi�tir. ERP Y�neticisine Haber Veriniz."
			
			Set mism = Nothing
	
			BeforeSave=-1
			Exit Function
		End If
	
		Set mism = Nothing
	End If
	'***********************************************************************
End Function

Function BeforeCommand(cmd)
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then	
		Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
		Eno=Trim(S20E.EVRAKNO)
		Set S20EE=Doc.Select1("sasad","STOK20E","EVRAKNO='"&Eno&"'","",-1)
		If S20EE.GetRecCount=0 then
			If Trim(cmd)="USR_SENT_FORMENT_USE1" Then
				MsgBox "Kaydedilmemi� �retim fi�ten ��kt� al�namaz.�nce fi�i kaydedin.��lem iptal."
				BeforeCommand=-1
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Function

Sub YMLotlama()
		Set S20T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
		Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
		Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
			MPSNo = Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
			Job=Trim(S20T.REFTEXT01(i))
			If Left(S20T.KOD(i),5)="30 02" Then
				If SonOperasyonmu(Job,MPSNo) then
					If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
						'YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("YMSAYAC")
						'Sayac="M"&yil&"-"&YMSayac
						S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=MPSNo
					End If
				Else
					If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
						'YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("YMSAYAC")
						'Sayac="M"&yil&"-"&YMSayac
						S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=MPSNo
					End If
				End If
			End If
		Next
End Sub

Sub AGS_YMLotlama()

		Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
		Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
		For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
			MPSNo = Trim(S20T.MPSNO(i))
			Job=Trim(S20T.REFTEXT01(i))
			If Left(S20T.KOD(i),8)="30 01 25" Then
				If SonOperasyonmu(Job,MPSNo) then
					If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
						AGS_YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("AGSSAYAC")
						Sayac="HPU"&yil&"-"&AGS_YMSayac
						S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
						S20T.SERINO(i)=Sayac
					End If
				End If
			End If
		Next
End Sub

Sub Siparise_Bagli_YMleri_Lotla()

	Set S20E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Yil=Right(Doc.StrDate_Year(S20E.TARIH),2)
	For i= 1 to S20T.GetRecCount
		SipNo = Trim(S20T.SIPNO(i))
		Asama=Trim(S20T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
		If SipNo<>"" Then
			If Left(S20T.KOD(i),2)<>"40" Then
				 If Left(S20T.KOD(i),2)<>"41" then
					If Trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i))="" Then
						YMSayac=Doc.Sayac0danAl("YMLOT")
						Sayac=yil&"-"&YMSayac
						S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Sayac
						S20T.SERINO(i)=Sayac
					End If
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Function BeforeSave()
	UserN=Trim(Doc.Getd7username)

	IF Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
		IF UserN<>"UPLN1" and UserN<>"T" and UserN<>"DEPOSEFI" and UserN<>"D7KSUPER"  Then
			MSgBox "Bu evraktan kayit islemi yapilamaz.Islem iptal."
			BeforeSave="CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		End If
	End If
End Function

Kod : 
Function BeforeSave()
	Set S23E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK23E")
	Set S23T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK23T")
	S23T.SafePack
	If TRim(S23E.AMBCODE)="HM" Then
		For i= 1 To S23T.GetRecCount
			If Trim(S23T.LOCATION_NEW1(i))="" Then
				MsgBox "Lokasyon bilgisi girlmeyen sat�r var.��lem iptal."&vbcrlf& Trim(S23T.SRNUM(i)) &". sat�r lokasyon bilgisi eksik."
				BeforeSave=-1
			End If
		Next
	End If
End Function

Function AfterSave()
	Set S23E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK23E")
	DepoKodu=Trim(S23E.AMBCODE)
	HangiMasrafMerkezi=Trim(Doc.getDbFieldValue("GDEF00","GK_3",Depokodu,""))
	If HangiMasrafMerkezi="10" Then
		Masomer="000000000000000000000015"
	ElseIf HangiMAsrafMerkezi="20" Then
		Masomer="000000000000000000000014"
	ElseIf HangiMAsrafMerkezi="30" Then
		Masomer="000000000000000000000027"
	Else
		Masomer="000000000000000000000004"
	End If
	Call CikisOlustur(Masomer)
End Function

Sub CikisOlustur(MasrafMerkezi)

	Set S23T=Doc.GetTableOBject("STOK23T")
	Set S23E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK23E")
	ENo=Trim(S23E.EVRAKNO)
	S23T.SafePack()
	Set S21E=Doc.Select1("asdsad","STOK21E","NOTES='"&ENo&"'","",-1)
	If S21E.GetRecCount=0 then
		Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK21","")
		Set S21E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")
		Set S21T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")
		nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
		If nret=0 Then
			S21E.AMBCODE=S23T.AMBCODE
			S21E.NOTES=Trim(S23T.EVRAKNO)
			For i= 1 To S23T.GetRecCount
				Skod=Trim(S23T.KOD(i))
				gk6=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_6",Skod,""))
				If gk6="KYOK" Then
					satir=S21T.AddRow()
					S21T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
					S21T.KOD(satir)=Skod
					S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR(satir)=S23T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					S21T.SF_SF_UNIT(satir)=S23T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
					S21T.KARSI_MH_A(satir)=MasrafMerkezi
					S21T.LOCATION1(satir)=S23T.LOCATION_NEW1(i)
					S21T.LOCATION2(satir)=S23T.LOCATION_NEW2(i)
					S21T.LOCATION3(satir)=S23T.LOCATION_NEW3(i)
					S21T.LOCATION4(satir)=S23T.LOCATION_NEW4(i)
					S21T.LOTNUMBER(satir)=S23T.LOTNUMBER(i)
					S21T.SERINO(satir)=S23T.SERINO(i)
				End If
			Next
			nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher
			If nret=0 Then
				nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			End If
		End If
	End If

End Sub

Public CikisYapilacaklar
Function BeforeSave()
	Set S23E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK23E")
	Set S23T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK23T")
	S23T.SafePack
	If TRim(S23E.AMBCODE)="HM" Then
		For i= 1 To S23T.GetRecCount
			If Trim(S23T.LOCATION_NEW1(i))="" Then
				MsgBox "Lokasyon bilgisi girlmeyen sat�r var.��lem iptal."&vbcrlf& Trim(S23T.SRNUM(i)) &". sat�r lokasyon bilgisi eksik."
				BeforeSave=-1
			End If
		Next
	End If
	CikisYapilacaklar=0
	For i= 1 To S23T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S23T.KOD(i))
		gk6=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_6",Skod,""))
		If gk6="KYOK" and TRim(S23E.AMBCODE)<>"GKD" and TRim(S23E.AMBCODE)<>"IADE"  Then
			CikisYapilacaklar=CikisYapilacaklar+1
		End If
	Next
End Function

Function AfterSave()
	Set S23E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK23E")
	Set S23T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK23T")
	DepoKodu=Trim(S23E.AMBCODE)
	HangiMasrafMerkezi=Trim(Doc.getDbFieldValue("GDEF00","GK_3",Depokodu,""))
	If HangiMasrafMerkezi="10" Then
		Masomer="000000000000000000000015"
	ElseIf HangiMAsrafMerkezi="20" Then
		Masomer="000000000000000000000014"
	ElseIf HangiMAsrafMerkezi="30" Then
		Masomer="000000000000000000000027"
	Else
		Masomer="000000000000000000000004"
	End If
	ENo=Trim(S23E.EVRAKNO)
	Set S21E=Doc.Select1("_temp21E","STOK21E","NOTES='"&ENo&"'","",1)
	If CikisYapilacaklar>0 and S21E.GetRecCount=0 Then
		Call CikisOlustur(Masomer)
	End If
End Function

Sub CikisOlustur(MasrafMerkezi)

	Set S23T=Doc.GetTableOBject("STOK23T")
	Set S23E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK23E")
	ENo=Trim(S23E.EVRAKNO)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK21","")
	Set S21E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")
	Set S21T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")
	nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
	If nret=0 Then
		S21E.AMBCODE=S23E.AMBCODE
		S21E.NOTES=ENo
		For i= 1 To S23T.GetRecCount
			Skod=Trim(S23T.KOD(i))
			gk6=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_6",Skod,""))
			If gk6="KYOK" Then
				satir=S21T.AddRow()
				S21T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
				S21T.KOD(satir)=Skod
				S21T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=S23T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR(satir)=S23T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				S21T.SF_SF_UNIT(satir)=S23T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
				S21T.KARSI_MH_A(satir)=MasrafMerkezi
				S21T.LOCATION1(satir)=S23T.LOCATION_NEW1(i)
				S21T.LOCATION2(satir)=S23T.LOCATION_NEW2(i)
				S21T.LOCATION3(satir)=S23T.LOCATION_NEW3(i)
				S21T.LOCATION4(satir)=S23T.LOCATION_NEW4(i)
				S21T.LOTNUMBER(satir)=S23T.LOTNUMBER(i)
				S21T.SERINO(satir)=S23T.SERINO(i)
				S21T.SF_VRI_NUM1(satir)=S23T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
				S21T.SF_VRI_RECETE(satir)=S23T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i)
			End If
		Next
		nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher
		If nret=0 Then
			nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		End If
	end If
End Sub

Function HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi(YetkiKodu)
	HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi = False

	Set U12 = Doc.Select1("de443d44t", "UDEFT12", "KOD='" & Doc.Getd7UserName & "'& OZEL_HAK_KODU='"& YetkiKodu &"' ", "", -1)

	If U12.GetRowCount > 0 Then HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi = True
End Function
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave()
	'************ hm depodan transfer yetki ************************************
	Set S25E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	VerenDepo_Evrak_Val = Trim(S25E.AMBCODE)
	AlanDepo_Evrak_Val = Trim(S25E.TARGETAMBCODE)
	VerenDepo_Satir_Var = False
	
	For i= 1 To S25T.GetRecCount
		VerenDepo_Satir_Val = Trim(S25T.AMBCODE_TR(i))
		AlanDepo_Satir_Val = Trim(S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(i))
		
		If VerenDepo_Satir_Val = "HM" and AlanDepo_Satir_Val = "HM" Then
		Else
			If VerenDepo_Satir_Val = "HM" Then
				VerenDepo_Satir_Var = True
			End If
		End If
	Next	

	If VerenDepo_Satir_Var Then
		If Not HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi("STOK25_HM") Then				
			MsgBox "[ HM ] Depodan Transfer Yetkiniz Yok." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Kullan�c� Tan�ml� Mod�l Hakk� Tan�m� : STOK25_HM"
			BeforeSave=-1

			Exit Function
		End If
	Else
		If VerenDepo_Evrak_Val = "HM" and AlanDepo_Evrak_Val = "HM" Then
		Else
			If VerenDepo_Evrak_Val = "HM" Then
				If Not HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi("STOK25_HM") Then
					MsgBox "[ HM ] Depodan Transfer Yetkiniz Yok." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Kullan�c� Tan�ml� Mod�l Hakk� Tan�m� : STOK25_HM"
					BeforeSave=-1
		
					Exit Function
				End If
			End If
		End If
	End If
	'**************************************************************************************************

End Function

Function HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi(YetkiKodu)

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave()
	Set S26E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set S26T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26T")
	ENo=Trim(S26E.EVRAKNO)
	VerenDepo=Trim(S26E.AMBCODE)
	ToplamHmSatir=0

	'************ hm depodan transfer yetki ************************************
	VerenDepo_Evrak_Val = Trim(S26E.AMBCODE)
	AlanDepo_Evrak_Val = Trim(S26E.TARGETAMBCODE)
	VerenDepo_Satir_Var = False
	'**************************************************************

	For i= 1 To S26T.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S26T.AMBCODE_TR(i))="HM" Then
			ToplamHMSatir=ToplamHmSatir+1
		End If

		'************ hm depodan transfer yetki ************************************
		VerenDepo_Satir_Val = Trim(S26T.AMBCODE_TR(i))
		AlanDepo_Satir_Val = Trim(S26T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(i))

		If VerenDepo_Satir_Val = "HM" and AlanDepo_Satir_Val = "HM"  Then
		Else
			If VerenDepo_Satir_Val = "HM" Then
				VerenDepo_Satir_Var = True
			End If
		End If
		'***************************************************************************
	Next	

	DUserName=Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	Set UDF=Doc.Select1("_TEMP","UDEF","KOD='"&DuserName&"'","",1)
	If Trim(UDF.GRUP)<>"DEPO" Then
		If VerenDepo="HM" Then
			Set S26=Doc.Select1("dfdf","STOK26E","EVRAKNO='"&ENo&"'","",1)
			If S26.GetRecCount=0 then
				Call DepoGrubunaMailAt(ENo)
			End If
		End If
	End If

	'************ hm depodan transfer yetki ************************************

	If VerenDepo_Satir_Var Then
		If Not HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi("STOK26_HM") Then				
			MsgBox "[ HM ] Depodan Transfer Yetkiniz Yok." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf &"Kullan�c� Tan�ml� Mod�l Hakk� Tan�m� : STOK26_HM"
			BeforeSave=-1

			Exit Function
		End If
	Else
		If VerenDepo_Evrak_Val = "HM" and AlanDepo_Evrak_Val = "HM"  Then
		Else
			If VerenDepo_Evrak_Val = "HM" Then
				If Not HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi("STOK26_HM") Then
					MsgBox "[ HM ] Depodan Transfer Yetkiniz Yok." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Kullan�c� Tan�ml� Mod�l Hakk� Tan�m� : STOK26_HM"
					BeforeSave=-1
		
					Exit Function
				End If
			End If
		End If
	End If
	'**************************************************************************************************
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------
Sub DepoGrubunaMailAt(Fis)

	Mailler="depo@ozak-t.com"  
	Mesaj = trim(Fis)& " no'lu fi� ile HM depodan, depo personeli harici ��k�� yap�lm��t�r.B�LG�N�ZE" 
	Konu = "Fi� No : " & trim(Fis) & " - ## NO'LU DEPOLAR ARASI TRANSFER F���##"
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Konu
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=Mailler
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=Mesaj
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"
End Sub

Public MailGittimi

Function EnSonLot()
	EnSonLot = 0

	Set S29T = Doc.Select1("asdad", "STOK29T", "IADEMI<>'1' & TARIH='"& STOK29E.TARIH &"' & LOTNUMBER<>'' ", "<LOTNUMBER", -1)

	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		Tire = InStr(1, S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")

		If Tire > 0 Then
			EnSonLot = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))

			Exit Function
		End If
	Next
End Function
'------------------------------------------------
Sub BosLotlariDoldur()
	LastLotIndex = EnSonLot

	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")

	S29T.SafePack

	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()

		If trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
			sirano=sirano+1
			Tarih=replace(Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK29E.TARIH),"/","")

			YilVal=Right(Tarih,2)
			GunAy=Left(Tarih, 4)

			LotFormat=GunAy & YilVal

			S29T.LOTNUMBER(i) = LotFormat & "-" & Right("000" & (sirano + LastLotIndex), 3)

		End If

	Next
End Sub

				Set S47E=Doc.Select1("sdsdfd","STOK47E","EVRAKNO='"&Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO)&"'","",1)
				Gk=Trim(S47E.CODEDBOLUM)
				MailAdresi=Trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("DEPT","AD_L2",Gk,-1))
				If MailAdresi<>"" Then
					MailText=MailText&vbcrlf&"Talep No:"&Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO)&"  Kodu :"& Mkod &"  "&"Malzeme A��klamas� :" &MAd&" "&"Gelen Miktar:"&Miktar&vbcrlf 	
				End If	
				Call TalepAcanaMailAt(mailAdresi,mailText,Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO))
			End If
		End If
	Next
ElseIf Trim(S29E.IADEMI)="1" Then
If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	ENo=Trim(S29T.EVRAKNO)
	Set S29EE=Doc.Select1("STOK29E_tbl","STOK29E","EVRAKNO='"&ENo&"'","",-1)
	For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S29T.KOD(i))
		SeriNo=Trim(S29T.SERINO(i))
		If Left(Skod,2)>="30" and Left(Skod,2)<="41" and SeriNo<>"" Then
			Set S01=Doc.Select1("STOK01_tbl","STOK01","SERINO='"&SeriNo&"'","",-1)
			Set S60T=Doc.Select1("STOK60T_tbl","STOK60T","SERINO='"&SeriNo&"'&KOD='"&Skod&"'","",-1)
			Set S29T2=Doc.Select1("STOK29T_tbl","STOK29T","SERINO='"&SeriNo&"'&EVRAKNO<>'"&ENo&"'","",-1)
			If S01.GetRecCount>0 and S29EE.GetRecCount=0 Then
				Msg1=Msg1&i&" . sat�r ile giri�i yap�lan seri stokta mevcut."&vbcrlf
				MsgBox Msg1
				BeforeSave=-1
				Exit For
				Exit Function
			ElseIF S01.GetRecCount=0 and S60T.GetRecCount>0  Then
				Msg2=Msg2&i&" . sat�r ile giri�i yap�lan seri daha �nce "&Trim(S60T.EVRAKNO) & " Nolu sat�� irsaliyesi ile sat�lm��."&vbcrlf&"��leme devam etmek istiyor musunuz?"
			ElseIF S01.GetRecCount=0 and S60T.GetRecCount=0 and S29T2.GetRecCount>0 Then
				Msg3=Msg3&i&" . sat�r ile giri�i yap�lan seri daha �nce "&Trim(S29T2.EVRAKNO) & " Nolu sat�� irsaliyesi ile sat�lm��."&vbcrlf&"��leme devam etmek istiyor musunuz?"
			End If
		End If
	Next
	If Msg2<>"" Then
		k=Doc.d7MsgBox(Msg2,"Dinamo ERP",MB_YESNO+MB_ICONQUESTION)
		If k=7 Then
			BeforeSave=-1
		End If
	End If
	If Msg3<>"" Then
		k=Doc.d7MsgBox(Msg3,"Dinamo ERP",MB_YESNO+MB_ICONQUESTION)
		If k=7 Then
			BeforeSave=-1
		End If
	End If
End If
End If

'STOK68 de yap�lan 16 L� kodlar�n GLVELK deposuna kalite onay�ndan sonra otomatik d��me �al��mas�n�n STOK29a uyarlanmas� RD 07112024 Ba�lang��

	If S29T.GetRecCount>0 Then
			For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
				Skod=Trim(S29T.KOD(i))
				If Left(Skod,2)="16" and (Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="E" OR Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="S")  Then
					S29T.AMBCODE_TR(i)="GLVELK"
				End If
				If (Skod="119DL638" OR Skod="119DL639" OR Skod="10 00 26 0033" OR Skod="10 00 26 0034") and (Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="E" OR Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="S")  Then
					S29T.AMBCODE_TR(i)="FSLAZ1"
				End If
				
			Next
	End If

Function AfterSave()

	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S29T.KOD(i))
		LotNo=Trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i))
		If Skod<>"" then
			If Left(Skod,8)="58 00 01" Then
				Set QVAL=Doc.Select1("_TEMPXXXX","QVAL02E","KOD='"&Skod&"'&LOTNUMBER='"&LotNo&"'","",1)
				If QVAL.GetRecCount=0 Then
					'msgbox "i="& i
					a=i
					Call AhsapKaliteFoyuAc(i)
					i=a	
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next

	Set S29E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	Set S29TT=Doc.Select2("dfsdf","STOK29T","KOD>'15'&KOD<'17'","",-1)

		Set S47E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
		Set S47T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
