			ENo=Trim(S47E.EVRAKNO)
			S47E.CODEDBOLUM="YGS - PLN"
			S47E.CODEDKISI="0000000000000056"
			For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
				Skod=Trim(S29T.KOD(i))
				If Left(Skod,2)="16" and Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="E" Then
					Miktar=S29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					Set BOMT=Doc.Select1("_TMEP","BOMU01T","BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='"+Skod+"'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'","",1)
					If BOMT.GetReCCount>0 Then
						FasonKod=Trim(BOMT.BOMREC_CODE)	

									satir=S47T.AddRow()
									S47T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
									S47T.KOD(satir)=FasonKod
									S47T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=miktar
									S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satir)=Doc.Bugun
									S47T.REFTEXT02(satir)="�RET�M PLANLAMA"

						'Call FasonTalepAc(FasonKod,miktar)
						say=say+1
					End If
					S29T.AMBCODE_TR(i)="GLVELK"
				End If
			Next
			If say>0 Then
				If nret=0 Then
					nrt=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
					If nrt=0 Then
						nrtt=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
						S29E.GK_3="OK"
						Call MailGonder(Eno)
					End If
				End If
			Else
				nret=Doc1.Delete_Voucher()
				Set doc1=Nothing
			End If
		End IF
	End If

Sub TalepOlustur()
	Set S29=Doc.Select2("1ff1dasgf","STOK29T","QCONAY='I'&REFTEXT03=''","",-1)
	If S29.GetRecCount>0 Then
		Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK47","")
		Set S47E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
		Set S47T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
		nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
		If nret<>0 then
			Msgbox "Evrak Alinamiyor"
		End If
		S47E.CODEDBOLUM="DEPO"
		S47E.CODEDKISI="0000000000000101"
		S47E.GK_1="IADE"
		Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
		For i= 1 To S29T.GetReccount
			If Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="I" and Trim(S29T.REFTEXT03(i))="" Then
				satir=S47T.AddRow()
				S47T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
				S47T.KOD(satir)=S29T.KOD(i)
				S47T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=S29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satir)=Doc.StrDate_Increment(Doc.Bugun(),10)
				S47T.REFTEXT01(satir)=S29T.SIPNO(i)
				S47T.REFTEXT02(satir)="�RETIM"
				S29T.REFTEXT03(i)="1"
			End If
		Next
		nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
		If nret=0 Then
			nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		End If
	End If
End Sub

Sub AhsapKaliteFoyuAc(satir)
'msgbox "Satir:" & satir & " - i="& i
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	Set S29E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	StokKodu=Trim(S29T.KOD(satir))
	Lot=Trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(satir))
	Set QVAL=Doc.Select1("_XXXXXXCHE","QVAL10T","EVRAKNO='06'","",-1)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("QVAL20","")
	Set QVAL2E=Doc1.GetTableObject("QVAL02E")
	Set QVAL2T=Doc1.GetTableObject("QVAL02T")
	nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
	If nret<>0 Then
		MsgBox "Kalite Kontrol plan f�y� olusturulamadi."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	QVAL2E.KOD=Trim(S29T.KOD(satir))
	QVAL2E.CONTAINER_1=Trim(S29T.EVRAKNO)
	QVAL2E.LOTNUMBER=Trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(satir))
	QVAL2E.SERINO=Trim(S29T.SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO(satir))
	QVAL2E.ILK_CH_KODU=Trim(S29T.CARIHESAPCODE)
	QVAL2E.ILK_SF_MIKTAR=S29T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)
	QVAL2E.ILK_SF_UNIT=S29T.SF_SF_UNIT(satir)
	QVAL2E.REFTARIH1=S29E.TARIH
	For i= 1 To QVAL.GetRecCount
		satir=QVAL2T.AddRow()
		QVAL2T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARCODE(satir)=QVAL.VARCODE(i)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARTYPE(satir)=QVAL.VARTYPE(i)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARLEN(satir)=QVAL.VARLEN(i)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARSIG(satir)=QVAL.VARSIG(i)
		QVAL2T.VARASPNAME(satir)=QVAL.VARASPNAME(i)
	Next
	nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	If nret=0 Then
		nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	End If
End Sub

Sub MailGonder(Eno)
	Mailler="planlama@ozak-t.com" &";" & "satinalma@ozak-t.com" & ";" & "depo@ozak-t.com"  
'	Mailler="recep.deniz@ozak-t.com"  

	Mesaj = "Evrak No : " & trim(Eno) & " no'lu Fason Galvaniz talebi a��lm��t�r. " 
	Konu = "Talep No : " & trim(ENo) & " - ## NO'LU GALVAN�Z FASON SATINALMA TALEB�##"
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Konu
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=Mailler
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=Mesaj
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"
End Sub

Sub TalepAcanaMailAt(MailAdresi,MailText,TalepNo)

	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	MailGittimi=False
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Trim(TalepNo) & " No'lu  Talebiniz Hk."
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=trim(MailAdresi)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=MailText
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"
	MailGittimi=True
	If MailGittimi Then
		For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
			S29T.REFTEXT01(i)="Mail Gitti"
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Function AfterCommand(cmd)
	If Trim(cmd)="CMD_STOK46_SEC2" Then
		Set S46T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
		Set S29T=doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
		For i=1 To S46T.GetRecCount
			For j= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
				If Trim(S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2(i))<>"" Then
					If Trim(S46T.ARTNO(i))=Trim(S29T.SIPARTNO(j)) Then
						S29T.REFTEXT02(j)=Trim(S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2(i))
					End If
					S29T.D_ID_KAYNAK(j)=Trim(S46T.REFTEXT03(i))	
				End If
			Next
		Next
	End If
'Bu fonksiyonda STOK46 Sipari� Mod�l�ndeki Laser Kesim Numaras�n� otomatik STOK29 un REFTEXT02 alan�na eklemesini yap�yor. Ayn� Zamanda Uretici Kodu dedikleri STOK46 daki Reftext03 alan�ndaki de�eri STOK29 da D_ID alan�na yaz�yor. 
End FunctionSet S29E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")

	S29E.RED_DEPOKODU="��D"

Function BeforeSave()
	doc.RunCommand("CMD_SATINALMA_FATURASINDAN_FIYATLANDIR")	
	Set S29E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	Set S29TT=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	If Trim(S29E.ITHALAT_DOSYA_NO)<>"" Then
		S29E.NITELIK="ITHALAT"
	End If
	If Trim(S29E.XIRSALIYETARIH)<>"" Then
		If Trim(S29E.REFDATE01)="" Then
			S29E.REFDATE01=S29E.TARIH
		End If
		S29E.TARIH=S29E.XIRSALIYETARIH
	Else
		If Trim(S29E.REFDATE01)="" Then
			S29E.REFDATE01=S29E.TARIH
		End If	
	End If
	ENo=Trim(S29E.EVRAKNO)
	'dUser=Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	'If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
	'	Set UDFT=doc.Select1("_TEMPXX","UDEF","KOD='"&Duser&"'","",1)
	'	If Trim(UDFT.GRUP)="DEPO" Then	
	'		Set UDF=Doc.Select1("TEMPDDD","UDEFT12","KOD='"&dUser&"'&OZEL_HAK_KODU='KALITE'","",1)
	'		If UDF.GetRecCount>0 Then
	'			For i= 1 To S29TT.GetRecCount
	'				Skod=Trim(S29TT.KOD(i))
	'				DepoOnaylistesindemi=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_4",Skod,""))
	'				If	 DepoOnaylistesindemi<>1 and S29TT.QCONAY(i)<>"E" Then
	'					S29TT.QCONAY(i)="H"
	'				End If
	'			Next
	'		Else

	'	End If

	If Trim(S29E.IADEMI)<>"1" Then
		Call BosLotlariDoldur()
		S29TT.SafePack()
		Set S29=Doc.Select2("11dasgf","STOK29T","QCONAY='I'","",-1)
		S29.SafePack
		'If S29.GetRecCount>0 Then
			Call TalepOlustur()
		'End If
		For i= 1 To S29TT.GetRecCount
			Skod=Trim(S29TT.KOD(i))
			GK5=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_5",Skod,""))
			GK3=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_3",Skod,""))
			'If GK5="TALA�LI" tHEN
			'	Call KaliteFoyAc(i)
			'End If
			GK7=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",Skod,""))
			If GK7="" Then
				S29TT.QCONAY(i)="E"
			End If
			'If Trim(S29TT.REFTEXT02(i))="" Then
			'	S29TT.REFTEXT02(i)=GK3
			'End If
		If Trim(S29TT.REFTEXT01(i))="" Then
			SipNo=Trim(S29TT.SIPNO(i))
			SipArtNo=Trim(S29TT.SIPARTNO(i))
			Set S46T1=Doc.Select1("zxcxv","STOK46T1","EVRAKNO='"&SipNo&"'&ARTNO='"&SipArtNo&"'","",-1)
			If Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO)<>"" Then
				Miktar=S29TT.SF_MIKTAR(i)

					MAd=Trim(S29TT.NONSTOK_AD(i))
				Else
					MAd=Trim(S29TT.KOD_STOK00_AD(i))

				End If	
				Call TalepAcanaMailAt(mailAdresi,mailText,Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO))
			End If
		End If
	Next
ElseIf Trim(S29E.IADEMI)="1" Then
If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	ENo=Trim(S29T.EVRAKNO)
	Set S29EE=Doc.Select1("STOK29E_tbl","STOK29E","EVRAKNO='"&ENo&"'","",-1)
	For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S29T.KOD(i))
		SeriNo=Trim(S29T.SERINO(i))
		If Left(Skod,2)>="30" and Left(Skod,2)<="41" and SeriNo<>"" Then
			Set S01=Doc.Select1("STOK01_tbl","STOK01","SERINO='"&SeriNo&"'","",-1)

				Msg1=Msg1&i&" . sat�r ile giri�i yap�lan seri stokta mevcut."&vbcrlf
				MsgBox Msg1
				BeforeSave=-1
				Exit For
				Exit Function
			ElseIF S01.GetRecCount=0 and S60T.GetRecCount>0  Then
				Msg2=Msg2&i&" . sat�r ile giri�i yap�lan seri daha �nce "&Trim(S60T.EVRAKNO) & " Nolu sat��irsaliyesi ile sat�lm��."&vbcrlf&"��leme devam etmek istiyor musunuz?"
			ElseIF S01.GetRecCount=0 and S60T.GetRecCount=0 and S29T2.GetRecCount>0 Then

			End If
		End If
	Next
	If Msg2<>"" Then
		k=Doc.d7MsgBox(Msg2,"Dinamo ERP",MB_YESNO+MB_ICONQUESTION)
		If k=7 Then
			BeforeSave=-1
		End If
	End If
	If Msg3<>"" Then
		k=Doc.d7MsgBox(Msg3,"Dinamo ERP",MB_YESNO+MB_ICONQUESTION)
		If k=7 Then
			BeforeSave=-1
		End If
	End If
End If
End If

'STOK68 de yap�lan 16 L� kodlar�n GLVELK deposuna kalite onay�ndan sonra otomatik d��me �al��mas�n�n STOK29a uyarlanmas� RD 07112024 Ba�lang��
'119DL638,119DL639,10 00 26 0033,10 00 26 0034 Malzemelerinin giri�i sonras� FSLAZ1depoya aktar�lmas� Ba�lang��
Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	If S29T.GetRecCount>0 Then
			For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
				Skod=Trim(S29T.KOD(i))
				If Left(Skod,2)="16" and (Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="E" OR Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="S")  Then
					S29T.AMBCODE_TR(i)="GLVELK"
				End If
				If (Skod="119DL638" OR Skod="119DL639" OR Skod="10 00 26 0033" OR Skod="10 00 26 0034") and (Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="E" OR Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="S")  Then
					S29T.AMBCODE_TR(i)="FSLAZ1"
				End If
			Next
	End If
'119DL638,119DL639,10 00 26 0033,10 00 26 0034 Malzemelerinin giri�i sonras� FSLAZ1depoya aktar�lmas� Biti�
'STOK68 de yap�lan 16 L� kodlar�n GLVELK deposuna kalite onay�ndan sonra otomatik d��me �al��mas�n�n STOK29a uyarlanmas� RD 07112024 Biti�

End Function

Function AfterSave()

	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S29T.KOD(i))
		LotNo=Trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i))
		If Skod<>"" then
			If Left(Skod,8)="58 00 00" Then
				Set QVAL=Doc.Select1("_TEMPXXXX","QVAL02E","KOD='"&Skod&"'&LOTNUMBER='"&LotNo&"'","",1)
				If QVAL.GetRecCount=0 Then
					Call AhsapKaliteFoyuAc(i)
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next

End Function

Sub BosLotlariDoldur()
	LastLotIndex = EnSonLot
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	S29T.SafePack
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
			sirano=sirano+1
			Tarih=replace(Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK29E.TARIH),"/","")
			Tarih1=Right(Tarih,2)
			Tarih2=Mid(Tarih,1,4)
			LotFormat=Tarih2&Tarih1
			S29T.LOTNUMBER(i) = LotFormat & "-" & Right("000" & (sirano + LastLotIndex),3)
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Function EnSonLot()
	EnSonLot = 0
	QStr = "TARIH='"+STOK29E.TARIH+"'&LOTNUMBER<>' '"
	Set S29T = Doc.Select1("asdad", "STOK29T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		Tire = InStr(1, S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
		if Tire > 0 Then
			EnSonLot = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function

Sub TalepOlustur()
	Set S29=Doc.Select2("1ff1dasgf","STOK29T","QCONAY='I'&REFTEXT03=''","",-1)
	If S29.GetRecCount>0 Then
		Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK47","")
		Set S47E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
		Set S47T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
		nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
		If nret<>0 then
			Msgbox "Evrak Alinamiyor"
		End If
		S47E.CODEDBOLUM="DEPO"
		S47E.CODEDKISI="0000000000000101"
		S47E.GK_1="IADE"
		Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
		For i= 1 To S29T.GetReccount
			If Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="I" and Trim(S29T.REFTEXT03(i))="" Then
				satir=S47T.AddRow()
				S47T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
				S47T.KOD(satir)=S29T.KOD(i)
				S47T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=S29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satir)=Doc.StrDate_Increment(Doc.Bugun(),10)
				S47T.REFTEXT01(satir)=S29T.SIPNO(i)
				S47T.REFTEXT02(satir)="�RETIM"
				S29T.REFTEXT03(i)="1"
			End If
		Next
		nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
		If nret=0 Then
			nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		End If
	End If
End Sub

Sub AhsapKaliteFoyuAc(satir)
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	Set S29E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	StokKodu=Trim(S29T.KOD(satir))
	Lot=Trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(satir))
	Set QVAL=Doc.Select1("_XXXXXXCHE","QVAL10T","EVRAKNO='06'","",-1)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("QVAL20","")
	Set QVAL2E=Doc1.GetTableObject("QVAL02E")
	Set QVAL2T=Doc1.GetTableObject("QVAL02T")
	nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
	If nret<>0 Then
		MsgBox "Kalite Kontrol plan f�y� olusturulamadi."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	QVAL2E.KOD=Trim(S29T.KOD(satir))
	QVAL2E.CONTAINER_1=Trim(S29T.EVRAKNO)
	QVAL2E.LOTNUMBER=Trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(satir))
	QVAL2E.SERINO=Trim(S29T.SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO(satir))
	QVAL2E.ILK_CH_KODU=Trim(S29T.CARIHESAPCODE)
	QVAL2E.ILK_SF_MIKTAR=S29T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)
	QVAL2E.ILK_SF_UNIT=S29T.SF_SF_UNIT(satir)
	QVAL2E.REFTARIH1=S29E.TARIH
	For i= 1 To QVAL.GetRecCount
		satir=QVAL2T.AddRow()
		QVAL2T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARCODE(satir)=QVAL.VARCODE(i)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARTYPE(satir)=QVAL.VARTYPE(I)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARLEN(satir)=QVAL.VARLEN(I)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARSIG(satir)=QVAL.VARSIG(I)
		QVAL2T.VARASPNAME(satir)=QVAL.VARASPNAME(I)
	Next
	nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	If nret=0 Then
		nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	End If
End Sub

Sub KaliteFoyAc(satir)

Sub TalepAcanaMailAt(MailAdresi,MailText,TalepNo)

	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	MailGittimi=False
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Trim(TalepNo) & " No'lu  Talebiniz Hk."
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=trim(MailAdresi)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=MailText
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"
	MailGittimi=True
	If MailGittimi Then
		For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
			S29T.REFTEXT01(i)="Mail Gitti"
		Next
	End If
End SubSet S29E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	S29E.TES_DEPOKODU="GKD"
	S29E.RED_DEPOKODU="��D"

Function AfterNew()
	Set S29E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
		S29E.TES_DEPOKODU="GKD"
		S29E.RED_DEPOKODU="IADE"
	End If
End Function

Function AfterCommand(cmd)
	If Trim(cmd)="CMD_STOK46_SEC2" Then
		Set S46T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T2")
		Set S29T=doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
		For i=1 To S46T.GetRecCount
			For j= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
				If Trim(S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2(i))<>"" Then
					If Trim(S46T.ARTNO(i))=Trim(S29T.SIPARTNO(j)) Then
						S29T.REFTEXT02(j)=Trim(S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2(i))
					End If
				End If
			Next
		Next
	End If
'Bu fonksiyonda STOK46 Sipari� Mod�l�ndeki Laser Kesim Numaras�n� otomatik STOK29 un REFTEXT02(Kesim Numaras�) alan�na eklemesini yap�yor. 
End Function

			'	S29TT.REFTEXT02(i)=GK3
			'End If
		If Trim(S29TT.REFTEXT01(i))="" Then
			SipNo=Trim(S29TT.SIPNO(i))
			SipArtNo=Trim(S29TT.SIPARTNO(i))
			Set S46T1=Doc.Select1("zxcxv","STOK46T1","EVRAKNO='"&SipNo&"'&ARTNO='"&SipArtNo&"'","",-1)
			If Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO)<>"" Then
				Miktar=S29TT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				MKod=Trim(S29TT.KOD(i))
				If Trim(S29TT.STOK_NONSTOK(i))<>"" Then
					MAd=Trim(S29TT.NONSTOK_AD(i))
				Else
					MAd=Trim(S29TT.KOD_STOK00_AD(i))
				End If
				Set S47E=Doc.Select1("sdsdfd","STOK47E","EVRAKNO='"&Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO)&"'","",1)
				Gk=Trim(S47E.CODEDBOLUM)
				MailAdresi=Trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("DEPT","AD_L2",Gk,-1))
				If MailAdresi<>"" Then
					MailText=MailText&vbcrlf&"Talep No:"&Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO)&"  Kodu :"& Mkod &"  "&"Malzeme A��klamas� :" &MAd&" "&"Gelen Miktar:"&Miktar&vbcrlf 	
				End If	
				Call TalepAcanaMailAt(mailAdresi,mailText,Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO))
			End If
		End If
	Next
ElseIf Trim(S29E.IADEMI)="1" Then
If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	ENo=Trim(S29T.EVRAKNO)
	Set S29EE=Doc.Select1("STOK29E_tbl","STOK29E","EVRAKNO='"&ENo&"'","",-1)
	For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S29T.KOD(i))
		SeriNo=Trim(S29T.SERINO(i))
		If Left(Skod,2)>="30" and Left(Skod,2)<="41" and SeriNo<>"" Then
			Set S01=Doc.Select1("STOK01_tbl","STOK01","SERINO='"&SeriNo&"'","",-1)
			Set S60T=Doc.Select1("STOK60T_tbl","STOK60T","SERINO='"&SeriNo&"'&KOD='"&Skod&"'","",-1)
			Set S29T2=Doc.Select1("STOK29T_tbl","STOK29T","SERINO='"&SeriNo&"'&EVRAKNO<>'"&ENo&"'","",-1)
			If S01.GetRecCount>0 and S29EE.GetRecCount=0 Then
				Msg1=Msg1&i&" . sat�r ile giri�i yap�lan seri stokta mevcut."&vbcrlf
				MsgBox Msg1
				BeforeSave=-1
				Exit For
				Exit Function
			ElseIF S01.GetRecCount=0 and S60T.GetRecCount>0  Then
				Msg2=Msg2&i&" . sat�r ile giri�i yap�lan seri daha �nce "&Trim(S60T.EVRAKNO) & " Nolu sat�� irsaliyesi ile sat�lm��."&vbcrlf&"��leme devam etmek istiyor musunuz?"
			ElseIF S01.GetRecCount=0 and S60T.GetRecCount=0 and S29T2.GetRecCount>0 Then
				Msg3=Msg3&i&" . sat�r ile giri�i yap�lan seri daha �nce "&Trim(S29T2.EVRAKNO) & " Nolu sat�� irsaliyesi ile sat�lm��."&vbcrlf&"��leme devam etmek istiyor musunuz?"
			End If
		End If
	Next
	If Msg2<>"" Then
		k=Doc.d7MsgBox(Msg2,"Dinamo ERP",MB_YESNO+MB_ICONQUESTION)
		If k=7 Then
			BeforeSave=-1
		End If
	End If
	If Msg3<>"" Then
		k=Doc.d7MsgBox(Msg3,"Dinamo ERP",MB_YESNO+MB_ICONQUESTION)
		If k=7 Then
			BeforeSave=-1
		End If
	End If
End If
End If
End Function

Function AfterSave()
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S29T.KOD(i))
		LotNo=Trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i))
		If Skod<>"" then
			If Left(Skod,8)="58 00 00" Then
				Set QVAL=Doc.Select1("_TEMPXXXX","QVAL02E","KOD='"&Skod&"'&LOTNUMBER='"&LotNo&"'","",1)
				If QVAL.GetRecCount=0 Then
					Call AhsapKaliteFoyuAc(i)
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Function

Sub BosLotlariDoldur()
	LastLotIndex = EnSonLot
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	S29T.SafePack
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
			sirano=sirano+1
			Tarih=replace(Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK29E.TARIH),"/","")
			Tarih1=Right(Tarih,2)
			Tarih2=Mid(Tarih,1,4)
			LotFormat=Tarih2&Tarih1
			S29T.LOTNUMBER(i) = LotFormat & "-" & Right("000" & (sirano + LastLotIndex),3)
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Function EnSonLot()
	EnSonLot = 0
	QStr = "TARIH='"+STOK29E.TARIH+"'&LOTNUMBER<>' '"
	Set S29T = Doc.Select1("asdad", "STOK29T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		Tire = InStr(1, S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
		if Tire > 0 Then
			EnSonLot = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function

Sub TalepOlustur()
	Set S29=Doc.Select2("1ff1dasgf","STOK29T","QCONAY='I'&REFTEXT03=''","",-1)
	IfS29.GetRecCount>0 Then
		Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK47","")
		Set S47E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
		Set S47T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
		nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
		If nret<>0 then
			Msgbox "Evrak Alinamiyor"
		End If
		S47E.CODEDBOLUM="DEPO"
		S47E.CODEDKISI="0000000000000101"
		S47E.GK_1="IADE"
		Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
		For i= 1 To S29T.GetReccount
			If Trim(S29T.QCONAY(i))="I" and Trim(S29T.REFTEXT03(i))="" Then
				satir=S47T.AddRow()
				S47T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
				S47T.KOD(satir)=S29T.KOD(i)
				S47T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=S29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satir)=Doc.StrDate_Increment(Doc.Bugun(),10)
				S47T.REFTEXT01(satir)=S29T.SIPNO(i)
				S47T.REFTEXT02(satir)="�RETIM"
				S29T.REFTEXT03(i)="1"
			End If
		Next
		nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
		If nret=0 Then
			nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		End If
	End If
End Sub

Sub AhsapKaliteFoyuAc(satir)
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	Set S29E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	StokKodu=Trim(S29T.KOD(satir))
	Lot=Trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(satir))
	Set QVAL=Doc.Select1("_XXXXXXCHE","QVAL10T","EVRAKNO='06'","",-1)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("QVAL20","")
	Set QVAL2E=Doc1.GetTableObject("QVAL02E")
	Set QVAL2T=Doc1.GetTableObject("QVAL02T")
	nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
	If nret<>0 Then
		MsgBox "Kalite Kontrol plan f�y� olusturulamadi."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	QVAL2E.KOD=Trim(S29T.KOD(satir))
	QVAL2E.CONTAINER_1=Trim(S29T.EVRAKNO)
	QVAL2E.LOTNUMBER=Trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(satir))
	QVAL2E.SERINO=Trim(S29T.SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO(satir))
	QVAL2E.ILK_CH_KODU=Trim(S29T.CARIHESAPCODE)
	QVAL2E.ILK_SF_MIKTAR=S29T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)
	QVAL2E.ILK_SF_UNIT=S29T.SF_SF_UNIT(satir)
	QVAL2E.REFTARIH1=S29E.TARIH
	For i= 1 To QVAL.GetRecCount
		satir=QVAL2T.AddRow()
		QVAL2T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARCODE(satir)=QVAL.VARCODE(i)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARTYPE(satir)=QVAL.VARTYPE(I)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARLEN(satir)=QVAL.VARLEN(I)
		QVAL2T.QS_VARSIG(satir)=QVAL.VARSIG(I)
		QVAL2T.VARASPNAME(satir)=QVAL.VARASPNAME(I)
	Next
	nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	If nret=0 Then
		nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	End If
End Sub

Sub TalepAcanaMailAt(MailAdresi,MailText,TalepNo)

	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	MailGittimi=False
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Trim(TalepNo) & " No'lu  Talebiniz Hk."
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=trim(MailAdresi)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=MailText
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"
	MailGittimi=True
	If MailGittimi Then
		For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
			S29T.REFTEXT01(i)="Mail Gitti"
		Next
	End If
End SubSet S29E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	S29E.TES_DEPOKODU="GKD"
	S29E.RED_DEPOKODU="��D"

Function BeforeSave()
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" then
		Set S29T=Doc.GetTableoBJECT("STOK29T")
		For i= 1 To S29T.GetRecCount
			If Trim(S29T.KOD(i))<>"" Then
				If Trim(S29T.SATIS_EVRAKNO(i))="" and Trim(S29T.MALIYETDEPODAN10(i))<>"1" Then		
					MaliyetBilgisiGirilmeyenSatir=MaliyetBilgisiGirilmeyenSatir+1
				End If
			End If
		Next
		If MaliyetBilgisiGirilmeyenSatir>0 Then
			MsgBox "�adeler i�in sat�� irsaliye numaras� veya depodaki maliyet alan� i�aretlenmeli.L�tfen ilgili alanlar� doldurun."
			Beforesave=-1
		End If
	End If

End function

Function BeforeSave()
	Set SA=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK2AT")
	For i= 1 To SA.GetRecCount
		If Trim(SA.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))<>"" Then
			SA.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)=Doc.RightJustify(Trim(SA.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)),10)
		End If
	Next
End Function

End function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : MAKR8S

Function AfterCommand(cmd)

	If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "D7KSUPER" Then Exit Function

	If cmd = "CMD_TEKL40_SEC" Then
		Set S40E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")

		Set S40TStack=Doc.Select2("fsdfsd", "STOK40T", "TEKLIFNO<>'' ", "", -1)

		If S40TStack.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			TeklifNoVal = S40TStack.TEKLIFNO(1)
			Set T40EStack=Doc.Select1("fsdfsd", "TEKL40E", "EVRAKNO='"& TeklifNoVal &"' ", "", -1)
			If T40EStack.GetRecCount > 0 Then S40E.REFTEXT10 = T40EStack.REFTEXT10(1)
			Set T40EStack=Nothing
		End If

		Set S40TStack=Nothing
	End If
End Function

Sub AfterLoad

If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "BTXXXXX" Then Exit Sub

	Set S40E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ZUS40E = Doc.GetTableObject("ZU_STOK40E")

	If S40E.TARIH < "2025/12/10" Then
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYGRUPOK", "READONLY", 1
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYDETAY", "READONLY", 1
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYIPTAL", "READONLY", 1

		For i = 1 To 3
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1

			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "BCKCOL", "GRAY"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "TXTCOL", "WHITE"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1

			If i=3 Then
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_ONAY_4", "READONLY", 1
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "READONLY", 1

				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "BCKCOL", "GRAY"
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "TXTCOL", "WHITE"
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "READONLY", 1
			End If
		Next

		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_EVRAKONAYOK", "READONLY", 0

	Else
		'******************* onay grubu se�im kutular� *****************
	
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_EVRAKONAYOK", "READONLY", 1

		ZUS40E.KUTU_ONAY_1 ="E"

		If ZUS40E.ONAYGRUPOK = "E" Then
			For i = 2 To 3
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1
			Next

			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYGRUPOK", "READONLY", 1
		Else
			For i = 1 To 3
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 0
			Next

			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYGRUPOK", "READONLY", 0
		End If
	
		'********************************************************

		For i = 1 To 3
			If ZUS40E.GetFieldValue("KUTU_ONAY_" & i, 1) = "E" Then
				If i=3 Then
					If Trim(ZUS40E.GetFieldValue("ONAY_KULLANICI_" & i, 1)) = "" Then  
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "BCKCOL", "YELLOW"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "TXTCOL", "BLACK"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 0
					Else
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "BCKCOL", "GREEN"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "TXTCOL", "BLACK"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1
						sayac = sayac + 1
					End If

					'YGS PROJE

					If Trim(ZUS40E.GetFieldValue("ONAY_KULLANICI_4", 1)) = "" Then  
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "BCKCOL", "YELLOW"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "TXTCOL", "BLACK"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "READONLY", 0
					Else
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "BCKCOL", "GREEN"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "TXTCOL", "BLACK"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "READONLY", 1
						sayac = sayac + 1
					End If
				Else
					If Trim(ZUS40E.GetFieldValue("ONAY_KULLANICI_" & i, 1)) = "" Then  
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "BCKCOL", "YELLOW"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "TXTCOL", "BLACK"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 0
					Else
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_"& i, "BCKCOL", "GREEN"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "TXTCOL", "BLACK"
						Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1
						sayac = sayac + 1
					End If
				End If
			Else
				If i = 3 Then
					Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "BCKCOL","GRAY"
					Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "TXTCOL","WHITE"
					Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1

					'YGS proje
					Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "BCKCOL","GRAY"
					Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "TXTCOL","WHITE"
					Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_4", "READONLY", 1
				Else
					Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "BCKCOL","GRAY"
					Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "TXTCOL","WHITE"
					Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1
				End If
			End If
		Next

		'onay tamamlanm�� ise buton pasif

		If S40E.ONAY = "E" Then
			For i = 1 To 4
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1
			Next

			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYGRUPOK", "READONLY", 1
		Else
			For i = 1 To 4
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 0
			Next
		End If

		If ZUS40E.ONAYGRUPOK = "E" Then
			For i = 2 To 3
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1
			Next

			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYGRUPOK", "READONLY", 1
		Else
			For i = 2 To 3
				Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 0
			Next

			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYGRUPOK", "READONLY", 0
		End If
	End If

	Doc.Frame_Refresh_Controls
	'********************************************************
	
	'************ testler i�in b�t�n kutular BT kullan�c�s� i�in aktif ********************
	If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "BT" Then
		For i = 1 To 3
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 0
		Next

		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYGRUPOK", "READONLY", 0
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYDETAY", "READONLY", 0
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYIPTAL", "READONLY", 0

		For i = 1 To 4
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY",  0
		Next

		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_EVRAKONAYOK", "READONLY", 0

		Doc.Frame_Refresh_Controls
	End If
	'**************************************
End Sub

	par1 = MailKonusu
	par2 = MailList
	Doc.RunMacro2 "PFRM7S.URETIM-PDF", "PrintForm2", par1, par2
End Sub

Function OzelHakVarMi(HakKodu)
	OzelHakVarMi = False
	Set U12 = Doc.Select1("de443d44t", "UDEFT12", "KOD='" & Doc.Getd7UserName() & "'&OZEL_HAK_KODU='" & HakKodu & "'", "", -1)
	If U12.GetRowCount() > 0 Then OzelHakVarMi = True
End Function

Sub OnayYetkiKontrol_AktifPAsif
	Set S40E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")

	For i = 1 To 3
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","KUTU_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 0
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAYGRUPOK", "READONLY", 0
	Next

	For i = 2 To 4
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "BCKCOL","GRAY"
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "TXTCOL","WHITE"
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & i, "READONLY", 1
	Next

	Doc.Frame_Refresh_Controls
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave()

If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "BTXXX" Then Exit Function

	Set S40T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	If Trim(STOK40E.GK_1)="YI" Then
		For i= 1 To S40T.GetRecCount
			Indx =Trim(S40T.EVRAKNO(i))&Trim(S40T.TRNUM(i))
			res= Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("KTA","STOK40T",Indx)
			Set KTA	= Doc.GetTableObject("KTA")
			res= KTA.SetFieldValue("ULKE",-1,"052")
			KTA.Exec "SAVE"
		Next
	End If

	For i= 1 To S40T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S40T.KOD(i))
		FoKod=Trim(S40T.FOKOD(i))
		Miktar=S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		SArtNo=Trim(S40T.ARTNO(i))
		ENo=Trim(S40T.EVRAKNO)
		If Left(Skod,2)="40" Then
			Set S01=Doc.Select1("vxccv","STOK01","KOD='"&Skod&"'&ALLOC_SIPNO='"&ENo&"'&ALLOC_SIPARTNO='"&SArtNo&"'","",-1)
			If S01.GetRecCount>0 Then
				If Trim(S01.FOKOD)<>FoKod Then
					msg=msg&""&vbcrlf&Skod &"  " & i &".satir"
				End If
			End If			
		End If

		If Skod <> "" Then
			If S40T.ACIKKAPALI(i) <> "K" Then
				If S40T.RTESTARIH(i)<STOK40E.TARIH Or S40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i)<STOK40E.TARIH Then
					Doc.Msgbox "Hata ( Event Makro ) : Teslim Tarihi veya Revize Teslim Tarihi Sipari� Tarihinden �nce Olamaz. Sipari� Sat�r�=[" & S40T.SRNUM(i) & "]"
					BeforeSave=1
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next

	If msg<>"" Then
		Msgbox msg & vbcrlf &" kodlu �r�nlere rezervasyon yap�lm��.Sipari�te de�i�iklik yap�lamaz.��lem iptal.(�zel event makroda tan�ml� mesaj)"
		BeforeSave=-1
	End If

'Revizyon alan� kontrol�- 24/02/2021 
	Set S40E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	SipNo=Trim(S40E.EVRAKNO)
	Set S40EE=Doc.Select1("_TEMP","STOK40E","EVRAKNO='"&SipNo&"'","",1)
	Set S40TT=Doc.Select2("xczc","STOK40T","SGK_2=''&ACIKKAPALI<>'K'","",-1)
	S40TT.GROUPBY("SGK_2")
	If S40EE.GetRecCount>0 and Doc.DataEntrymode="GUI" Then
		If Doc.IsChanged Then
			If S40TT.GetRecCount>0 Then
				K=doc.D7Msgbox ("Sipari�te revizyon varsa, revizyon olan sipari� sat�r�na revizyon bilgisi girmeyi unutmay�n.","D�KKAT",MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING)
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Function

Sub DemoUrunKontrol()

	Set S40T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	For i= 1 To S40T.GetRecCount
		TeklifNo=Trim(S40T.TEKLIFNO(i))
		TArtNo=Trim(S40T.TEKLIFARTNO(i))
		GK4=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("TEKL40T","SGK_4",TArtNo,"ARTNO")
		If Trim(GK4)<>"" Then
			S40T.SGK_4(i)=GK4
		End If
	Next
End SubFunction BeforeSave()

	Set S40T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	For i= 1 To S40T.GetRecCount
		TeklifNo=Trim(S40T.TEKLIFNO(i))
		If TeklifNo<>"" Then
			Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("TEKL40","")
			Set T40E=Doc1.GetTableObject("TEKL40E")
			Set T40T=Doc1.GetTableObject("TEKL40T")
			nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(TeklifNo)
			If nret=0 Then
				For j= 1 To T40T.GetRecCount
					T40T.SetCurrentRow(j)
					T40T.ACIKKAPALI="K"
					nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher	
					If nret=0 Then
						nter=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
					End If
				Next
			Else
				Msgbox "Teklif bulunamad�"
				Exit Function
			End If
		End If		
	Next

End Function

Function AfterCommand(Strcmd)
	If	Trim(Strcmd)="USR_SENT_FORMENT_USE1" Then
		Set FRMSEC=Doc.Select2("dfdsf","SCFFEN","SEL='1'","",-1)
		If Trim(FRMSEC.EVRAKNO)="URETIM-E" Then
			STOK40E.GK_3="UP"
			Doc.Save_Voucher()
		End If
	End If
End Function

Sub PDF_JPG_SecimKontrolu
	Set ADOC=Doc.GetTableObject("ATCDOC")

	If Trim(ADOC.GK_1) = "JPG_DOC" or Trim(ADOC.GK_1) = "PDF_DOC" Then

		If Trim(ADOC.REFTEXT02) = "" Then ADOC.REFTEXT02 = ADOC.SUBSRNUM

		If Trim(ADOC.FULLFILENAME) <> "" AND Trim(ADOC.GK_1) = "JPG_DOC"  AND Trim(ADOC.BELGETIPI) = "PDF"  Then
			Msgbox "Eklenen Dosya PDF." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "GK-1 de�eri JPG_DOC Se�ilemez."
		
			ADOC.GK_1 = ""
			ADOC.FULLFILENAME = ""
			ADOC.SAFEDIR_UPDATE_OPT = ""
		End If

		If Trim(ADOC.FULLFILENAME) <> "" AND Trim(ADOC.GK_1) = "PDF_DOC"  AND Trim(ADOC.BELGETIPI) <> "PDF"  Then
			Msgbox "Eklenen Dosya PDF De�il." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "GK-1 de�eri PDF_DOC Se�ilemez."
		
			ADOC.GK_1 = ""
			ADOC.FULLFILENAME = ""
			ADOC.SAFEDIR_UPDATE_OPT = ""
		End If

		ADOC.SAFEDIR_UPDATE_OPT = "N"
	End If
End SubFunction BeforeSave()

	Set S40T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

			Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("TEKL40","")

				For j= 1 To T40T.GetRecCount
					T40T.SetCurrentRow(j)
					T40T.ACIKKAPALI="K"

					If nret=0 Then
						nter=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
					End If

			Else
				Msgbox "Teklif bulunamad�"

End Function

Function FOKOD_OnValidate()

Exit Function
	Set S40T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	For i= 1 To S40T.GetRecCount
		FOKodu = Trim(S40T.FOKOD(i))		
		For j = 1 To 10
			opsiyon = trim(Doc.MakeFONam(FOKodu,10,50,j,20))
			If opsiyon<>"" Then
				Set FOSYDT=Doc.Select1("sdfsddfh","FOSYSDT","OPTION1='"&opsiyon&"'","",-1)
				If FOSYDT.GetRecCount>0 Then
					For jj=1 To FOSYDT.GetRecCount
						ENo=Trim(FOSYDT.EVRAKNO(jj))
						ArtNo=Trim(FOSYDT.TRNUM(jj))
						Index=ENo&ArtNo
						Fiyat=Doc.GetUDFValue("FOSYSDT",Index,"FIYAT",0)
						If Fiyat<>0 Then
							Toplam=Toplam+Fiyat
						End If
						S40T.OR_FIYAT=toplam
					Next
				End If 
			End If
		Next
		Toplam=0
	Next
End Function

Public BuSiparisTL
Function BeforeSave()
'		PrintEdildimi=Doc.Ulog00_Log_Count("P","STOK40",STOK40E.EVRAKNO,"URETIM-E")
'		If Doc.DataEntrymode="GUI" Then

		Next 
		Hata = RiskAcikHesapKontrol()
		if Hata <> "" Then
			k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Hata, "D�KKAT- R�SK L�M�T� A�IMI", MB_OK)
			BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End if
	End If

	Set S40T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	If Trim(STOK40E.GK_1)="YI" Then

			res= Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("KTA","STOK40T",Indx)
			Set KTA	= Doc.GetTableObject("KTA")
			res= KTA.SetFieldValue("ULKE",-1,"052")
			KTA.Exec "SAVE"
		Next
	End If
	For i= 1 To S40T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S40T.KOD(i))
		FoKod=Trim(S40T.FOKOD(i))
		Miktar=S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		SArtNo=Trim(S40T.ARTNO(i))
		ENo=Trim(S40T.EVRAKNO)
		If Left(Skod,2)="40" Then
			Set S01=Doc.Select1("vxccv","STOK01","KOD='"&Skod&"'&ALLOC_SIPNO='"&ENo&"'&ALLOC_SIPARTNO='"&SArtNo&"'","",-1)
			If S01.GetRecCount>0 Then
				If Trim(S01.FOKOD)<>FoKod Then
					msg=msg&""&vbcrlf&Skod &"  " & i &".satir"
				End If
			End If			
		End If
	Next

	If msg<>"" Then
		Msgbox msg&vbcrlf&" kodlu �r�nlere rezervasyon yap�lm��.Sipari�te de�i�iklik yap�lamaz.��lem iptal"
		BeforeSave=-1
	End If


End Function

Function RiskAcikHesapKontrol()
	RiskAcikHesapKontrol = ""
	Hata = ""
	CHKodu = Trim(STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU)
	RiskLimiti = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "B_RISKLIMITI", CHKodu, "")
	if RiskLimiti = 0 Then Exit Function
	AcikSiparislerTutari=0
	AcikSiparislerTutari=siparislerToplami+BuSiparisTL
	CHRisk = Doc.CSRisk_Toplam("",CHKodu,"","")
	BorcBakiyesi = Doc.CariHesapToplamlariBA(CHKodu, "BB")
	ToplamCHRisk = AcikSiparislerTutari + BorcBakiyesi+CHRisk
	If RiskLimiti > 0 and ToplamCHRisk > RiskLimiti Then
	    Hata = Hata & vbCrlf & vbCrlf & _
		    " CAR� R�SK KONTROL� " & vbCrlf & vbCrlf & _
		    "  - Bu sipari� ve t�m a��k sipari�lerin toplam de�eri Risk limitini a��yor." & vbCrlf & vbCrlf & _
		    "  - Toplam Risk Tutar�:" & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamCHRisk,12,2) & "     Tan�ml� Risk Limiti Tutar�:" & Doc.NumFormat(RiskLimiti,12,0) & vbCrlf & vbCrlf & _
		    "  - Risk Limitini A�an Tutar:" & Doc.NumFormat((ToplamCHRisk - RiskLimiti),12,2)
	End If
	RiskAcikHesapKontrol = Hata
End Function

Function siparislerToplami()
	siparislerToplami=0
	CHKodu = Trim(STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU)
	Eno=Trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO)
	Set S40T=Doc.Select1("111xddfsdf","STOK40T","MUSTERIKODU='"&ChKodu&"'&ACIKKAPALI<>'K'&EVRAKNO<'"&ENo&"'","",-1)
	For i= 1 To S40T.GetRecCount
		ToplamTutar=ToplamTutar+S40T.TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(i)
	Next
	siparislerToplami=ToplamTutar
End FunctionFunction BeforeSave()

	Set S40T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	For i= 1 To S40T.GetRecCount

		If TeklifNo<>"" Then
			Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("TEKL40","")

			If nret=0 Then
				For j= 1 To T40T.GetRecCount
					T40T.SetCurrentRow(j)
					T40T.ACIKKAPALI="K"
					nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher	
					If nret=0 Then
						nter=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
					End If

			Else
				Msgbox "Teklif bulunamad�"

		End If		
	Next

End Function

Sub OnayYetkiKontrol_AktifPAsif(OnayNo)
If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "D7KSUPER" Then Exit Sub
	Set ZUS40E=Doc.GetTableObject("ZU_STOK40E")

	If ZUS40E.GetFieldValue("KUTU_ONAY_" & OnayNo, 1) <> "E" Then
		If OnayNo = 3 Then
			OnayNo = 3
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "BCKCOL","GRAY"  'YGS
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "TXTCOL","WHITE"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "READONLY", 1

			OnayNo = 4
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "BCKCOL","GRAY" 'YGS PROJE
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "TXTCOL","WHITE"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "READONLY", 1
		Else
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "BCKCOL","GRAY"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "TXTCOL","WHITE"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "READONLY", 1
		End If
	Else
		If OnayNo = 3 Then
			OnayNo = 3
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "BCKCOL","YELLOW"  'YGS
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "TXTCOL","BLACK"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "READONLY", 0

			OnayNo = 4 
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "BCKCOL","YELLOW"  'YGS PROJE
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "TXTCOL","BLACK"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "READONLY", 0
		Else
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "BCKCOL","YELLOW"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "TXTCOL","BLACK"
			Doc.SetFormControlProperty "","BTN_ONAY_" & OnayNo, "READONLY", 0
		End If
	End If

	Doc.Frame_Refresh_Controls
End Sub

Function BeforeSave()

	Set S46T1=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T1")
	Set S46T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
	For j=1 To S46T.GetRecCount	
		S46T.SetCurrentRow(j)
		For i= 1 To S46T1.GetRecCount
			TalepNo=Trim(S46T1.TALEPNO(i))
			TArtNo=Trim(S46T1.TALEPARTNO(i))
			S46T.REFTEXT02(j)=TalepNo

			Set S47T=Doc.Select1("sdfdsf","STOK47T","ARTNO='"&TArtNo&"'","",-1)
			If Trim(S47T.REFTEXT02)<>"" Then
				S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_1=Trim(S47T.REFTEXT02)
			End If
			If Trim(S47T.REFTEXT03)<>"" Then
				S46T.REFTEXT03(j)=Trim(S47T.REFTEXT03)
			End If
			If Trim(S47T.NOTE2)<>"" Then
				S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2=S47T.NOTE2
			End If
		Next
	Next

	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
		For i= 1 to S46T.GetRecCount
			If Trim(S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_1(i))="" then
				MsgBox "Sipari� hangi b�l�m i�in a��ld� bilgisi eksik.L�tfen sipari�in a��ld��� b�l�m� se�in.��lem iptal."
				BeforeSave=-1
			End If
		Next
		For i= 1 To S46T.GetRecCount
			Skod=Trim(S46T.KOD(i))
			RevVarmi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_4",Skod,"")
			If Trim(RevVarmi)<>"" Then
				msg=msg&""&vbcrlf&Skod &"  " & i &".satir"
			End If
		Next
		If msg<>"" Then
			Msgbox msg&vbcrlf&" kodlu malzeme(ler)de revizyon talebi a��k.Sipari� a��lamaz.��lem iptal."
			BeforeSave=-1
		End If
	End If
End Function

Function BeforeCommand(StrCmd)
	If StrCmd = "USR_SENT_FORMENT_USE1" Then

		Set SCF = Doc.GetTableObject("SCFFEN") 
		If Trim(SCF.EVRAKNO) <> "OZAK-TALEP" Then Exit Function

		Set T = Doc.Select2("dsffsf", "STOK47T", "KOD<>'' ", "", -1 )
		
		If T Is Nothing Then Exit Function
		rc = T.GetRecCount
		
		Kontrol_STKGK10_AD_Lazer = True

		hataSay = 0
		msg = ""

		If rc = 0 Then Exit Function

		For i = 1 To rc
			v = Trim(T.STKGK10_AD(i))

			If v <> STKGK10_IZINLI Then
				Doc.D7MsgBox "[D�� �retim (Lazer)] i�eren taleplerde farkl� Stok Gk-10 De�erleri Girilemez.", "Uyari", 0
				Kontrol_STKGK10_AD_Lazer = False
				
				Doc.RUN_APP_OK

				BeforeCommand = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			
				Exit Function
			End If
		Next

	End If
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave()
	UserName=Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	Set S47T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
	S47T.SafePack

''''''''''''''''''RD_start
	Set S47E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
	Liste=Trim(S47E.FIYATLISTESI)
	Mesaj = ""

	For i= 1 To S47T.GetRecCount
		Miktar=S47T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		kod=Trim(S47T.KOD(i))
		
		'************* lazer kesim no ***********
		If S47E.TARIH >= "2026/04/13" Then S47T.NOTE2(i) = Trim(S47T.EVRAKNO)
		'***************************************

		TeminS=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_ORTTEMINSURES",kod,"")
		If TeminS<>0 and Trim(S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(i))="" Then		
			TeslimTarihi=Doc.Date_NumToStr(Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.Bugun) + TeminS)
			S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(i)=TeslimTarihi
		End If

		If kod<>"" Then
			Set S0U=Doc.Select1("sdfsd","STOK0U","EVRAKNO='"&kod&"'&SUNIT='*'","",-1)
			If S0U.getREcCount>1 and S47T.REFTEXT03(i)="" Then
				S47T.SetCurrentRow i
				S47T.REFTEXT03(i)=Trim(S0U.UCODE(1))
			Else 
				S47T.SetCurrentRow i
				S47T.REFTEXT03(i)=Trim(S0U.UCODE)
			End If
	
			gk8=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_8",kod,"")
			If Trim(gk8)="NS" Then
				S47T.SetCurrentRow i
				S47T.STOK_NONSTOK(i)="NS"
			End If
		End If

		If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then 
			If Trim(S47T.STKGK10_AD(i)) = "" Then
				gk10val = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_10", kod, "")
				
				S47T.STKGK10_AD(i) = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGKA","AD", gk10val, -1)
			End If

	

		End If

		If Trim(S47T.STOK_NONSTOK(i))="NS" and Trim(S47T.NONSTOK_AD(i))=""  Then
			MsgBox "Genel sarf malzeme kodunun malzeme a��klamas� yaz�lmadan talep a��lamaz.L�tfen Stok kodu olmayan malzeme ad� alan�na malzeme a��klamas�n� yaz�n.!"

			BeforeSave=-1
			Exit Function
		End If

		If Trim(S47T.REFTEXT02(i))="" and Trim(S47T.KOD(i))<>"" Then
			MsgBox "Hangi B�l�m�n Talebi oldu�u belirtilmeyen sat�rlar var.L�tfen b�l�m belirtilmeyen talep sat�rlar�ndan b�l�m se�in.��lem iptal."

			BeforeSave=-1
			Exit for
		End If

		If S47T.SF_MIKTAR(i)=0 Then
			MsgBox "Talep Miktar� S�f�r (0) Olamaz. ��lem iptal."

			BeforeSave=-1
			Exit for
		End If

		If Liste<>""Then
			Set S48T=Doc.Select1("111asdasd55","STOK48T","EVRAKNO='"& Liste &"'& KOD='"& Kod &"' & MIKTARB<='"& Miktar &"'& MIKTARE>='"& Miktar &"'","",-1)
			If S48T.GetRecCount>0 Then
				For j= 1 To S48T.GetRecCount
					Fiyat=S48T.PRICE(j)
					ListeFiyat=S48T.REF_NUMBER_1(j)
				Next
			Else
				Set S48T=Doc.Select1("222asdasd","STOK48T","EVRAKNO='"& Liste &"' & KOD='"& Kod &"' & MIKTARB<='"& Miktar &"' & MIKTARE='0' ","",-1)
				For j= 1 To S48T.GetRecCount
					Fiyat=S48T.PRICE(j)
					ListeFiyat=S48T.REF_NUMBER_1(j)
				Next
			End If

			If S47T.ECH_KODU_1_OR_FIYAT(i)="" OR S47T.ECH_KODU_1_OR_FIYAT(i)=0 then
				S47T.ECH_KODU_1_OR_FIYAT(i)=Fiyat
			End If
		End If

		If UserName<>"STLM1" and UserName<>"STLM2" and UserName<>"STLM3" Then
			PartiB=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU",kod,"")
			If PartiB>0 Then
				If S47T.SF_MIKTAR(i)>PartiB*2 Then
					Mesaj=mesaj & i & ". sat�rdaki �r�n� parti b�y�kl���nden fazla talep ediyorsunuz." & vbcrlf
				End If
			End If
		End If

		RevVarmi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","REFTEXT01",kod,"")
		RevYapan=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","REFTEXT02",kod,"")

		If RevVarmi<>"" Then
			Revbilgisi=RevVarmi
		Else
			Revbilgisi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_4",kod,"")
		end If

		If Trim(RevBilgisi)<>"" Then
			S47T.REFTEXT05(i)=RevBilgisi
			S47T.REFTEXT06(i)=RevYapan
		End If

		Index=Trim(S47T.EVRAKNO(i)) & Trim(S47T.TRNUM(i))

		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","STOK47T",Index)
		Dim T_01
		set T_01=doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")

		If Trim(S47T.REFTEXT05(i)) <>"" Then
			K=T_01.SetfieldValue("REF05",-1,S47T.REFTEXT05(i))
		End If

		If Trim(S47T.REFTEXT06(i)) <>"" Then
			K=T_01.SetfieldValue("REF06",-1,S47T.REFTEXT06(i))
		End If

		T_01.Exec "Save"
	Next

	If UserName<>"STLM1" and UserName<>"STLM2" and UserName<>"STLM3" Then
		If Mesaj<>"" Then
			Msg=Mesaj & vbcrlf & "Emin misiniz?"
			k=Doc.d7MsgBox(Msg,"DinamoERP",MB_YESNO+MB_ICONWARNING)

			If k=7 Then
				beforesave=-1
			End If
		End IF
	End If

End Function

Function AfterCommand(cmd)
	If trim(cmd)="USR_SENT_STOK19_FILL" Then
		Set S47T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
		Set SC=Doc.GetTableObject("SC19EN")
		EvrakNo=Trim(SC.STOK19E_E)
		Set S19T=Doc.Select1("sadfsdf","STOK19T","EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'","",-1)
		For i= 1 to S47T.GetRecCount
			S47T.NOTE0(i)=S19T.REFTEXT01(i)
		Next
	End If
End Function

Function AfterLoad()

	If Doc.DataEntryMode<>"GUI" Then Exit Function

	Set T47E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
	Set T47T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
	For i= 1 To T47T.GetRecCount
		Indx=Trim(T47T.EVRAKNO(i))&Trim(T47T.TRNUM(i))
		Skod=Trim(T47T.KOD(i))

		If Trim(T47T.STKGK10_AD(i)) = "" Then
			gk10val = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_10", Skod, "")
			
			T47T.STKGK10_AD(i) =Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGKA","AD", gk10val, -1)
		End If


		RevVarmi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","REFTEXT01",Skod,"")
		If Trim(RevVarmi)="" Then
			RevVarmi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_4",Skod,"")
		End If
		If Trim(RevVarmi)<>"" Then
			Rev=Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK47T",Indx,"REF05",0)
			RevP=Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK47T",Indx,"REF06",0)
			IF Trim(Rev)<>"" Then
				T47T.REFTEXT05(i)=Rev
			End If
			If Trim(RevP)<>"" Then
				T47T.REFTEXT06(i)=RevP
			End If
		End If
	Next	
End FunctionSet S47T1=Doc.getTableObject("STOK47T1")
	if Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" then
		For i = 1 To S47T1.GetRecCount
			SKur=Doc.GetKur(Trim(S47T1.OR_PB(i)),"TL",Doc.Bugun,"1",-1)
			S47T1.KUR(i)=SKur
			S47T1.TOPLAM(i)=S47T1.OR_FIYAT(i)*Skur		
			S47T1.TOPLAMTL(i)=S47T1.TOPLAM(i)*S47T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)		
		Next	
	End If
End Function


Function AfterLoad()
	Set S47T1=Doc.getTableObject("STOK47T1")
	For i = 1 To S47T1.GetRecCount
		Eno=Trim(S47T1.EVRAKNO(i))
		TrN=Trim(S47T1.TRNUM(i))
		Index=ENo&TrN
		Olcu1=Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK47T1",Index,"TOPLAM",0))
		Olcu2=Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK47T1",Index,"TOPLAMTL",0))
		S47T1.TOPLAM(i)=Olcu1
		S47T1.TOPLAMTL(i)=Olcu2
	Next	
End Function

Function OR_PB_OnValidate()
	Set S47T1=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T1")
	For i = 1 To S47T1.GetRecCount
		SKur=Doc.GetKur(Trim(S47T1.OR_PB(i)),"TL",Doc.Bugun,"1",-1)
		S47T1.KUR(i)=SKur
		S47T1.TOPLAM(i)=S47T1.OR_FIYAT(i)*Skur		
		S47T1.TOPLAMTL(i)=S47T1.TOPLAM(i)*S47T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)		
	Next	
	For i= 1 to S47T1.GetReccount
		Tutar=Trim(S47T1.TOPLAM(i))
		TTutar=Trim(S47T1.TOPLAMTL(i))
		Index=Trim(S47T1.EVRAKNO(i))&Trim(S47T1.TRNUM(i))
		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","STOK47T1",Index)
		Dim T_01
		set T_01=doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")
		K=T_01.SetfieldValue("TOPLAM",-1,Tutar)
		K=T_01.SetfieldValue("TOPLAMTL",-1,TTutar)
		T_01.Exec "Save"
	Next
End Function

Function BeforeSave()

		For i= 1 To S47T.GetRecCount
			If Trim(S47T.REFTEXT02(i))="" and Trim(S47T.KOD(i))<>"" Then
				MsgBox "Hangi B�l�m�n Talebi oldu�u belirtilmeyen sat�rlar var.L�tfen b�l�m belirtilmeyen talep sat�rlar�ndan b�l�m se�in.��lem iptal."
				BeforeSave=-1

			End If
		Next
	End If

' temin s�resinin g�n�n tarihine ekleyerek teslim tarihini 
' otomatik hesaplama eklendi - 20 Mart 2019
	For i= 1 To S47T.GetRecCount

		End If
	Next

'Aktif �retici kodu stok birime *karakteri ile kodland�.
	For i= 1 To S47T.GetRecCount

			Set S0U=Doc.Select1("sdfsd","STOK0U","EVRAKNO='"&Skod&"'&SUNIT='*'","",-1)

			Else

				S47T.REFTEXT03(i)=Trim(S0U.UCODE)
			End If	
			gk8=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_8",Skod,"")
			If Trim(gk8)="NS" Then
				S47T.SetCurrentRow i
				S47T.STOK_NONSTOK(i)="NS"
			End If
		End If
	Next
	For i= 1 To S47T.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S47T.STOK_NONSTOK(i))="NS" and Trim(S47T.NONSTOK_AD(i))=""  Then
			MsgBox "Genel sarf malzeme kodunun malzeme a��klamas� yaz�lmadan talep a��lamaz.L�tfen Stok kodu olmayan malzeme ad� alan�na malzeme a��klamas�n� yaz�n.!"
			BeforeSave=-1
			Exit Function
		End If
	Next

	UserName=Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)

	If UserName<>"STLM1" and UserName<>"STLM2" and UserName<>"STLM3" Then
		For i= 1 ToS47T.GetRecCount
			Skod=Trim(S47T.KOD(i))
			PartiB=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU",Skod,"")

				If S47T.SF_MIKTAR(i)>PartiB*2 Then
					Mesaj=mesaj&i&". sat�rdaki �r�n� parti b�y�kl���nden fazla talep ediyorsunuz."&vbcrlf

			End If
		End IF
	End If

		Skod=Trim(S47T.KOD(i))
		RevVarmi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","REFTEXT01",Skod,"")
		RevYapan=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","REFTEXT02",Skod,"")
		If Trim(RevVarmi)<>"" Then
			S47T.REFTEXT05(i)=RevVarmi
			S47T.REFTEXT06(i)=RevYapan
		End If
	Next
If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
	For i= 1 to S47T.GetReccount
		Index=Trim(S47T.EVRAKNO(i))&Trim(S47T.TRNUM(i))
		RevVarmi=Trim(S47T.REFTEXT05(i))

Function AfterLoad()

	Set T47E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
	Set T47T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
	For i= 1 To T47T.GetRecCount
		Indx=Trim(T47T.EVRAKNO(i))&Trim(T47T.TRNUM(i))
		Skod=Trim(T47T.KOD(i))
		RevVarmi=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","REFTEXT01",Skod,"")
		If Trim(RevVarmi)<>"" Then
			Rev=Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK47T",Indx,"REF05",0)
			RevP=Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK47T",Indx,"REF06",0)
			IF Trim(Rev)<>"" Then
				T47T.REFTEXT05(i)=Rev
			End If
			If Trim(RevP)<>"" Then
				T47T.REFTEXT06(i)=RevP
			End If
		End If
	Next	
End FunctionSet S47T1=Doc.getTableObject("STOK47T1")
	if Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" then
		For i = 1 To S47T1.GetRecCount
			SKur=Doc.GetKur(Trim(S47T1.OR_PB(i)),"TL",Doc.Bugun,"1",-1)
			S47T1.KUR(i)=SKur
			S47T1.TOPLAM(i)=S47T1.OR_FIYAT(i)*Skur		
			S47T1.TOPLAMTL(i)=S47T1.TOPLAM(i)*S47T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)		
		Next	
	End If
End Function


Function AfterLoad()
	Set S47T1=Doc.getTableObject("STOK47T1")
	For i = 1 To S47T1.GetRecCount
		Eno=Trim(S47T1.EVRAKNO(i))
		TrN=Trim(S47T1.TRNUM(i))
		Index=ENo&TrN
		Olcu1=Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK47T1",Index,"TOPLAM",0))
		Olcu2=Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK47T1",Index,"TOPLAMTL",0))
		S47T1.TOPLAM(i)=Olcu1
		S47T1.TOPLAMTL(i)=Olcu2
	Next	
End Function

Function OR_PB_OnValidate()
	Set S47T1=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T1")
	For i = 1 To S47T1.GetRecCount
		SKur=Doc.GetKur(Trim(S47T1.OR_PB(i)),"TL",Doc.Bugun,"1",-1)
		S47T1.KUR(i)=SKur
		S47T1.TOPLAM(i)=S47T1.OR_FIYAT(i)*Skur		
		S47T1.TOPLAMTL(i)=S47T1.TOPLAM(i)*S47T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)		
	Next	
	For i= 1 to S47T1.GetReccount
		Tutar=Trim(S47T1.TOPLAM(i))
		TTutar=Trim(S47T1.TOPLAMTL(i))
		Index=Trim(S47T1.EVRAKNO(i))&Trim(S47T1.TRNUM(i))
		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","STOK47T1",Index)
		Dim T_01
		set T_01=doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")
		K=T_01.SetfieldValue("TOPLAM",-1,Tutar)
		K=T_01.SetfieldValue("TOPLAMTL",-1,TTutar)
		T_01.Exec "Save"
	Next
End Function

Sub AfterCommand(StrCmd)

	If StrCmd = "CMD_STOK40_SEC" or StrCmd = "CMD_STOK40_SEC_2"  Then
exit sub
		Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")

		For i = 1 To S60T.GetRecCount
			S60T.SetCurrentRow i
			SatisSiparisNo = Trim(S60T.SIPNO(i))
			SanalPaletKodu = Trim(S60T.KOD(i))

			If Left(Trim(S60T.KOD(i)), 2) = "58" Then

				SQL = " SELECT STOK00.KOD, STOK01.SF_MIKTAR, STOK01.AMBCODE, STOK01.LOTNUMBER FROM STOK00 "
				SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN STOK01 ON STOK01.KOD = STOK00.KOD "

				SQL = SQL & " WHERE 1=1 "
				SQL = SQL & " AND AD LIKE '%"& SatisSiparisNo &"%' "
				SQL = SQL & " AND STOK01.SF_MIKTAR > 0 "

				Set S00SQL = Doc.RunSqlQuery("S00_SQL", SQL)

				If S00SQL.GetRecCount > 0 Then
					For j = 1 to S00SQL.GetRecCount
						If j = 1 Then
							S60T.KOD(i)= S00SQL.KOD(j)
							S60T.LOTNUMBER(i)= S00SQL.LOTNUMBER(j)
							S60T.ST_BILGISATIRIE(i)=""
							S60T.AMBCODE_TR(i)=S00SQL.AMBCODE(j)
							S60T.FATURASIZMI(i)="E"
							S60T.SP_BILGISATIRI10(i)="1"
							S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)=S00SQL.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							S60T.SIPNO(i)=SatisSiparisNo
						Else
							SatirNo = S60T.AddRow
							S60T.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
	
							S60T.KOD= S00SQL.KOD(j)
							S60T.LOTNUMBER= S00SQL.LOTNUMBER(j)
							S60T.ST_BILGISATIRIE=""
							S60T.AMBCODE_TR=S00SQL.AMBCODE(j)
							S60T.FATURASIZMI="E"
							S60T.SP_BILGISATIRI10="1"
							S60T.SF_MIKTAR=S00SQL.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							S60T.SIPNO=SatisSiparisNo
						End If
					Next

				ElseIf  S00SQL.GetRecCount = 0 Then
					msgbox "[ " &  Trim(SanalPaletKodu) & " ] Kodun Sat�� Sipari�i ile �li�kili Stok Kodu Bulunamad�." 
				End If
			End If
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub BeforeCommand(StrCmd)
	'A��klama : Sevk �rsaliyesinde (e-�rsaliye Olu�tur) butonuna t�kland���nda 
	' cari hesap kart�na gidiyor, (e-irsaliye bilgileri) sekmesindeki alias alan� bo� ise
	'arka planda cari kart�n� y�kl�yor, (e-fatura e-irsaliye Bilgileri G�ncelleme) sekmesindeki
	'(web servis ile e-irsaliye m�kellef listesinden kontrol et (alias) i�levini �al��t�r�yor.
	'e�er sonu� d�ner ise (e-irsaliye bilgileri) sekmesindeki alias alan� doluyor ve bu bilgiyi
	 'cari karttaki (e-fatura bilgileri) sekmesindeki alias alan�na yaz�yor ve kaydediyor.

	If StrCmd = "MT_RUNMACRO_NOCALC('MK1SET.STOK60','EDESPATCH_CREATE')" Then
		Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")

		Set C00X1 = Doc.Select1("CARI00X1DB","CARI00X1","KOD = '"& S60E.CH_KODU &"' & CH_EINV_INFO_FX_10 = 1 ", "", -1)
		
		If C00X1.GetRecCount > 0 Then Exit Sub

		Set C00DB = Doc.Select1("C00_DB","CARI00","KOD = '"& S60E.CH_KODU &"' & ESHP_RECEIVER_ALIAS <> '' ", "", -1)

		If C00DB.GetRecCount = 0 Then
			Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("CARI00","")
			Set C00 = Doc1.GetTableObject("CARI00")
		
			Doc1.Load_Voucher S60E.CH_KODU
			
			Doc1.RunCommand("MT_RUNMACRO_NOCALC('MK1SET.CARI00','CONTROL_WAYBILL_ALIAS')")

			If Trim(C00.ESHP_RECEIVER_ALIAS) <> "" Then
				C00.EINV_RECEIVER_ALIAS = C00.ESHP_RECEIVER_ALIAS
			End If

			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		End If
	End If
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave()
	Hata=AsamaKodluUrunKontrol
	If Hata<>"" Then
		MsgBox Hata
		BeforeSave=-1
		Exit Function
	End If
	Set S60E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set S60T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	starih=S60E.TARIH
	Call GarantiSuresiHesapla()
	for i= 1 To S60T.getrecCount	
		Skod=Left(Trim(S60T.KOD(i)),2)
		kod=Trim(S60T.KOD(i))
		seri=Trim(S60T.SERINO(i))
		skt=S60T.REFTARIH1(i)
		Set SRV=Doc.Select1("__CXC","SRVSC0","KOD='"&seri&"'","",1)
		If Skod="40" and seri<>"" and SRV.GetRecCount=0 Then	
			Call ServisKartiAc(kod,seri,skt)
		End If
		If Trim(S60E.REFTEXT01)="" then
			If Skod="40" and seri<>"" Then
				Set QVALE=Doc.Select1("__mxxx","QVAL02E","LOTNUMBER='"&seri&"'","",1)
				If QVALE.GetRecCount>0 Then
					Call KaliteBilgilerineCariyiYaz(seri,starih)
				End If
			End IF
		End If
	Next
	If Trim(S60E.GK_2)="T" Then
		S60E.NOTES_1="TA�IMA AMA�LIDIR, FATURA ED�LMEYECEKT�R."
	End If
	Set S60T=doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
' Ambalaj malzemeleri stoklanmad��� i�in ambalaj konsiye sat�rlar� bilgi sat�r� olarak i�aretlendi.
' Uygulama, yurti�i/yurtd��� sat�� ile yap�lan toplant�dan bildrilen sonuca g�re geli�tirildi.07/11/2018
	For i= 1 to S60T.getRecCount
		If Trim(S60T.FATURASIZMI(i))="E" AND Trim(S60T.REFTEXT03(i))="" Then
		'If Trim(S60T.FATURASIZMI(i))="E" AND Trim(S60T.REFTEXT03(i))="" and S60T.SP_BILGISATIRI10(i)<>"1" Then
			S60T.ST_BILGISATIRIE(i)="E"
		end If
	Next

' Revizyon olan sipari� sat�r�n�n sevkiyat�n� engelleyen b�l�m	
	For i= 1 to S60T.GetRecCount
		SipANo=Trim(S60T.SIPARTNO(i))
		If SipAno<>"" Then
			Set S40T=Doc.Select1("vCVCV","STOK40T","ARTNO='"&SipANo&"'","",1)
			If Trim(S40T.SGK_2)<>"" Then
				Hata=S60T.KOD(i)&vbcrlf&"kodlu �r�nlerde revizyon var.Kalite onay� olmadan sevk edilemez."
			End If
		End If
	Next
	If Hata<>"" Then
		MsgBox Hata
		BeforeSave=-1
	End If
' Revizyon sipari� kontrol sonu
End Function

Sub ServisKartiAc(kod,seri,skt)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("SRVSC0","")
	Set SRV=Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVSC0")
	Set S60E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(seri)
	If nret=0 Then
		SRV.SERINO=Seri
		SRV.AD1=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","AD",kod,"")
		SRV.MUSTERI=S60E.CH_KODU
		SRV.STOKKODU=kod
		SRV.GARANTIBITISTARIH=skt
		nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
		If nret=0 then
			nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		End If
	End If
End Sub

Sub GarantiSuresiHesapla()
	Set S60T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	For i= 1 To S60T.GetRecCount
		SipNo=Trim(S60T.SIPARTNO(i))
		Set S40T=Doc.Select1("xgxdxc","STOK40T","ARTNO='"&SipNo&"'","",-1)
		If S40T.REFNUM01<>0 Then
			Garanti=S40T.REFNUM01*30+5
			S60T.REFTARIH1(i)=Doc.StrDate_Increment(S60T.TARIH,Garanti)
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Function AsamaKodluUrunKontrol()
	Set S60T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	For i= 1 To S60T.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S60T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))<>"" Then
			Mesaj=Mesaj&S60T.SRNUM(i)&vbcrlf
		End If
	Next
	If Mesaj<>"" Then
		AsamaKodluUrunKontrol=Mesaj&". sat�rlardaki �r�nler a�ama kodlu sevk edilemez.��lem iptal"
	End If
End Function

Sub KaliteBilgilerineCariyiYaz(serino,tarih)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("QVAL20","")
	Set QVALE=Doc1.GetTableObject("QVAL02E")
	Set QVALT=Doc1.GetTableObject("QVAL02T")
	Set S60E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	If SeriNo<>"" then
		Set QVAL=Doc.Select1("_tempQ","QVAL02T","LOTNUMBER='"&SeriNo&"'","",1)
		If QVAL.GetRecCount>0 then
			ENo=Trim(QVAL.EVRAKNO)
			loadV=Doc1.Load_Voucher(ENo)
			If loadV=0 Then
				QVALT.REFTEXT03(1)=S60E.CH_KODU
				QVALT.REFTARIH1(1)=tarih
				nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
				If nret=0 Then
					nnn=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
				End If
			End If		
		End If
	End If
	S60E.REFTEXT01="KAL�TE B�LG�LER� YAZILDI."
End SubSEVK �RSAL�YE�SNDEN SATI� S�PAR��� (SATIR BAZINDA) SE��L�P, AKTAR DEND���NDE MAKRO DEVREYE G�RER.

Function BeforeSave()

	Set S61T=Doc.Select2("sdsds","STOK61T","ST_BILGISATIRIE='E'","",-1)
	If S61T.getrecCount>0 Then
		If Trim(S60E.NITELIK)<>"KONSINYE" Then
			MsgBox "Konsiye ��k��larda NITELIK alan�ndan KONSINYE se�ilmelidir."
			BeforeSave=-1	
			Exit Function
		End If
	End If
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" and Trim(S60E.NITELIK)<>"KONSINYE" Then

'************
		S60E.ALANAMBARDASREZ="E"
'************

		Doc.RunCommand("HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL")
	End If
End Function

Function AfterSave()
Set S60E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
Eno=Trim(S60E.EVRAKNO)
If S60E.Targetambcode = "FASON2" Then
	Call MailGonder(Eno)
End If

End Function


Sub MailGonder(Eno)
	Mailler="planlama@ozak-t.com" &";" & "depo@ozak-t.com"
	Mesaj = "Evrak No : " & trim(Eno) & " no'lu Fason2 Depo Malzeme Girisi yap�lm��t�r." 
	Konu = "Talep No : " & trim(ENo) & " - ## NO'LU FASON2 Depo ya Malzeme Girisi##"
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Konu
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=Mailler
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=Mesaj
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"
End Sub

Function REFNUM01_AfterValidate()
	Set S63T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	For i= 1 To S63T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S63T.KOD(i))
		Agirlik=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",Skod,"")
		S63T.SF_MIKTAR(i)=Agirlik*S63T.REFNUM01(i)
	Next
End Function

Function BeforeSave()
	Doc.RunCommand("HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL")
	Set S63T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	set dm=Doc.CreateSObject("DocumentMode")
	dm.BeginMode "COMMAND_WITHOUT_GUI_ERROR_DISPLAY"
	Set be = Doc.CreateSObject("BatchErrorMode")
	Doc.Clean_BatchError_Logs
	For i= 1 To S63T.GetRecCount
		GonderilenMiktar=S63T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		If Trim(S63T.REFTEXT02(i))<>"" and GonderilenMiktar>0 Then
			SipNo=Trim(S63T.REFTEXT02(i))
			SipANo=Trim(S63T.REFTEXT03(i))
			Set S46T=Doc.SElect1("_TEMPXXX","STOK46T","EVRAKNO='"&SipNo&"'&ARTNO='"&SipANo&"'&ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_8=0","",1)
			If S46T.GetRecCount>0 Then
				Call SAS_Siparisine_Fason_Bilgisini_Kaydet(SipNo,SipANo,GonderilenMiktar)
			End If	
		End If
	Next

	Set S60E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")

	Tedarikci=Trim(S60E.CH_KODU)
	CariGK5Val = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00", "GK_5", Tedarikci, ""))

	If CariGK5Val = "FASONSTKC" Then   ' cari kart�nda GK_5 de FASONSTKC tan�ml� olanlar i�in stok ��k�� fi�i olu�tur
		For i= 1 To S63T.GetRecCount
'Msgbox "Kesim Numaras�:" & S63T.REFTEXT01(i) & "Kesim Numaras� Karakter Sayisi:" & LEN(TRIM(S63T.REFTEXT01(i)))

			If Trim(S63T.REFTEXT01(i))="" OR LEN(TRIM(S63T.REFTEXT01(i)))>12 Then
				NoKesim=NoKesim+1
			End If
		Next
	End If
	If NoKesim>0 Then
		MsgBox "Kesim Numaras� Belirtilemeyen veya Kesim Numaras� 12 Karakterden Uzun sat�rlar var! L�tfen kesim numaras�n� Kontrol ediniz."
	End If

	'**************** Fason ��k�� fi�ine girilen kesim numaralar� sat�nalma sipari�inde yok ise kaydetmeye izin verme **********************************
	KesimNoOlmayanVar = False
	KesimNolar = ""

	Set S63TDB=Doc.Select2G("AAAAAAAAA", "STOK63T", "REFTEXT01 <> '' ", "", "REFTEXT01", -1)

	If S63TDB.GetRecCount > 0 Then
		For n= 1 To S63TDB.GetRecCount			
			Set S46TDB=Doc.Select1("_TEMP66XXX","STOK46T","ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2='"& S63TDB.REFTEXT01(n) & "' ", "", -1)
			If S46TDB.GetRecCount = 0 Then
				KesimNoOlmayanVar = True
				KesimNolar = Trim(S63TDB.REFTEXT01(n)) & "," & KesimNolar
			End If
		Next

		If KesimNoOlmayanVar Then
			MsgBox "Kesim Numaras� Sat�nalma Sipari�inde Bulunamad�...Kontrol ediniz." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "KesimNolar : " & KesimNolar & vbcrlf & vbcrlf &  "Kay�t ��lemi �ptal"
			beforesave=-1
		End If
	End If
	'**********************************************************************************************************************************************
End Function

Function BeforeDelete()
	Set S63T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	For i= 1 To S63T.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S63T.REFTEXT02(i))<>"" Then
			SipNo=Trim(S63T.REFTEXT02(i))
			Call SAS_Siparisinden_Fason_Bilgisini_Sil(SipNo)
		End If
	Next
End Function
Sub SAS_Siparisine_Fason_Bilgisini_Kaydet(SipNo,SipANo,Miktar)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK46","")
	Set S46E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46E")
	Set S46T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
	nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(SipNo)
	If nret=0 Then
		For i= 1 To S46T.GetRecCount
			S46T.SetCurrentRow i
			If S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_8(i)<=Miktar Then
				S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_8(i)=S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_8(i)+Miktar
			End If
			If S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_8(i)>=S46T.SF_MIKTAR(i) and Trim(S46T.ARTNO(i))=SipANo Then
				S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_8(i)="FASON �IKI�A ALINDI."
			End IF
			say=say+1
		Next
		If say>0 Then
			nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			If nret=0 Then
				nn=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Sub
Sub SAS_Siparisinden_Fason_Bilgisini_Sil(SipNo)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK46","")
	Set S46E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46E")
	Set S46T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
	nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(SipNo)
	If nret=0 Then
		For i= 1 To S46T.GetRecCount
			'If Trim(S46T.ARTNO(i))=SipANo Then
				S46T.SetCurrentRow i
				S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_8(i)=""
				S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_8(i)=0
				say=say+1
			'End If
		Next
		If say>0 Then
			nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			If nret=0 Then
				nn=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Sub

Function AfterSave()
	If Doc.GetDocType="STOK63" then
		Set S60E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
		ENo=Trim(S60E.EVRAKNO)
		Set S63TT=Doc.Select2("_RAMTEMP","STOK63T","KOD<>''","",-1)
		If ENo<>"" and Trim(S60E.NITELIK)="" Then
			Set S21EE=Doc.Select1("TEMPEES","STOK21E","NOTES='"&ENo&"'","",1)
			If S21EE.GetRecCount=0 then
				Call Sarf_Cikis()
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Function

Sub Sarf_Cikis()
Dim kontrol63
kontrol63=0
	Set S63T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	Set S60E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set S63TT=Doc.Select2("sdsdsd","STOK63T","KOD<>''&SF_VRI_RECETE=''","",-1)
	If S63TT.GetRecCount>0 Then
		Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK21","")
		Set S21E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")
		Set S21T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")
		nrt=Doc1.New_Voucher()
		If nrt=0 Then
			FisNo=Trim(S21E.EVRAKNO)
			S21E.NITELIK="FASON"
			'S21E.AMBCODE="FASON"
			S21E.AMBCODE=S60E.TARGETAMBCODE
			S21T.SafePack()
			For i= 1 To S63T.GetRecCount
				Skod=Trim(S63T.KOD(i))
				FasonDusumKontrol=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_5",Skod,"")
				If FasonDusumKontrol<>1 Then
					kontrol63=1
					Miktar=S63T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					Lot=Trim(S63T.LOTNUMBER(i))
					Seri=Trim(S63T.SERINO(i))
					FoKod=Trim(S63T.FOKOD(i))
					Asama=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
					Num1=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))
					Num2=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))
					If Asama="" and Left(Skod,2)<>"16" and Left(Skod,8)<>"20 02 12" Then
						satir=S21T.AddRow()
						S21T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
						S21T.KOD(satir)=Skod
						S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR(satir)=miktar
						S21T.LOTNUMBER(satir)=Lot
						S21T.SERINO(satir)=Seri
						S21T.FOKOD(satir)=FoKod
						S21T.SF_VRI_NUM1(satir)=Num1
						S21T.SF_VRI_NUM2(satir)=Num2
						S21T.KARSI_MH_A(satir)="000000000000000000000012"
					End If
				End If
			Next
'Otomatik bo� kay�t STOK21 a�mal� engelleme ba�lang��
			if kontrol63=1 then
				Doc1.RunCommand("HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL")
				S21E.NOTES=Trim(S60E.EVRAKNO)
				nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			end if
'Otomatik bo� kay�t STOK21 a�mal� engelleme biti�

			If nret=0 Then
				If Trim(S60E.NITELIK)="" Then
					S60E.NITELIK="FASON"
					S60E.NOTES_4=Trim(fisNo)
					Doc.Save_Voucher()
				end If
				nrtt=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Sub23/09/2020

			If S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_8(i)>=S46T.SF_MIKTAR(i) and Trim(S46T.ARTNO(i))=SipANo Then
				S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_8(i)="FASON �IKI�A ALINDI."
			End IF
			say=say+1
		Next
		If say>0 Then
			nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			If nret=0 Then
				nn=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Sub

Function AfterSave()
	Set S60E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Cari=Trim(S60E.CH_KODU)
	FasonTipi=Trim(Doc.getDbFieldValue("CARI00","GK_7",Cari,""))
	If FasonTipi<>"KUMLAMA" then
		Call Sarf_Cikis()
	End If
End Function

Sub Sarf_Cikis()

	Set S63T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	Set S60E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Tedarikci=Trim(S60E.CH_KODU)
	ENo=Trim(S60E.EVRAKNO)
	IfENo<>"" Then
		Set S21EE=Doc.Select1("TEMPEES","STOK21E","NOTES='"&ENo&"'","",-1)
		If S21EE.GetRecCount=0 then
			Set S63TT=Doc.Select2("sdsdsd","STOK63T","KOD<>''&SF_VRI_RECETE=''","",-1)
			If S63TT.GetRecCount>0 Then
				Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK21","")
				Set S21E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")
				Set S21T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")
				nrt=Doc1.New_Voucher()
				If nrt=0 Then
					FisNo=Trim(S21E.EVRAKNO)
					S21E.NITELIK="FASON"
					S21E.AMBCODE="FASON"
					S21T.SafePack()
					For i= 1 To S63T.GetRecCount
						Skod=Trim(S63T.KOD(i))
						FasonDusumKontrol=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_5",Skod,"")
						If FasonDusumKontrol<>1 Then
							Miktar=S63T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
							Lot=Trim(S63T.LOTNUMBER(i))
							Seri=Trim(S63T.SERINO(i))
							FoKod=Trim(S63T.FOKOD(i))
							Asama=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_RECETE(i))
							Num1=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))
							Num2=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))
							If Asama="" and Left(Skod,2)<>"16" andLeft(Skod,8)<>"20 02 12" Then
								satir=S21T.AddRow()
								S21T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
								S21T.KOD(satir)=Skod
								S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR(satir)=miktar
								S21T.LOTNUMBER(satir)=Lot
								S21T.SERINO(satir)=Seri
								S21T.FOKOD(satir)=FoKod
								S21T.SF_VRI_NUM1(satir)=Num1
								S21T.SF_VRI_NUM2(satir)=Num2
								S21T.KARSI_MH_A(satir)="000000000000000000000012"
							End If
						End If
					Next
					Doc1.RunCommand("HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL")
					S21E.NOTES=Trim(S60E.EVRAKNO)
					nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
					If nret=0 Then
						If Trim(S60E.NITELIK)="" Then
							S60E.NITELIK="FASON"
							S60E.NOTES_4=Trim(fisNo)
							Doc.Save_Voucher()
						end If
						nrtt=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
					End If
				End If
			End If
		else
			Exit Sub
		End If
	Else
		Exit Sub
	End If
End Sub23/09/2020

Function BeforeSave()

	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	Set S68TI=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68TI")
	For j= 1 to S68TI.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S68TI.KARSITRNUM(j))="" Then
			kodd=Trim(S68TI.KARSICODE(j))
			Set S66=Doc.Select2("fsaas","STOK68T","KOD='"&kodd&"'","",-1)
			S68TI.KARSITRNUM=S66.KARSITRNUM
		End If
	Next

End Function

Function AfterNew()
	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
	S68E.TES_DEPOKODU="GKD"
	S68E.RED_DEPOKODU="��D"
End Function

Function BeforeSave()
	If Trim(STOK68E.GK_1)="" Then 
		MsgBox "Grup Kodu 1 alanini doldurmadan isleme devam edilemez.Islem iptal"
		BeforeSave=-1
		Exit Function
	End If
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
	Set S68TI=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68TI")
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
		If Trim(S68E.GK_1)="FASON" Then 
			For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
				If S68T.QCONAY(i)="E" Then
					Skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(i))
					Call ProsesKontrol(Skod,i)
				End If
			Next
		End If
	End If

	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
		Call BosLotlariDoldur()
		For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
			If Trim(S68T.MPSNO(i))<>"" Then
'				Doc.RunCommand("CMD_HMM_FASONDAN_GELEN_OLARAK_DOLDUR")
				For j= 1 to S68TI.GetRecCount
					If Trim(S68T.KARSITRNUM(i))= Trim(S68T.KARSITRNUM(j)) Then
						'S68TI.LOTNUMBER(j)=S68T.LOTNUMBER(i)
						S68TI.SF_VRI_RECETE(j)=S68T.REFTEXT02(i)
					End If
				Next
				FasCikisENo=Trim(S68T.REFTEXT03(i))
				Miktar=S68T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				kod=Trim(S68T.KOD(i))
			'	Call FasonCikisKapat(FasCikisEno,kod,miktar)
			End If
		Next
	End If
End Function

Function AfterSave()
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
		MpsNo=Trim(S68T.MPSNO(i))
		If mpsno<>"" Then
			Miktar=S68T.SF_MIKTAR(i)	
			FireMiktar=S68T.REFNUM01(i)
			skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(i))
			lot=trim(S68T.REFTEXT01(i))
			asama=Trim(S68T.REFTEXT02(i))
			Set MPS10E=Doc.Select1("dfssdf","MMPS10E","EVRAKNO='"&MpsNo&"'","",-1)
			MPSMiktar=MPS10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI
			If Miktar<MPSMiktar Then
				If FireMiktar>0 Then
					toplam=Miktar+FireMiktar
					If toplam>=MPSMiktar Then
						Call MPSKapat(MpsNo)
						If mpsno<>"" Then
							Call FireDepoyaTransferEt(skod,lot,seri,asama,FireMiktar)
						End If
					End If
				End IF
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Function

Sub ProsesKontrol(Kodd,satir)
		Set S68t=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
		Skod=Trim(Kodd)
		Set BOMT=Doc.Select1("zsasda","BOMU01T","BOMREC_CODE='"&Skod&"'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'",">BOMREC_SIRANO",-1)
		For j= 1 To BOMT.GetRecCount
			Tezgahx=Trim(BOMT.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(1))
			If Left(Tezgahx,5)="FASON" Then
				Tezgah=Trim(BOMT.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(1))
			End If
		Next
		Depo=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("IMLT00","GK_6",Tezgah,"")
		If Trim(Depo)<>"" then
			S68T.AMBCODE_TR(satir)=Depo
		End If
End Sub

Sub BosLotlariDoldur()
	LastLotIndex = EnSonLot
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
			Tarih=replace(Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK68E.TARIH),"/","")
			Tarih1=Right(Tarih,2)
			Tarih2=Mid(Tarih,1,4)
			LotFormat=Tarih2&Tarih1
			If Trim(S29T.MPSNO(i))<>"" Then
				S29T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Trim(S29T.MPSNO(i))
			Else
				S29T.LOTNUMBER(i) = LotFormat & "-" & Right("000" & (i + LastLotIndex),3)
			End If
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Function EnSonLot()
	EnSonLot = 0
	QStr = "TARIH='"+STOK68E.TARIH+"'&LOTNUMBER<>' '"
	Set S29T = Doc.Select1("asdad", "STOK68T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		Tire = InStr(1, S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
		if Tire > 0 Then
			EnSonLot = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function


Sub MPSKapat(mpsno)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("MMPS10","")
	Set MPSE=Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(mpsno)
	MPSE.ACIK_KAPALI="K"
	nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()	
	If nret=0 Then
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	End If
End Sub

Sub FireDepoyaTransferEt(kod,lot,seri,asama,Firemiktar)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK26","")
	Set S26E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set S26T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26T")
	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
	nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S26E.AMBCODE=S68E.IMALATAMBCODE
	S26E.TARGETAMBCODE="FKAYIP"
	satir=S26T.AddRow
	S26T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
	S26T.KOD(satir)=kod
	S26T.SF_SF_UNIT(satir)=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",kod,"")
	S26T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=FireMiktar
	S26T.LOTNUMBER(satir)=lot
	S26T.SF_VRI_RECETE(satir)=asama
	nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()	
	If nret=0 Then
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	End If
End Sub


Sub FasonCikisKapat(Evrakno,kod,miktar)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK63","")
	Set S63T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	Set S63E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK63E")
	nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(EvrakNo)
	For i= 1 To S63T.GetRecCount
		If kod=Trim(S63T.KOD(i)) Then
			If miktar>=S63T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				S63T.REFTEXT03(i)="KAPANDI"
			End If
		End IF
	Next
	neret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
End SubSub ProsesKontrol()
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
		Skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(i))
		Set BOMT=Doc.Select1("zsasda","BOMU01T","BOMREC_CODE='"&Skod&"'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'","",-1)
		For j= 1 To BOMT.GetRecCount
			Tezgahx=Trim(BOMT.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j))
			If Left(Tezgahx,5)="FASON" Then
				Tezgah=Trim(BOMT.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j))
				Exit For
			End If
		Next
		Depo=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("IMLT00","GK_6",Tezgah,"")
		If Trim(Depo)<>"" Then
			If Trim(S68T.QCONAY(i))="E" Then
				S68T.AMBCODE_TR(i)=Depo
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : MAKR8S
Makro Kodu : STOK68-2
Hangi Tablo icin : STOK68
Ilave Hangi Uygulamalar icin :
