	'Msgbox "S68 de Ka� Farkl� Kesim Numaras� Var? " & ss-1
	
				For jj= 1 To S63T.GetRecCount
					For i68=1 to ss-1
						If S63T.REFTEXT01(jj)=KNo(i68) then
		'Msgbox "U�acak Kay�t Bilgisi: i68="&i68 & "    jj="& jj & "    ii=  "& ii & "Stok Kodu" &Trim(S63T.KOD(jj))
							Skod=Trim(S63T.KOD(jj))
							Miktar=S63T.SF_MIKTAR(jj)
							Lot=Trim(S63T.LOTNUMBER(jj))
							Seri=Trim(S63T.SERINO(jj))
							FoKod=Trim(S63T.FOKOD(jj))
							Asama=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_RECETE(jj))
							Num1=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_NUM1(jj))
							Num2=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_NUM2(jj))
							
							Set S01DB = Doc.Select1("fdsf", "STOK01", "KOD='"& Skod &"' & LOTNUMBER='"& Lot &"' & SERINO='"& Seri &"' & VRI_NUM1 = '"& Num1 &"' & VRI_NUM2 = '"& Num2 &"' & AMBCODE='"& S68E.IMALATAMBCODE &"' ", "", -1)
							If S01DB.GetRecCount > 0 Then
								satir=S21T.AddRow
								S21T.SetCurrentRow satir
									
								S21T.KOD = Skod
								S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR = miktar
								S21T.LOTNUMBER = Lot
								S21T.SERINO = Seri
								S21T.FOKOD = FoKod
								'**********************************************
								S21T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = Num1
								S21T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = Num2
								'**********************************************
								S21T.KARSI_MH_A = "000000000000000000000012"
								S21T.REFTEXT01=Eno63(ii) & " - " & KNo(i68) 
							End If
						End If
					Next
					Set S01DB = Nothing
				Next

				Doc1.RunCommand("HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL")
				S21E.NOTES="RD:" & Trim(S63T.EVRAKNO)

				S21T.SafePack

				If S21T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
					nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
					If nret=0 Then
						nrtt=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
					End If
				Else
					nrtt=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Function BeforeSave()

	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then

		Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
		Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
		Set S68TI=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68TI")

		FasonTipi=Trim(S68E.GK_1)
		AlanDepoVal=Trim(S68E.AMBCODE)

		If Trim(S68E.XIRSALIYETARIH)<>"" Then
			If Trim(S68E.REFDATE01)="" Then
				S68E.REFDATE01=S68E.TARIH
			End If
			S68E.TARIH=S68E.XIRSALIYETARIH
		Else
			If Trim(S68E.REFDATE01)="" Then
				S68E.REFDATE01=S68E.TARIH
			End If	
		End If
		If FasonTipi="" then
			MsgBox "Fason Tipi Se�ilmemi�.��lem �ptal"
			BeforeSave=-1
			Exit Function
		End If
		Dim say
		say=0
		Call BosLotlariDoldur()
		If FasonTipi="KUMLAMA" then
			For i= 1 to S68T.GetRecCount
				S68T.F_S_LOTNUMBER_10(i)="1"
			Next
		End IF
		For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
			Skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(i))
			SArtNo=Trim(S68T.SIPARTNO(i))
			Set S46T=Doc.Select1("__TEMP","STOK46T","ARTNO='"&SArtNo&"'","",1)
			If S46T.GetRecCount>0 then
				S68T.REFTEXT01(i)=Trim(S46T.NOTE0)
				S68T.REFTEXT02(i)=S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2
			end If
			GK7=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",Skod,""))
			If GK7="" Then
				S68T.QCONAY(i)="E"
			End If
		Next
		If FasonTipi="GALVANIZ" Then
			Doc.RunCommand("CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_001")
		ElseIf FasonTipi="KUMLAMA" Then
			Doc.RunCommand("CMD_HMM_FASONDAN_GELEN_OLARAK_DOLDUR")
			For i= 1 To S68TI.GetRecCount
				S68TI.LOTNUMBER(i)=""
				S68TI.SERINO(i)=""
			Next
		ElseIf FasonTipi="MONTAJ" Then
			Doc.RunCommand("CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_001")
		End If

		'16 L� kodlar�n GLVELK deposuna kalite onay�ndan sonra otomatik d��me �al��mas�nda iyile�tirme RD 07112024 Ba�lang��
		Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
		If S68T.GetRecCount>0 Then
			For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
				Skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(i))
				If Left(Skod,2)="16" and (Trim(S68T.QCONAY(i))="E" OR Trim(S68T.QCONAY(i))="S")  Then
					'MSGBOX "Evet �r�n 16 l� kod ve Kalite Onay� Evet"
					S68T.AMBCODE_TR(i)="GLVELK"
				End If
			Next
		End If
	
		'16 L� kodlar�n GLVELK deposuna kalite onay�ndan sonra otomatik d��me �al��mas�nda iyile�tirme RD 07112024 Biti�
		
	'************** LAZER KES�M SON PART� M� KONTROL� ***************************
		If Trim(S68E.GK_1) = "LAZER" Then
			Call LazerKesimNoTanimliKayitlar(LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar, KesimNoVal)
	
			If Trim(S68E.GK_2) <> "SON" AND Trim(S68E.GK_2) <> "ONCEKI"  Then

'If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "BT" Then
'End If
				Set S68TDBGX = Doc.Select2G("S68T_DBX", "STOK68T", "KOD<>'' & REFTEXT02 <>'' ", "", "REFTEXT02", -1)
			
				If S68TDBGX.GetRecCount > 0 and AlanDepoVal = "HM" Then

					k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Lazer Kesim Son Parti mi ?", "Dikkat", MB_YESNO)

					If k = 6 Then
						Call FasonHammadde_Masraflarinin_OnHesaplamasi(KesimNoVal)  '�n hesaplama
					Else
						S68E.GK_2 = "ONCEKI" 

						'***** son parti de�il ise masraflar� s�f�rla ****************
						For j = 1 To  S68T.GetRecCount
							S68T.MASRAFPAYIFIYATM(j) = 0
							S68T.MASRAFPAYITUTARM(j) = 0
						Next
						'********************************************************
					End If
				End If

				Set S68TDBGX = Nothing
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Function
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function EvraklarAcikmikontrolu(AcikEvrakNo)
	AcikEvrakNo = ""

	Call LazerKesimNoTanimliKayitlar(LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar, KesimNoVal)

	If Trim(LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar) <> "" Then
		EvrakNoArray = Split(LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar, "-")

		OncekiEvrakNoVal = ""

		For i = 0 to uBound(EvrakNoArray)
			OncekiEvrakNoVal = EvrakNoArray(i)

			EvrakAcik = False

			Set D7W = Doc.Select1("sef3332fs", "D7WLOCK", "LOCKDOC='STOK68E' & LOCKVOUCHER='"& Doc.RightJustify(Trim(OncekiEvrakNoVal), 200) &"' ", "", -1)	
			If D7W.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Function
			
			AcikEvrakNo = OncekiEvrakNoVal
			Exit For

			Set D7W = Nothing
		Next
	End If
	
End Function
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub FasonMaliyetHesapla(EvrakNoVal, OranVal, S63DB, Doc1)

	OncekiEvrak = False

	If Trim(EvrakNoVal) = "Mevcut" Then
		Set S68E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
		TarihVal = S68E.TARIH
	
		Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")

		Set FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET = Doc.GetTableObject("ZU_STOK68FM")
		FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.Empty

	Else
		OncekiEvrak = True

		Doc1.Load_Voucher EvrakNoVal

		Set S68E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
		Set S68T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK68T")

		Set FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET = Doc1.GetTableObject("ZU_STOK68FM")
		FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.Empty

		TarihVal = S68E.TARIH
	End If

	TanimsizUyariVer = False

	For i = 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S68T.REFTEXT02(i)) <> "" Then
			S68T.REFTEXT04(i) = ""
	
			FasonGelen_StokKoduVal = S68T.KOD(i)
			FasonGelen_StokAdiVal = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S68T.KOD(i), "")
			FasonGelen_MiktarVal = S68T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			FasonGelen_StokBirimiVal = S68T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo = S68T.REFTEXT02(i)

			FasonGelen_IcinTuketimPayi = OranVal * FasonGelen_MiktarVal
	
			'******** t�ketilen kodlar�n gelen fason �r�nlere da��l�m� ********************************
			If S63DB.GetRecCount > 0 Then
				For j = 1 To S63DB.GetRecCount
					FasonCikis_EvrakNoVal = Trim(S63DB.EVRAKNO(j))
					FasonCikis_TuketilenStokKodu = S63DB.KOD(j) 
					FasonCikis_TuketilenStokAdi = S63DB.AD(j) 
					FasonCikis_TuketilenStokBirimi = S63DB.IUNIT(j) 
			
					'*********************** t�ketilenin son sat�nalma fiyat� *************************
					SonSatinAlmaFiyatArray = Doc.SonSatinalma(FasonCikis_TuketilenStokKodu, TarihVal)
			
					If IsArray(SonSatinAlmaFiyatArray) Then
						SonSatinalmaBirim_Birim_TL_Fiyati = SonSatinAlmaFiyatArray(2)
					End If
					'******************************************************************************
		
					FasonCikis_HammaddeBirimTuketimPayi = OranVal * S63DB.HMBAZINDATUKETIMORANI(j)
	
					SatirNo = FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.AddRow
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
		
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.ACIKLAMA = "STOK63T- ( " & Trim(FasonCikis_EvrakNoVal) & " )"
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_KOD = FasonGelen_StokKoduVal
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_AD = FasonGelen_StokAdiVal
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_SF_MIKTAR = FasonGelen_MiktarVal
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_BIRIM = FasonGelen_StokBirimiVal
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_LAZERKESIMNO = FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo
		
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_KOD = FasonCikis_TuketilenStokKodu
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_AD = FasonCikis_TuketilenStokAdi
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_BIRIM_MIKTAR = FasonCikis_HammaddeBirimTuketimPayi
		
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_TOPLAM_MIKTAR = FasonCikis_HammaddeBirimTuketimPayi * FasonGelen_MiktarVal
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_BIRIM = FasonCikis_TuketilenStokBirimi
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_LAZERKESIMNO = FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo
		
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_TL_FIYAT = SonSatinalmaBirim_Birim_TL_Fiyati
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_REFTARIH = TarihVal
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_TL_BIRIMMALIYET = Doc.Div((SonSatinalmaBirim_Birim_TL_Fiyati * FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_TOPLAM_MIKTAR), FasonGelen_MiktarVal)
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_TL_TOPLAMMALIYET = FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_TL_BIRIMMALIYET * FasonGelen_MiktarVal
				Next
			Else

				'tan�ml� kesim noya g�re fason ��k�� fi�i yok 
 
				SatirNo = FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.AddRow
				FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
	
				FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_KOD = FasonGelen_StokKoduVal
				FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_AD = FasonGelen_StokAdiVal
				FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_SF_MIKTAR = FasonGelen_MiktarVal
				FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_BIRIM = FasonGelen_StokBirimiVal
				FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_LAZERKESIMNO = FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo
	
				FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.ACIKLAMA = "FASON �IKI� F��� YOK"
				FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_LAZERKESIMNO = "FASON �IKI� F��� YOK"
	
				'***** �r�n a�ac�nda t�ketilen tan�ml� m� kontrol� *************

				If UrunAgacindaTanimliMi(FasonGelen_StokKoduVal, "", EvrakNo, B01T) Then

					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.ACIKLAMA = "FASON �IKI� F��� YOK -BOMU01T - ( " & Trim(EvrakNo) & " )"
					FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_LAZERKESIMNO = "�R�N A�ACINDAN"
	
					If B01T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
	
						For x = 1 To B01T.GetRecCount 



							If x > 1 Then
								SatirNo = FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.AddRow
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
					
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_KOD = FasonGelen_StokKoduVal
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_AD = FasonGelen_StokAdiVal
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_SF_MIKTAR = FasonGelen_MiktarVal
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_BIRIM = FasonGelen_StokBirimiVal
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_LAZERKESIMNO = FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo
							End If
	
							FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_KOD = B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(x)
							FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(x), "")
							FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_BIRIM = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(x), "")
			
							SonSatinAlmaFiyatArray = Doc.SonSatinalma(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(x), TarihVal)
					
							If IsArray(SonSatinAlmaFiyatArray) Then
								SonSatinalmaBirim_Birim_TL_Fiyati = SonSatinAlmaFiyatArray(2)
							End If
			
							FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_TL_FIYAT = SonSatinalmaBirim_Birim_TL_Fiyati
							FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_REFTARIH = TarihVal
	
							UrunAgaci_BirimUretilenAdetIcinTuketilenMiktar = Doc.Div(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(x), B01T.BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR(x))
		
							FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_BIRIM_MIKTAR = UrunAgaci_BirimUretilenAdetIcinTuketilenMiktar

							FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_TOPLAM_MIKTAR = UrunAgaci_BirimUretilenAdetIcinTuketilenMiktar * FasonGelen_MiktarVal

							FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_TL_TOPLAMMALIYET = SonSatinalmaBirim_Birim_TL_Fiyati * UrunAgaci_BirimUretilenAdetIcinTuketilenMiktar * FasonGelen_MiktarVal
							FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_TL_BIRIMMALIYET = Doc.Div(FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_TL_TOPLAMMALIYET,FasonGelen_MiktarVal) 

						Next
					End If
				Else

					'***** �r�n a�ac�nda t�ketilen tan�ml� de�il ise stok kart�nda tan�ml� m� kontrol� *************

					If StokKartindaTanimliMi(FasonGelen_StokKoduVal, "", ZUS00) Then
						FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.ACIKLAMA = "FASON �IKI� F��� YOK - STOK00 - ( " & Trim(FasonGelen_StokKoduVal) & " )"
						FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_LAZERKESIMNO = "STOK KARTINDAN"
	
						If ZUS00.GetRecCount > 0 Then
							For x = 1 To ZUS00.GetRecCount 	
	
								If x > 1 Then
									SatirNo = FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.AddRow
									FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.SetCurrentRow SatirNo

									FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_KOD = FasonGelen_StokKoduVal
									FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_AD = FasonGelen_StokAdiVal
									FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_SF_MIKTAR = FasonGelen_MiktarVal
									FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_BIRIM = FasonGelen_StokBirimiVal
									FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_LAZERKESIMNO = FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo
								End If
								
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_KOD = ZUS00.HMSTKKOD(x)
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ZUS00.HMSTKKOD(x), "")
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_BIRIM = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", ZUS00.HMSTKKOD(x), "")
				
								SonSatinAlmaFiyatArray = Doc.SonSatinalma(ZUS00.HMSTKKOD(x), TarihVal)
						
								If IsArray(SonSatinAlmaFiyatArray) Then
									SonSatinalmaBirim_Birim_TL_Fiyati = SonSatinAlmaFiyatArray(2)
								End If
				
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_TL_FIYAT = SonSatinalmaBirim_Birim_TL_Fiyati
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_REFTARIH = TarihVal
	
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_BIRIM_MIKTAR = ZUS00.MIKTAR(x)
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_TOPLAM_MIKTAR = ZUS00.MIKTAR(x) * FasonGelen_MiktarVal

								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_TL_TOPLAMMALIYET = SonSatinalmaBirim_Birim_TL_Fiyati * ZUS00.MIKTAR(x) * FasonGelen_MiktarVal
								FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_TL_BIRIMMALIYET = Doc.Div(FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.FASONGELEN_TL_TOPLAMMALIYET, FasonGelen_MiktarVal) 

							Next
						End If
					Else
						FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.ACIKLAMA = "TANIMSIZ"
						FRMSTACKMAKROTABLET.TUKETILEN_LAZERKESIMNO = "TANIMSIZ"

						TanimsizUyariVer = True
					End If
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next

	GK2_SONPARTI = False

	For j = 1 To  S68T.GetRecCount
		S68T.MASRAFPAYIFIYATM(j) = 0
		S68T.MASRAFPAYITUTARM(j) = 0

		If OncekiEvrak Then
			Set FRMSTACKMAKROTABLETSTACK2 = Doc1.Select2G("ZU_STOK68FM_STACK2doc1", "ZU_STOK68FM", "FASONGELEN_KOD = '"& S68T.KOD(j) &"' & FASONGELEN_LAZERKESIMNO = '"& S68T.REFTEXT02(j)  &"' ", "", "FASONGELEN_KOD+FASONGELEN_LAZERKESIMNO", -1)
			
			Set S68S = Doc1.Select2("sss", "STOK68T", "KOD='"& S68T.KOD(j) &"' & REFTEXT02 <> '' ", "", -1)
			KodSayisi = S68S.GetRecCount  

		Else
			Set FRMSTACKMAKROTABLETSTACK2 = Doc.Select2G("ZU_STOK68FM_STACK2", "ZU_STOK68FM", "FASONGELEN_KOD = '"& S68T.KOD(j) &"' & FASONGELEN_LAZERKESIMNO = '"& S68T.REFTEXT02(j)  &"' ", "", "FASONGELEN_KOD+FASONGELEN_LAZERKESIMNO", -1)
			
			Set S68S = Doc.Select2("sss", "STOK68T", "KOD='"& S68T.KOD(j) &"' & REFTEXT02 <> '' ", "", -1)
			KodSayisi = S68S.GetRecCount  

		End If

		If FRMSTACKMAKROTABLETSTACK2.GetRecCount > 0 Then	
			S68T.MASRAFPAYITUTARM(j) = Doc.Div((FRMSTACKMAKROTABLETSTACK2.FASONGELEN_TL_BIRIMMALIYET(1) * S68T.SF_MIKTAR(j)), KodSayisi)
			S68T.MASRAFPAYIFIYATM(j) = Doc.Div(S68T.MASRAFPAYITUTARM(j), S68T.SF_MIKTAR(j))

			If S68T.MASRAFPAYITUTARM(j) > 0 Then
				GK2_SONPARTI=True
			End If
		End If

		Set FRMSTACKMAKROTABLETSTACK2 = Nothing
		Set S68S =  Nothing
	Next

	If TanimsizUyariVer Then
		msgbox "[ " & EvrakNoVal & " ] No' lu Evrakdaki [Da��t�lacak Maliyetler] Sekmesindeki " & vbcrlf & "Sac T�ketim Maliyet Tablosunda Tan�ms�z Sat�r/lar Var. Kontrol Ediniz."
	End If

	If OncekiEvrak Then
		S68E.GK_2 = "ONCEKI" 

		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Else
		If GK2_SONPARTI Then
			S68E.GK_2 = "SON" 
		End If

		Doc.Save_Voucher
	End If
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub FasonHammadde_Masraflarinin_OnHesaplamasi(FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo)
	Call EvraklarAcikmikontrolu(AcikEvrakNo)
	
	If Trim(AcikEvrakNo) <> "" Then
		msgbox "[ " & Trim(AcikEvrakNo) & " ] No' lu Fason Geli� Evra�� A��k." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Fason Hammadde Maliyet Da��t�m ��lemi �ptal"

		Exit Sub
	End If

	'*************** kesim noya ait toplam gelen miktar **********************************
	Set S68TDBG = Doc.Select1G("S68T_DB", "STOK68T", "KOD<>'' & REFTEXT02 = '"& FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo &"' ", "", "REFTEXT02", -1)

	If S68TDBG.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	ToplamFasondanGelenMiktar = S68TDBG.SF_MIKTAR(1)
	
	'*************** kesim noya ait toplam ��kan miktar **********************************
	dbYil = "OZAK_" & Year(Doc.Bugun)-1
	dbRef = "OZAK_" & Year(Doc.Bugun)

	sql = " SELECT STOK63T.KOD, EVRAKNO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, STOK63T.REFTEXT01, STOK00.AD, STOK00.IUNIT, "
	sql  = sql & " SUM(SUM(SF_MIKTAR)) OVER() AS TOPLAM_MIKTAR, "
	sql  = sql & " (SUM(SF_MIKTAR) / NULLIF(SUM(SUM(SF_MIKTAR)) OVER(), 0)) AS HMBAZINDATUKETIMORANI "
	sql  = sql & " FROM STOK63T "

	sql  = sql & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD = STOK63T.KOD "

	sql  = sql & " WHERE STOK63T.REFTEXT01 = '"& FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo &"' "
	sql  = sql & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO, STOK63T.KOD, STOK63T.REFTEXT01, STOK00.AD, STOK00.IUNIT  "
	
	Set S63DB = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dde34r55", sql)
	
	If S63DB.GetRecCount = 0 Then
		Set conn = CreateObject("ADODB.Connection")
		conn.ConnectionString = "Provider=SQLOLEDB;Data Source=MOZAN;Initial Catalog=master;Integrated Security=SSPI;"
		conn.Open
		
		sql = "SELECT name FROM sys.databases WHERE name = '" & dbYil & "'"
		Set rs = conn.Execute(sql)
		
		If rs.EOF Then
		   ' MSGBOX "Veritaban� BULUNAMADI: " & dbYil
		Else
			'ge�en y�la bak
			sql = " SELECT " & dbYil & ".dbo.STOK63T.KOD, EVRAKNO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, " & dbYil & ".dbo.REFTEXT01, STOK00.AD, STOK00.IUNIT, "
			sql  = sql & " SUM(SUM(SF_MIKTAR)) OVER() AS TOPLAM_MIKTAR, "
			sql  = sql & " (SUM(SF_MIKTAR) / NULLIF(SUM(SUM(SF_MIKTAR)) OVER(), 0)) AS HMBAZINDATUKETIMORANI "
			sql  = sql & " FROM " & dbYil & ".dbo.STOK63T "
	
			sql  = sql & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD = STOK63T.KOD "
	
			sql  = sql & " WHERE " & dbYil & ".dbo.REFTEXT01 = '"& FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo &"' "
			sql  = sql & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO, " & dbYil & ".dbo.STOK63T.KOD, " & dbYil & ".dbo., STOK00.AD, STOK00.IUNIT  "
	
			Set S63DB = Doc.RunSQLQuery("S63_DB", sql)
		End If
		
		rs.Close
		conn.Close
		Set rs = Nothing
		Set conn = Nothing
	End If

	OranVal = 0

	If S63DB.GetRecCount > 0 Then
		FasonCikis_ToplamTuketimMiktari = S63DB.TOPLAM_MIKTAR(1)
		
		OranVal = Doc.Div(FasonCikis_ToplamTuketimMiktari, ToplamFasondanGelenMiktar)
	Else
		'msgbox "[ " & Trim(FasonGelen_LazerKesimNo) & " ] No' lu Kesim Numaras�na Ait Fason ��k�� Fi�i Bulunamad�."

		'Exit Sub
	End If

	'*************** mevcut erak i�in maliyet hesaplama ********************************
	EvrakNoVal = "Mevcut"

	Call FasonMaliyetHesapla(EvrakNoVal, OranVal, S63DB, "")
	'************************************************************

	'********* �nceki fason geli� evrakler� ********************************************
	Call LazerKesimNoTanimliKayitlar(LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar, KesimNoVal)

	If Trim(LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar) <> "" Then
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK68", "")

		EvrakNoArray = Split(LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar, "-")

		OncekiEvrakNoVal = ""

		Sayac = 0

		For i = 0 to uBound(EvrakNoArray)
			Sayac = Sayac+1
			OncekiEvrakNoVal = EvrakNoArray(i)

			Call FasonMaliyetHesapla(OncekiEvrakNoVal, OranVal, S63DB, Doc1)
		Next

		Set Doc1 = Nothing

	End If

	Set S63DB = Nothing 

End Sub
'-----------------------------------------
Sub LazerKesimNoTanimliKayitlar(LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar, KesimNoVal)
	KesimNoVal = ""

	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")

	Set S68TSTACKG = Doc.Select2G("S68T_STACKG", "STOK68T", "REFTEXT02 <> '' ", "", "REFTEXT02", -1)

	If S68TSTACKG.GetRecCount = 1 Then
		LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar = ""

		KesimNoVal = Trim(S68TSTACKG.REFTEXT02(1))
		
		Set S68TDBG = Doc.Select1G("S68T_DBG", "STOK68T", "EVRAKNO <> '"& S68TSTACKG.EVRAKNO(1) &"' & REFTEXT02 = '"& KesimNoVal &"' ", "", "EVRAKNO+REFTEXT02", -1)
		
		If S68TDBG.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			For i = 1 To S68TDBG.GetRecCount 
				If i=1 Then
					LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar = Trim(S68TDBG.EVRAKNO(i))
				Else
					LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar = LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar & "-" & Trim(S68TDBG.EVRAKNO(i))
				End If
			Next

			S68E.ONCEKIKAYITLAR = LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar
		End If

		Set S68TDBG = Nothing

	ElseIf S68TSTACKG.GetRecCount > 1 Then
		msgbox "Farkl� Kesim Numaralar� Tan�ml�. Kontrol Ediniz."
	End If

	Set S68TSTACKG = Nothing

End Sub
'-------------------------------------------
Sub AfterLoad
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
		Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")

		If Trim(S68E.GK_1) = "LAZER" Then
			Call LazerKesimNoTanimliKayitlar(LazerKesimTanimliEvrakNolar, KesimNoVal)
		End If
	End If
End Sub
'-------------------------------
Function StokKartindaTanimliMi(FasonGelenStokKoduVal, TuketilenStokKodu, ZUS00)
	StokKartindaTanimliMi = False

	If Trim(TuketilenStokKodu) = "" Then
		Set ZUS00 = Doc.Select1("ZU_STOK00_DB", "ZU_STOK00", "MIKTAR > 0 & KOD = '"& FasonGelenStokKoduVal &"' ", "", -1)
	
		If ZUS00.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			StokKartindaTanimliMi = True
		End If
	Else
		Set ZUS00 = Doc.Select1("ZU_STOK00_DB", "ZU_STOK00", "HMSTKKOD = '"& TuketilenStokKodu &"' & MIKTAR > 0 & KOD = '"& FasonGelenStokKoduVal &"' ", "", -1)
	
		If ZUS00.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			StokKartindaTanimliMi = True
		End If
	End If
End Function
'-------------------------------------------------------------
Function UrunAgacindaTanimliMi(FasonGelenStokKoduVal, TuketilenStokKodu, EvrakNo, B01T)
	UrunAgacindaTanimliMi = False

	If Trim(TuketilenStokKodu) = "" Then
		Set B01T = Doc.Select1("BOMU01T_DB3", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_KAYNAK0 > 0 & AKTIF_PASIF = '*' & BOMREC_CODE = '"& FasonGelenStokKoduVal &"' & BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H' ", "", -1)

		If B01T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			UrunAgacindaTanimliMi = True
			EvrakNo = Trim(B01T.EVRAKNO(1))
		Else
			Set B01T = Doc.Select1("BOMU01T_DB4", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_KAYNAK0 > 0 & BOMREC_CODE = '"& FasonGelenStokKoduVal &"' & BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H' ", "", -1)
	
			If B01T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
				UrunAgacindaTanimliMi = True
				EvrakNo = Trim(B01T.EVRAKNO(1))
			End If
		End If

	Else

		Set B01T = Doc.Select1("BOMU01T_DB", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_KAYNAK0 > 0 & AKTIF_PASIF = '*' & BOMREC_CODE = '"& FasonGelenStokKoduVal &"' &  BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = '"& TuketilenStokKodu &"' & BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H' ", "", -1)
	
		If B01T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			UrunAgacindaTanimliMi = True
			EvrakNo = Trim(B01T.EVRAKNO(1))
		Else
			Set B01T = Doc.Select1("BOMU01T_DB2", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_KAYNAK0 > 0 & BOMREC_CODE = '"& FasonGelenStokKoduVal &"' &  BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = '"& TuketilenStokKodu &"' & BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H' ", "", -1)
	
			If B01T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
				UrunAgacindaTanimliMi = True
				EvrakNo = Trim(B01T.EVRAKNO(1))
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Function
'-------------------------------------------------------------
Function AfterNew()
	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
	S68E.TES_DEPOKODU="GKD"
	S68E.RED_DEPOKODU="IADE"
End Function
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function AfterCommand(cmd)
	If Trim(cmd)="CMD_STOK46_SEC2" Then
		Set S46T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T2")
		Set S68T=doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
		For i=1 To S46T.GetRecCount
			For j= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
				If Trim(S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2(i))<>"" Then
					If Trim(S46T.ARTNO(i))=Trim(S68T.SIPARTNO(j)) Then
						S68T.REFTEXT02(j)=Trim(S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2(i))
					End If
				End If
			Next
		Next
	End If
'Bu fonksiyonda STOK46 Sipari� Mod�l�ndeki Laser Kesim Numaras�n� otomatik STOK68 e eklemesini yap�yor. 
End function
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function AfterSave()
	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")

	TedarikciKoduVal = S68E.CHCODE
	CariGK5Val = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00", "GK_5", TedarikciKoduVal, ""))

If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "BT" Then
msgbox CariGK5Val
	exit Function
End If

	'******************************** F�RE TRANSFER ***********************************
	For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
		ENo=Trim(S68T.EVRAKNO(i))
		trn=Trim(S68T.TRNUM(i))

		Set S26T=Doc.Select1("sdsd", "STOK26T", "REFTEXT01='"& Eno &"' & REFTEXT02='"& trn &"' ","", -1)

		If S26T.getRecCount=0 and S68T.REFNUM01(i)>0 and Trim(S68T.QCONAY(i))="E" Then			
			Call DepoTransfer(i)
		End If 

		Set S26T=Nothing
	Next
	'*******************************************************************



	'Recep Deniz- Saclar i�in Kesim Numaras�na g�re giri� yaparken u�urmas�yla ilgili kod denemesi Ba�lang��
	'Recep Deniz STOK68 a��k iken ikinci bir i�lem yap�lmak istendi�inde de�i�kenler s�f�rlanmas� i�in yap�lan �al��ma Ba�lang��
	ss=1
	sayi3=1
	Enoo=""

	For i=1 to 100
		Eno63(i)=""
		KNo(i)=""
	Next 

	Set S63TT=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")

	If Doc.GetDocType="STOK68" then
		If CariGK5Val = "FASONSTKC" Then   ' cari kart�nda GK_5 de FASONSTKC tan�ml� olanlar i�in stok ��k�� fi�i olu�tur
			ENo=Trim(S68E.EVRAKNO)
			Set S68TT=Doc.Select2("_RAMTEMP", "STOK68T", "KOD<>'' ", "", -1)
			Ssay=S68TT.GetRecCount
			KNo(1)=""
			ss=1

			if Ssay>0 Then
				For i=1 to S68TT.GetRecCount
					if ss=1 then
						If KNo(ss)<>S68TT.REFTEXT02(i) and TRIM(S68TT.REFTEXT02(i))<>""  Then	
							KNo(ss)=S68TT.REFTEXT02(i)
	'msgbox "Kesim Numaras�" & ss & " " & S68TT.REFTEXT02(i)
	
							ss=ss+1
						end if			
					else
						If KNo(ss-1)<>S68TT.REFTEXT02(i) and TRIM(S68TT.REFTEXT02(i))<>""  Then
							KNo(ss)=S68TT.REFTEXT02(i)
	'msgbox "Kesim Numaras�" & ss & " " & S68TT.REFTEXT02(i)
							ss=ss+1
						end if	
					end if
				Next 
			end if
'Msgbox "Kesim Say�s�:"& ss-1

			Enoo=""
			sayi3=1
	
			For i=1 to ss-1
				Set S63T=Doc.Select1("TEMPEES_1","STOK63T","REFTEXT01='"&Kno(i)&"'","",1)
	'MSGBOX "Kesim Numaras�:" & Kno(i)
				if S63T.GetRecCount>0 then
	'msgbox "depo: " & TRIM(S68E.IMALATAMBCODE)
	'				if TRIM(S68E.IMALATAMBCODE)<>"FASON" then
	'msgbox "Evet Depo Fason depodan farkl�"
					For j=1 to S63T.GetRecCount
						if Enoo<>Trim(S63T.EVRAKNO) then
							Enoo=Trim(S63T.EVRAKNO)
							Eno63(sayi3)=S63T.EVRAKNO
	'Msgbox "Evrak No:" & Eno63(sayi3)
							sayi3=sayi3+1
						end if
					Next 
	'					end if
				end if
			next
'msgbox "Evrak Sayisi: " & sayi3
			For i=1 to Sayi3-1
				If sayi3-1>0 then
					kn=Eno63(i) & " - " & KNo(i) 
					Set S21TT=Doc.Select1("TEMPEES_2","STOK21T","REFTEXT01='"&kn&"'","",1)
	'					If S21TT.GetRecCount=0 then
					If S21TT.GetRecCount=0 and TRIM(S68E.GK_1)="LAZER" then
	'Msgbox 	"Fasondan u�ma i�lemi ba�l�yor..."
						Call Sarf_Cikis()
					End If
				End if
			Next
		End if
	End If

'Recep Deniz- Saclar i�in Kesim Numaras�na g�re giri� yaparken u�urmas�yla ilgili kod denemesi Biti�

	Set S68TT=Doc.Select2("dfsdf","STOK68T","KOD>'15'&KOD<'17'","",-1)
	If S68TT.GetRecCount>0 and Trim(S68E.GK_3)="" Then
		Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK47","")
		Set S47E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
		Set S47T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
		nrt=Doc1.New_Voucher()
		If nrt=0 Then
			ENo=Trim(S47E.EVRAKNO)
			S47E.CODEDBOLUM="YGS - PLN"
			S47E.CODEDKISI="0000000000000056"
			For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
				Skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(i))
				If Left(Skod,2)="16" and Trim(S68T.QCONAY(i))="E" Then
					Miktar=S68T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					Set BOMT=Doc.Select1("_TMEP","BOMU01T","BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='"+Skod+"'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'","",1)
					If BOMT.GetReCCount>0 Then
						FasonKod=Trim(BOMT.BOMREC_CODE)	
						Call FasonTalepAc(FasonKod,miktar)
						say=say+1
					End If
					S68T.AMBCODE_TR(i)="GLVELK"
				End If
			Next
			If say>0 Then
				If nret=0 Then
					nrt=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
					If nrt=0 Then
						nrtt=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
						S68E.GK_3="OK"
						Call MailGonder(Eno)
					End If
				End If
			Else
				nret=Doc1.Delete_Voucher()
				Set doc1=Nothing
			End If
		End IF
	End If
End Function
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub DepoTransfer(satir)
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	MpsNo=Trim(S68T.MPSNO(satir))

	KKontrol=Trim(S68T.QCONAY(satir))
	FireMiktar=S68T.REFNUM01(satir)

	skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(satir))
	lot=trim(S68T.LOTNUMBER(satir))
	seri=trim(S68T.SERINO(satir))
	asama=Trim(S68T.SF_VRI_RECETE(satir))

	If FireMiktar>0 and KKontrol="E" Then
		Call FireDepoyaTransferEt(skod,lot,seri,asama,FireMiktar)
	End If
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub BosLotlariDoldur()
	LastLotIndex = EnSonLot
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
			Tarih=replace(Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK68E.TARIH),"/","")
			Tarih1=Right(Tarih,2)
			Tarih2=Mid(Tarih,1,4)
			LotFormat=Tarih2&Tarih1
			If Trim(S29T.MPSNO(i))<>"" Then
				S29T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Trim(S29T.MPSNO(i))
			Else
				S29T.LOTNUMBER(i) = LotFormat & "-" & Right("000" & (i + LastLotIndex),3)
			End If
		End if
	Next
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function EnSonLot()
	EnSonLot = 0
	QStr = "TARIH='"+STOK68E.TARIH+"'&LOTNUMBER<>' '"
	Set S29T = Doc.Select1("asdad", "STOK68T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		Tire = InStr(1, S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
		if Tire > 0 Then
			EnSonLot = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub FireDepoyaTransferEt(kod,lot,seri,asama,Firemiktar)
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK26","")
	Set S26E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set S26T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26T")
	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")

	nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()

	S26E.AMBCODE=S68E.AMBCODE
	S26E.TARGETAMBCODE="FKAYIP"

	S26T.safepack

	satir=S26T.AddRow
	S26T.SetCurrentRow(satir)

	S26T.KOD(satir)=kod
	S26T.SF_SF_UNIT(satir)=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",kod,"")
	S26T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=FireMiktar
	S26T.SERINO(satir)=seri
	S26T.LOTNUMBER(satir)=lot
	S26T.SF_VRI_RECETE(satir)=asama
	S26T.REFTEXT01(satir)=Trim(S68T.EVRAKNO)
	S26T.REFTEXT02(satir)=Trim(S68T.TRNUM)

	nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()	

	If nret=0 Then
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	End If
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub MailGonder(Eno)

If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "BT" Then
	exit sub
End If

	Mailler="planlama@ozak-t.com" &";" & "satinalma@ozak-t.com" & ";" & "depo@ozak-t.com"  
	Mesaj = "Evrak No : " & trim(Eno) & " no'lu Fason Galvaniz talebi a��lm��t�r. " 
	Konu = "Talep No : " & trim(ENo) & " - ## NO'LU GALVAN�Z FASON SATINALMA TALEB�##"
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Konu
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=Mailler
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=Mesaj
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub FasonTalepAc(kod,miktar)
	satir=S47T.AddRow()
	S47T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
	S47T.KOD(satir)=kod
	S47T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=miktar
	S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satir)=Doc.Bugun
	S47T.REFTEXT02(satir)="�RET�M PLANLAMA"
End Sub' �lgili �r�n�n �r�n a�aca�ndaki sac�n son sat�nalma fiyat�na bak�lacak. form�lasyon ona g�re de�i�tirilecek.

Function BeforeSave()

	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
		Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
		Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
		Set S68TI=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68TI")
		FasonTipi=Trim(S68E.GK_1)
		If Trim(S68E.XIRSALIYETARIH)<>"" Then
			If Trim(S68E.REFDATE01)="" Then
				S68E.REFDATE01=S68E.TARIH
			End If
			S68E.TARIH=S68E.XIRSALIYETARIH
		Else
			If Trim(S68E.REFDATE01)="" Then
				S68E.REFDATE01=S68E.TARIH
			End If	
		End If
		If FasonTipi="" then
			MsgBox "Fason Tipi Se�ilmemi�.��lem �ptal"
			BeforeSave=-1
			Exit Function
		End If
		Dim say
		say=0
		Call BosLotlariDoldur()
		If FasonTipi="KUMLAMA" then
			For i= 1 to S68T.GetRecCount
				S68T.F_S_LOTNUMBER_10(i)="1"
			Next
		End IF
		For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
			Skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(i))
			SArtNo=Trim(S68T.SIPARTNO(i))
			Set S46T=Doc.Select1("__TEMP","STOK46T","ARTNO='"&SArtNo&"'","",1)
			If S46T.GetRecCount>0 then
				S68T.REFTEXT01(i)=Trim(S46T.NOTE0)
				S68T.REFTEXT02(i)=Trim(S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2)
			end If
			GK7=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",Skod,""))
			If GK7="" Then
				S68T.QCONAY(i)="E"
			End If
		Next
		If FasonTipi="GALVANIZ" Then
			Doc.RunCommand("CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_001")
		ElseIf FasonTipi="KUMLAMA" Then
			Doc.RunCommand("CMD_HMM_FASONDAN_GELEN_OLARAK_DOLDUR")
			For i= 1 To S68TI.GetRecCount
				S68TI.LOTNUMBER(i)=""
				S68TI.SERINO(i)=""
			Next
		ElseIf FasonTipi="MONTAJ" Then
			Doc.RunCommand("CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_001")
		End If
	End If
End Function

Function AfterSave()
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
		ENo=Trim(S68T.EVRAKNO(i))
		trn=trim(S68T.TRNUM(i))
		Set S26T=Doc.Select1("sdsd","STOK26T","REFTEXT01='"&Eno&"'&REFTEXT02='"&trn&"'","",-1)
		If S26T.getRecCount=0 and S68T.REFNUM01(i)>0 and Trim(S68T.QCONAY(i))="E" then			
			Call DepoTransfer(i)
		End If 
	Next

'Recep Deniz- Saclar i�in Kesim Numaras�na g�re giri� yaparken u�urmas�yla ilgili kod denemesi Ba�lang��

'Msgbox "Kesim Say�s�:"& ss-1

		if Enoo<>Trim(S63T.EVRAKNO) then

				sayi3=sayi3+1

'Recep Deniz- Saclar i�in Kesim Numaras�na g�re giri� yaparken u�urmas�yla ilgili kod denemesi Biti�


	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
	Set S68TT=Doc.Select2("dfsdf","STOK68T","KOD>'15'&KOD<'17'","",-1)
	If S68TT.GetRecCount>0 and Trim(S68E.GK_3)="" Then
		Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK47","")
		Set S47E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
		Set S47T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
		nrt=Doc1.New_Voucher()
		If nrt=0 Then
			ENo=Trim(S47E.EVRAKNO)
			S47E.CODEDBOLUM="YGS - PLN"
			S47E.CODEDKISI="0000000000000056"
			For i= 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
				Skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(i))
				If Left(Skod,2)="16" and Trim(S68T.QCONAY(i))="E" Then
					Miktar=S68T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					Set BOMT=Doc.Select1("_TMEP","BOMU01T","BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='"+Skod+"'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'","",1)
					If BOMT.GetReCCount>0 Then
						FasonKod=Trim(BOMT.BOMREC_CODE)	
						Call FasonTalepAc(FasonKod,miktar)
						say=say+1
					End If
					S68T.AMBCODE_TR(i)="GLVELK"
				End If
			Next
			If say>0 Then
				If nret=0 Then
					nrt=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
					If nrt=0 Then
						nrtt=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
						S68E.GK_3="OK"
						Call MailGonder(Eno)
					End If
				End If
			Else
				nret=Doc1.Delete_Voucher()
				Set doc1=Nothing
			End If
		End IF
	End If
End Function

Sub DepoTransfer(satir)
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	MpsNo=Trim(S68T.MPSNO(satir))
	KKontrol=Trim(S68T.QCONAY(satir))
	FireMiktar=S68T.REFNUM01(satir)
	skod=Trim(S68T.KOD(satir))
	lot=trim(S68T.LOTNUMBER(satir))
	seri=trim(S68T.SERINO(satir))
	asama=Trim(S68T.SF_VRI_RECETE(satir))
	If FireMiktar>0 and KKontrol="E" Then
		Call FireDepoyaTransferEt(skod,lot,seri,asama,FireMiktar)
	End If
End Sub

Sub BosLotlariDoldur()
	LastLotIndex = EnSonLot
	Set S29T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
			Tarih=replace(Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK68E.TARIH),"/","")
			Tarih1=Right(Tarih,2)
			Tarih2=Mid(Tarih,1,4)
			LotFormat=Tarih2&Tarih1
			If Trim(S29T.MPSNO(i))<>"" Then
				S29T.LOTNUMBER(i)=Trim(S29T.MPSNO(i))
			Else
				S29T.LOTNUMBER(i) = LotFormat & "-" & Right("000" & (i + LastLotIndex),3)
			End If
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Function EnSonLot()
	EnSonLot = 0
	QStr = "TARIH='"+STOK68E.TARIH+"'&LOTNUMBER<>' '"
	Set S29T = Doc.Select1("asdad", "STOK68T", QStr, "<LOTNUMBER", -1)
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		Tire = InStr(1, S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), "-")
		if Tire > 0 Then
			EnSonLot = Doc.CDbl(Mid(S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), Tire+1))
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function

Sub FireDepoyaTransferEt(kod,lot,seri,asama,Firemiktar)

	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK26","")
	Set S26E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set S26T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26T")
	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	nret=Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S26E.AMBCODE=S68E.AMBCODE
	S26E.TARGETAMBCODE="FKAYIP"
	S26T.safepack
	satir=S26T.AddRow
	S26T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
	S26T.KOD(satir)=kod
	S26T.SF_SF_UNIT(satir)=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",kod,"")
	S26T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=FireMiktar
	S26T.SERINO(satir)=seri
	S26T.LOTNUMBER(satir)=lot
	S26T.SF_VRI_RECETE(satir)=asama
	S26T.REFTEXT01(satir)=Trim(S68T.EVRAKNO)
	S26T.REFTEXT02(satir)=Trim(S68T.TRNUM)
	nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()	
	If nret=0 Then
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	End If
End Sub

Sub MailGonder(Eno)
	Mailler="planlama@ozak-t.com" &";" & "satinalma@ozak-t.com" & ";" & "depo@ozak-t.com"  
	Mesaj = "Evrak No : " & trim(Eno) & " no'lu Fason Galvaniz talebi a��lm��t�r. " 
	Konu = "Talep No : " & trim(ENo) & " - ## NO'LU GALVAN�Z FASON SATINALMA TALEB�##"
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Konu
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=Mailler
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=Mesaj
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"
End Sub


Sub FasonTalepAc(kod,miktar)
	satir=S47T.AddRow()
	S47T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
	S47T.KOD(satir)=kod
	S47T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=miktar
	S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satir)=Doc.Bugun
	S47T.REFTEXT02(satir)="�RET�M PLANLAMA"
End Sub


Sub Sarf_Cikis()
	
	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
'Msgbox "Kesim Numaras�n�n Gectigi Stok63 Evrak Say�s� - " & sayi3-1
For ii=1 to sayi3-1
	Set S63T=Doc.Select1("sdsdsd_01","STOK63T","EVRAKNO='"&Eno63(ii)&"'","",-1)
	If S63T.GetRecCount>0 Then
		Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK21","")
		Set S21E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")
		Set S21T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")
		nrt=Doc1.New_Voucher()
		If nrt=0 Then
			FisNo=Trim(S21E.EVRAKNO)
			S21E.NITELIK="FASON"
			S21E.AMBCODE=S68E.IMALATAMBCODE
			S21T.SafePack()
'Msgbox "S68 deki kesimlere ait S63 de Ka� Tane Kontrol Edilecek Sat�r Var? " & S63T.GetRecCount
'Msgbox "S68 de Ka� Farkl� Kesim Numaras� Var? " & ss-1

			For jj= 1 To S63T.GetRecCount
			For i68=1 to ss-1
				If S63T.REFTEXT01(jj)=KNo(i68) then
'Msgbox "U�acak Kay�t Bilgisi: i68="&i68 & "    jj=" & jj & "    ii=  "& ii & "Stok Kodu" &Trim(S63T.KOD(jj))
					Skod=Trim(S63T.KOD(jj))
					Miktar=S63T.SF_MIKTAR(jj)
					Lot=Trim(S63T.LOTNUMBER(jj))
					Seri=Trim(S63T.SERINO(jj))
					FoKod=Trim(S63T.FOKOD(jj))
					Asama=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_RECETE(jj))
					Num1=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_NUM1(jj))
					Num2=Trim(S63T.SF_VRI_NUM1(jj))
					satir=S21T.AddRow()
					S21T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
					S21T.KOD(satir)=Skod
					S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR(satir)=miktar
					S21T.LOTNUMBER(satir)=Lot
					S21T.SERINO(satir)=Seri
					S21T.FOKOD(satir)=FoKod
					S21T.SF_VRI_NUM1(satir)=Num1
					S21T.SF_VRI_NUM2(satir)=Num2
					S21T.KARSI_MH_A(satir)="000000000000000000000012"
				End If
			Next
			Next
			Doc1.RunCommand("HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL")
			S21E.NOTES=Trim(S63T.EVRAKNO)
			nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			If nret=0 Then
				nrtt=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			End If
		End If
	End If
Next

SUB KapBilgisiKopya()
	Set S70E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	Set S70T = Doc.GetRowSetObject("STOK70T")
	Set S70THP = Doc.GetRowSetObject("STOK70THP")

	' Sat�r F4 otomatik ilk sat�rdaki bilgileri yazma

	S70T.SafePack

	If S70T.GetRowCount > 1 Then
		S70T.SetCurrentRow(1)

		S70THP.SAFEPACK

		If S70THP.GetRowCount > 0 Then
			varThuTCode = S70THP.THU_TCODE(1)
			varThuId = S70THP.THU_ID(1)
			varPackagingTypeCode = S70THP.PACKAGINGTYPECODE(1)
			varPckId = S70THP.PCK_ID(1)
			varPckQuantity = S70THP.PCK_QUANTITY(1)
			varUBLPckBrand = S70THP.UBLTR_PCK_BRAND(1)
			varOuterPckId = S70THP.OUTER_PCK_ID(1)
			varTotalPackageQuantity = S70THP.TOTALPACKAGEQUANTITY(1)
			varTotGoodsItemQuantity = S70THP.TOTGOODSITEMQUANTITY(1)

			For i = 2 To S70T.GetRowCount
				S70T.SetCurrentRow(i)

				S70THP.SafePack

				row = 1

				If S70THP.GetRowCount = 0 Then
					row = S70THP.AddRow()
				End If

				S70THP.THU_TCODE(row) = varThuTCode
				S70THP.THU_ID(row) = varThuId
				S70THP.PACKAGINGTYPECODE(row) = varPackagingTypeCode
				S70THP.PCK_ID(row) = varPckId
				S70THP.PCK_QUANTITY(row) = varPckQuantity
				S70THP.UBLTR_PCK_BRAND(row) = varUBLPckBrand
				S70THP.OUTER_PCK_ID(row) = varOuterPckId
				S70THP.TOTALPACKAGEQUANTITY(row) = varTotalPackageQuantity
				S70THP.TOTGOODSITEMQUANTITY(row) = varTotGoodsItemQuantity
			Next
		End If
	End If
END SUB

Sub ServisFormuKapat()

	Set S70T=Doc.Select2("dsfsdf","STOK70T","REFTEXT02<>''","",-1)
	Set S70E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	S70T.GROUPBY("REFTEXT02")
	set doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("SRVS00","")
	Set SRV=Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVS00")
	For i= 1 To S70T.GetRecCount
		ServisNo=Trim(S70T.REFTEXT02(i))
		If ServisNo<>"" then
			nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(ServisNo)
			SRV.REFTEXT02="FATURALANDI"
			SRV.REFTEXT03=Trim(S70E.EVRAKNO)
			nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher
		End If
	Next
	If	nret=0 Then 
		doc1.LoadEmpty_voucher
	End If

End Sub

Sub SanalIrsOlustur()

	Set S70T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	Set S70E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	ENo=Trim(S70E.EVRAKNO)
	If ENo<>"" Then
		IrsNo="S"&ENo
	End If
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK60","")
	Set S60E=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set S60T=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	
	If Eno<>"" Then
		nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(IrsNo)
	End If

	S60E.AMBCODE="TKSR1"
	S60E.CH_KODU=S70E.CH_KODU
	S60E.EXTERNALEVRAKNO_E=ENo
	S60T.SafePack()
	For i= 1 To S70T.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S70T.REFTEXT02(i))<>"" Then
			satir=S60T.AddRow()
			S60T.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			S60T.KOD(satir)=S70T.KOD(i)
			S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satir)=S70T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S60T.SF_SF_UNIT(satir)=S70T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			S60T.REFTEXT03(satir)=Trim(S70T.REFTEXT03(i))
		End If
	Next
	Doc1.RunCommand("HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL")	
	nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	If nret=0 Then
		nret=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	End If
End Sub

Sub IBANHesaplar()
	Set S70NS=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70NTS")
	S70NS.Safepack
	Set S70E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	Set S70T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	SipNo=Trim(S70T.SIPNO)
	Set S40E=Doc.Select1("dsfsdf","STOK40E","EVRAKNO='"&SipNo&"'","",-1)
	
	Cari=Trim(S70E.CH_KODU)
	BorcBakiyesi=Doc.CariHesapToplamlariBA(Cari,"B")
	AlacakBakiyesi=Doc.CariHesapToplamlariBA(Cari,"A")
	If BorcBakiyesi>0 Then
		Bakiye=Doc.Numformat(BorcBakiyesi,12,2)
	ElseIf AlacakBakiyesi>0 Then
		Bakiye=Doc.NumFormat(AlacakBakiyesi,12,2)
	End If
	Efat=Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","CH_EINVOICE_10",Cari,""))
	If Efat="1" Then
			satir=S70NS.AddRow()
			S70NS.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="GARANT� BANKASI TL:"&"TR72 0006 2001 1480 0006 2023 64"
			satir=S70NS.AddRow()
			
			S70NS.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="HALKBANK TL:" &"TR08 0001 2009 4430 0010 2600 57"
			satir=S70NS.AddRow()
			
			S70NS.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="YAPIKRED�  TL:"& "TR26 0006 7010 0000 0005 2246 32"
			satir=S70NS.AddRow()
			
			S70NS.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="GARANT� BANKASI EURO:"&"TR36 0006 2001 1480 0009 0953 58"
			satir=S70NS.AddRow()
			
			S70NS.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="HALKBANK EURO:"&"TR16 0001 2009 4430 0058 0000 15"
			satir=S70NS.AddRow()
			
			S70NS.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="YAPIKRED�  EURO:"& "TR51 0006 7010 0000 0045 6314 72"
			satir=S70NS.AddRow()
			
			S70NS.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="D�NAMO ERP FATURA NO : " &Trim(S70E.EVRAKNO)
			satir=S70NS.AddRow()
			
			
			S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="��BU FATURA �DEN�RKEN TCMB D�V�Z SATI� KURU BAZ ALINMALIDIR."
			satir=S70NS.AddRow()
			S70NS.SetCurrentRow(satir)
			If AlacakBakiyesi>0 and BorcBakiyesi=0 Then
				S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="Bu Evrak Hari� Alacak Bakiyeniz : " & Bakiye & " TL "		
			ElseIf AlacakBakiyesi=0 and BorcBakiyesi>0 Then
				S70NS.NOTE1(satir)="Bu Evrak Hari� Bor� Bakiyeniz : " & Bakiye &" TL"		
			End If


	End If
	S70E.IDFBUNIT_ITEMCODE="IBAN"

Sub MuhasebeKoduOku()
	Set S70T=doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	Set S70E=doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	Nitelik=Trim(S70E.NITELIK)
	For i= 1 To S70T.GetRecCount
		Skod=S70T.KOD(i)
		If Trim(S70T.S_MUHKODU(i))="" then
			StokMuhasebe=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","FAT_MUHASEBECODE",Skod,"")
			If Trim(StokMuhasebe)<>"" Then
				S70T.REFTEXT01(i)=StokMuhasebe
				'msgbox "1"
			Else
				Set TBL=Doc.Select1("sdsds","TABL01T","STOKB_CODE<='"&Skod&"'&STOKE_CODE>='"&Skod&"'&OTHER_NITELIK='"&Nitelik&"'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'","",1)
				If TBL.GetRecCount>0 and Trim(TBL.STOKB_CODE)<>"" and Trim(TBL.STOKE_CODE)<>"" then
					If Trim(TBL.VALUE_MUHASEBECODE)<>"" Then
						S70T.REFTEXT01(i)=Trim(TBL.VALUE_MUHASEBECODE)
						'msgbox "2"
					End If
				Else
					Set TBL=Doc.Select1("TEMPERO","TABL01T", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&OTHER_NITELIK<>''","",-1)
					For j= 1 to TBL.GetRecCount
						If Trim(TBL.OTHER_NITELIK(j))=Nitelik and Trim(TBL.STOKB_CODE(j))="" and Trim(TBL.STOKE_CODE(j))="" Then
							If Trim(TBL.VALUE_MUHASEBECODE(j))<>"" Then
								S70T.REFTEXT01(i)=Trim(TBL.VALUE_MUHASEBECODE(j))
								'msgbox Trim(TBL.VALUE_MUHASEBECODE(j))
							End If
						End IF			
					Next
				End If
			End If
		Else
			S70T.REFTEXT01(i)=Trim(S70T.S_MUHKODU(i))
			'msgbox "4"
		End If
	Next
End Sub

	q1=Doc.d7MsgBox(mesaj,"dinamoERP",MB_ICONQUESTION+MB_YESNOCANCEL)
	If q1=6 Then 
		Set S70E=Doc.Select1("dsfdsf","STOK70E","EVRAKNO LIKE OZK","<EVRAKNO",1)

		End If
			Doc.SetUserSpecifiedNewVoucherNo YeniEFaturaNo

		If S70E.GetRecCount>0 Then
			YeniEFaturaNo=Doc.StrIncrement(S70E.EVRAKNO)
		Else

		End If
		Doc.SetUserSpecifiedNewVoucherNo YeniEFaturaNo

		End If

End Function


Function BeforeSave()

	If Trim(STOK70E.GK_1)="01" then
		Call ServisFormuKapat()
		FatNo=Trim(STOK70E.EVRAKNO)
		Set S60E=Doc.Select1("sadasd","STOK60E","EXTERNALEVRAKNO_E='"&FatNo&"'","",-1)
		If S60E.GetRecCount=0 Then
			Call SanalIrsOlustur()
		End If
	End If
	
	Set S70T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	For i= 1 To S70T.GetRecCount
		SysPosNo=Trim(S70T.SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO(i))
		Set S60T=Doc.Select1("ddsfsd1","STOK60T","SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO='"&sysposNo&"'","",-1)
		If Trim(S60T.REFTEXT03)<>"" Then
			S70T.SetCurrentRow i
			S70T.REFTEXT03(i)=Trim(S60T.REFTEXT03)
		End If		
	Next

	Set S70E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	Cari=Trim(S70E.CH_KODU)
	EFat=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","CH_EINVOICE_10",Cari,"")
	If Trim(S70E.NITELIK)<>"IR" Then
		If Trim(S70E.IDFBUNIT_ITEMCODE)="" then
			Call IBANHesaplar()
		End If
	End If
End Function

Function BeforeSave()
	Set S74E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK74E")
	ithno = Trim(S74E.ITHALAT_DOSYA_NO)
	If ithno<>"" Then
		Set S74T1=Doc.Select2("___tABLE74T1","STOK74T1","EVRAKNO='"&Trim(S74E.EVRAKNO)&"'","",-1)
		S74T1.GROUPBY("IRSALIYENO")
		For i= 1 to S74T1.GetRecCount
			irsno = S74T1.IRSALIYENO(i)
			Set S29EE=Doc.Select1("xfdsf","STOK29E","EVRAKNO='"&irsno&"'&ITHALAT_DOSYA_NO='"&ithno&"'","",1)
			If S29EE.GetRecCount=0 Then	
				Ithalat_DosyaNumarasi_Yazilmayan_Irsaliye=Ithalat_DosyaNumarasi_Yazilmayan_Irsaliye+1
			End If
		Next		
		If Ithalat_DosyaNumarasi_Yazilmayan_Irsaliye>0 Then
			Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK29","")
			Set S29E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
			For z=1  to S74T1.Getreccount
				irsno = S74T1.IRSALIYENO(z)
				If trim(irsno) <> "" Then
					retcode=Doc1.Load_Voucher(irsno)
					If retcode=0 Then
						S29E.ITHALAT_DOSYA_NO = ithno
						S29E.NITELIK="ITHALAT"
						retcode=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
						If retcode=0 Then
							nnn=Doc1.Loadempty_Voucher()
						End If
					End If
				End If
			Next
		End If
	End If
End Function

Function AfterSave()
	Doc.RunCommand("CMD_IRSALIYE_FIYATLANDIR")
End Function

Function BeforeSave()
	Set S74T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK74T")
	Set S74T1=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK74T1")
	Set S74E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK74E")
	FasonIrsFiyatYazdirildimi=Trim(S74E.REFTEXT01)
	If FasonIrsFiyatYazdirildimi="" Then
		For i= 1 To S74T.GetRecCount
			S74T.SetCurrentRow(i)
			IrsNo=Trim(S74T1.FI_IRSALIYENO)
			IrsArtNo=Trim(S74T1.FI_SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO)
			BirimFiyat=S74T.TL_FIYAT(i)
			Set S68T=Doc.Select1("dfhhd233","STOK68T","EVRAKNO='"&IrsNo&"'&SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO='"&IrsArtNo&"'","",-1)
			If S68T.GetRecCount>0 Then
				Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK68","")
				Set S68TT=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK68T")	
				nret=Doc1.Load_Voucher(S68T.EVRAKNO)
				If nret=0 Then
					For j= 1 To S68TT.GetRecCount
						S68TT.SetCurrentRow j
						S68TT.TL_FIYAT(j)=S68TT.TL_FIYAT(j)+BirimFiyat
					Next
				End If
				nret=Doc1.Save_Voucher()
				If nret=0 Then
					FasonIscilik=1
					nn=Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
					Set Doc1=Nothing
				End If
			End If
		Next
		If FasonIscilik=1 then
			S74E.REFTEXT01="Fason ���ilik Bedeli �rsaliyeye Yaz�ld�."
		End If
	End If
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : MAKR8S

Hangi Tablo icin : STOK83
Ilave Hangi Uygulamalar icin :

Kod : 
Sub AfterCommand(StrCmd)
	If StrCmd = "CMD_MMPS10E_KOPYA" Then
		Set S83T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
		
		For i = 1 To S83T.GetRecCount
			HmStokKoduVal = Trim(S83T.KOD(i))
			JobNoVal = S83T.JOBNO(i)

			If HmStokKoduVal <> "" Then
				Set M10T = Doc.Select1("fsdfs", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='"& JobNoVal &"'", "", -1)
				
				If M10T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
					BakiyeVal = M10T.R_MIKTAR0(1)- (M10T.R_KULLANILANKAYNAK(1) + M10T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN(1) + M10T.R_HM_ODONEMNET(1) - M10T.R_HM_IADE_EDILEN(1))

					S83T.REFNUM01(i) = M10T.R_MIKTAR0(1)
					S83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) = BakiyeVal
				End If
			End If
		Next

		S83T.SetCurrentRow 1
	End If
End Sub

Function HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi(YetkiKodu)
	HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi = False

	Set U12 = Doc.Select1("de443d44t", "UDEFT12", "KOD='" & Doc.Getd7UserName & "'& OZEL_HAK_KODU='"& YetkiKodu &"' ", "", -1)

	If U12.GetRowCount > 0 Then HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi = True
End Function

Function BeforeSave()
	If doc.dataentryMode="GUI" then
		Set S83T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
		Set S83E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
		IF Trim(S83E.MPSGIRISCIKISTYPE)="+" Then
			Doc.RunCommand("HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL")
			'VerenDepo=Trim(S83E.AMBCODE)	
			'For i= 1 To S83T.GetRecCount
			'	SKod=Trim(S83T.KOD(i))
			'	Lot=Trim(S83T.LOTNUMBER(i))
			'	Seri=Trim(S83T.SERINO(i))
			'	Set S01=Doc.Select1("dfgsdf","STOK01","KOD='"&Skod&"'&LOTNUMBER='"&Lot&"'&SERINO='"&Seri&"'","",-1)	
			'	If S01.SF_MIKTAR=0 then
			'		S83T.SetcurrentRow i
			'		S83T.KOD(i)=""
			'		S83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)=""
			'	End If
			'Next		
			For i= 1 To S83T.GetRecCount
				SKod=Trim(S83T.KOD(i))
				GK6=Trim(doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_6",Skod,""))
				If GK6="KYOK" then
					S83T.SetcurrentRow i
					S83T.KOD(i)=""
					S83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)=""
				End If
			Next		
		End If
	
	' *** KAPALI MPS PROSES�NE ATAMA YAPMAYI ENGELLEYEN BLOK ***
		ENo=Trim(S83E.EVRAKNO)
		Set S83E=Doc.Select1("fsfdd","STOK83E","EVRAKNO='"&ENo&"'","",1)
		If S83E.GetrecCount=0 then
			For i= 1 To S83T.GetRecCount
				Job=Trim(S83T.JOBNO(i))
				mpsno=Trim(S83T.MPSNO(i))
				Set MPST=Doc.Select1("dfdsfsd","MMPS10E","EVRAKNO='"&mpsno&"'","",-1)
				For j= 1 To MPST.GetRecCount
					If Trim(MPST.ACIK_KAPALI(j))="K" Then
						MsgBox "Kapal� i� emrine malzeme atamas� yap�lamaz.��lem iptal."
						BeforeSave=-1
						Exit Function
					End If
				Next
			Next
		End If
	' *** KAPALI MPS PROSES�NE ATAMA YAPMAYI ENGELLEYEN BLOK  SONU***
	'***************** HM DEPODAN BA�KA DEPOYA TRANSFER YETK�LEND�RME *********************
		Set S83E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
		Set S83T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	
		VerenDepo_Evrak_Val = Trim(S83E.AMBCODE)
		AlanDepo_Evrak_Val = Trim(S83E.TARGETAMBCODE)
	
		VerenDepo_Satir_Var = False
	
		For i = 1 to S83T.GetRecCount
			VerenDepo_Satir_Val = Trim(S83T.AMBCODE_TR(i))
			AlanDepo_Satir_Val = Trim(S83T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(i))
	
			If VerenDepo_Satir_Val = "HM" and AlanDepo_Satir_Val = "HM" Then
			Else
				If VerenDepo_Satir_Val = "HM" Then
					VerenDepo_Satir_Var = True
		
					Exit For
				End If
			End If
		Next
		
		If VerenDepo_Satir_Var Then
			If Not HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi("STOK83_HM") Then				
				MsgBox "[ HM ] Depodan Transfer Yetkiniz Yok." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Kullan�c� Tan�ml� Mod�l Hakk� Tan�m� : STOK83_HM"
				BeforeSave=-1
	
				Exit Function
			End If
		Else
			If VerenDepo_Evrak_Val = "HM" and AlanDepo_Evrak_Val = "HM" Then
			Else
				If VerenDepo_Evrak_Val = "HM" Then

					If Not HM_Depodan_Transfer_Yetkisi_Varmi("STOK83_HM") Then
						MsgBox "[ HM ] Depodan Transfer Yetkiniz Yok." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Kullan�c� Tan�ml� Mod�l Hakk� Tan�m� : STOK83_HM"
						BeforeSave=-1
			
						Exit Function
					End If
				End If
			End If
		End If
		'**************************************************************************************************
	End If
End Function

Public Mesaj, Enoo, Adepo,Bdepo

Function BeforeDelete()
'STOK83 Fi� silindi�inde mail atmas� i�in yaz�lm��t�r.
	Set S83E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set S83T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

Adepo=Trim(S83E.AMBCODE)
Bdepo=Trim(S83E.TARGETAMBCODE)
Enoo = S83E.EVRAKNO
Mesaj = ""

if S83T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
	For i= 1 To S83T.GetRecCount
		Mesaj=Mesaj & vbTab & "MPSNO:" & Trim(S83T.MPSNO(i)) & vbTab & "JOBNO:" & Trim(S83T.JOBNO(i)) & vbTab & "KOD:" & Trim(S83T.KOD(i)) & vbTab & "URUN ADI:" & Trim(S83T.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)) & vbTab & "LOT NO:" & Trim(S83T.LOTNUMBER(i)) & vbTab & "MIKTAR:" & Trim(S83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)) & vbTab & "BIRIM:" & Trim(S83T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)) & vbTab & "KULLANICI:" & Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName()) & " " & vbNewLine
	Next
End if

	If Trim(S83E.TARGETAMBCODE)<>"HM" and Trim(S83E.AMBCODE)="HM" Then
		MsgBox "Bu fi� silme i�lemine kapal�.��lem �ptal."
		BeforeDelete=-1
		Exit Function
	End if
End function

Function AfterDelete()
	If Adepo="HM" or Bdepo="HM" Then 
		Call MailGonder()
	End if	
End function

Sub MailGonder()
	Mailler="depo@ozak-t.com"  
	Konu = "Talep No : " & trim(Enoo) & " - ## NO'LU STOK83 FISI SILME ISLEMI##"
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E
	Dim EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E")
	Set EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T=Doc.GetTableObject("_EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T")
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E) then Exit Sub
	If Doc.IsBlank(EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T) then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_NONE_FORM"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.NOMESSAGE="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.Empty()
	RecNo=EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.AddRow()
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.SELECTED="1"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJSABLONDAN="0"
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_E.HITAP=Konu
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.M_TO=Mailler
	EMAIL_SEND_INFO_TABLE_T.MESAJ=Mesaj
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_EMAIL_SEND_ONE"
End Sub

Sub KOD_AfterValidate()
	Set T40E=Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL40E")
	Set T40T=Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL40T")

	If Trim(T40T.REV_RTESTARIH) = "" Then
		T40T.REV_RTESTARIH = T40E.TARIH
		T40T.RTESTARIH = T40E.TARIH
	End If

	SatirNoVal = T40T.GetCurrentRow

	If Trim(T40T.REFTEXT09) = "" Then T40T.REFTEXT09 = Right("00" & T40T.SRNUM, 2)  

	'For i = 1 To T40T.GetRecCount
	'	T40T.REFTEXT09(i)  = T40T.SRNUM(i)
	'Next

	'************** 01.12.2025 ****************************
	T40T.URUNSTKKODU = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", T40T.KOD, "URUN", 0))
	T40T.URUNSTKADI = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00", "AD", T40T.URUNSTKKODU, -1) 
	'***************************************************
End Sub

Function SF_MIKTAR_AfterValidate()
	Set T40E=Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL40E")
	Set T40T=Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL40T")
	Set T40TI=Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL40I")
	For i= 1 To T40T.GetRecCount
		T40T.PRICEUNIT(i)=T40E.TEKLIF_FIYAT_PB
	Next
	Liste=Trim(T40E.FIYATLISTESI)
	Miktar=T40T.SF_MIKTAR
	kod=Trim(T40T.KOD)
	If Liste<>"" Then
		Set S48T=Doc.Select1("111asdasd55","STOK48T","EVRAKNO='"&Liste&"'&KOD='"&Kod&"'&MIKTARB<='"&Miktar&"'&MIKTARE>='"&Miktar&"'","",-1)
		If S48T.GetRecCount>0 Then
			For j= 1 To S48T.GetRecCount
				Fiyat=S48T.PRICE(j)
				ListeFiyat=S48T.REF_NUMBER_1(j)
			Next
		Else
			Set S48T=Doc.Select1("222asdasd","STOK48T","EVRAKNO='"&Liste&"'&KOD='"&Kod&"'&MIKTARB<='"&Miktar&"'&MIKTARE='0'","",-1)
			For j= 1 To S48T.GetRecCount
				Fiyat=S48T.PRICE(j)
				ListeFiyat=S48T.REF_NUMBER_1(j)
			Next
		End If
		T40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_2=ListeFiyat
		T40T.OR_FIYAT=Fiyat
	End If
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Function AfterLoad()
	Set T40T=Doc.getTableObject("TEKL40T")
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then	
		For i = 1 To T40T.GetRecCount

			'************** 01.12.2025 ****************************
			T40T.URUNSTKKODU(i) = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", T40T.KOD(i), "URUN", 0))
			T40T.URUNSTKADI(i) = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00", "AD", T40T.URUNSTKKODU(i), -1) 
			'***************************************************

			Eno=Trim(T40T.EVRAKNO(i))
			TrN=Trim(T40T.TRNUM(i))
			Index=ENo&TrN
			ProList=Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40T",Index,"PRO",0))
			RblList=Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40T",Index,"RBLK",0))
			If Prolist<>"" Then
				T40T.FIYATLS(i)=ProList
			End IF
			If RblList<>"" Then
				T40T.ROADBL(i)=RblList
			End If
		Next	

		'***********************************************************
		Set ZUTEKL40T=Doc.getTableObject("ZU_TEKL40T")
		Set T40E=Doc.getTableObject("TEKL40E")

		IskontoTutari = 0
		IskontoluTutar = 0
		KdvDahilTutar = 0
		Toplam = 0

		Toplam_GH= 0
		IskontoTutari_GH = 0
		IskontoluTutar_GH = 0
		NetToplamTutar_GH = 0
		KdvDahilTutar_GH = 0

		For i = 1 To ZUTEKL40T.GetRecCount

			If Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", ZUTEKL40T.KOD(i), "URUN", 0)) <> "" Then
				'************** 01.12.2025 ****************************
				ZUTEKL40T.URUNSTKKODU(i) = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", ZUTEKL40T.KOD(i), "URUN", 0))
				ZUTEKL40T.URUNSTKADI(i) = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00", "AD", ZUTEKL40T.KOD(i), -1) 
			'***************************************************
			End If

			If Left(ZUTEKL40T.KOD(i), 1) <> "5" Then
				Toplam = ZUTEKL40T.OR_TUTAR(i) + Toplam
				IskontoTutari = ZUTEKL40T.OR_XISKONTO_TUTARI(i) + IskontoTutari
				IskontoluTutar = ZUTEKL40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(i) + IskontoluTutar
				KdvDahilTutar = ZUTEKL40T.OR_KDVDAHILTUTAR(i) + KdvDahilTutar
			End If

			Toplam_GH = ZUTEKL40T.OR_TUTAR(i) + Toplam_GH
			IskontoTutari_GH = ZUTEKL40T.OR_XISKONTO_TUTARI(i) + IskontoTutari_GH
			IskontoluTutar_GH = ZUTEKL40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(i) + IskontoluTutar_GH
			NetToplamTutar_GH = ZUTEKL40T.OR_KDVDAHILTUTAR(i) + NetToplamTutar_GH
			KdvDahilTutar_GH = ZUTEKL40T.OR_KDVDAHILTUTAR(i) + KdvDahilTutar_GH
		Next
	
		T40E.REFNUM_GH1 = Toplam_GH
		T40E.REFNUM_GH2 = Doc.Div(IskontoTutari_GH, Toplam_GH)  * 100 
		T40E.REFNUM_GH3 = IskontoTutari_GH
		T40E.REFNUM_GH4 = IskontoluTutar_GH  
		T40E.REFNUM_GH5 = KdvDahilTutar_GH 

		T40E.REFNUM_MH1 = Toplam
		T40E.REFNUM_MH2 = Doc.Div(IskontoTutari, Toplam)  * 100 
		T40E.REFNUM_MH3 = IskontoTutari
		T40E.REFNUM_MH4 = IskontoluTutar  
		T40E.REFNUM_MH5 = KdvDahilTutar 
	End If
End Function
'--------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave()
	Set TK40T=Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL40T")
	Set TK40E=Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL40E")
	FisTarihi=TK40E.TARIH

	'*********************************************************************************
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then	
		If Trim(TK40E.GK_1) = "YD" or Trim(TK40E.GK_1) = "YI" Then
			Doc.RunMacro0 "EFRM10.TEKL40_1", "cmd_OnCalismayi_Kopyala"
		End If
	End If
	'************* E�ER �n �al��ma tablosu bo� ise TEKL40T deki ref15 alan�n�ndaki 1 de�eri 0 olarak g�ncellenir.
	Set ZUT40TSTACK = Doc.Select2G("fsdf", "ZU_TEKL40T", "KOD<>'' ", "", "KOD", -1)
	If ZUT40TSTACK.GetRecCount = 0 Then
		For i = 1 To TK40T.GetRecCount
			TK40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_15(i) = 0
		Next
	End If
	'*******************************************************
'	For i= 1 To TK40T.GetRecCount
'		Kod=Trim(TK40T.KOD(i))
'		Miktar=TK40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
'		ListeNo=Trim(TK40T.FIYATLS(i))
'		If ListeNo<>"" and Trim(TK40T.SGK_3(i))="PRO" Then
'			PromosyonFiyat=FiyatBul(Kod,Miktar,ListeNo)
'			TK40T.SetCurrentRow i
'			TK40T.OR_FIYAT=PromosyonFiyat
'		End If
'	Next
	If Doc.DataEntryMode="GUI" Then
		If TRIM(TEKL40E.INCO_KOD)="CIF" OR TRIM(TEKL40E.INCO_KOD)="CFR" OR TRIM(TEKL40E.INCO_KOD)="CIP" THEN
			IF TRIM(TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_1)="" THEN
				MsgBox "Incoterms olarak CIF/CFR/CIP se�ildi�inde adres alan� bo� ge�ilemez. L�tfen Sevk Adresi alan�n� doldurun."
				Beforesave=-1
			END IF	
		End If	
'		Call PromosyonFiyatListesiniKaydet()
	End If

	For i= 1 To TK40T.GetRecCount
		TK40T.SetCurrentRow i

		If Trim(TK40T.KOD(i))<>"" Then
			If Trim(TK40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i))="" Then
				TK40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i)=FisTarihi
			End If

			If Trim(TK40T.REFTEXT09(i)) = "" Then TK40T.REFTEXT09(i) =  Right("00" & TK40T.SRNUM(i), 2)   
		End If
	Next

	TK40T.Sort("REFTEXT09")


End Function
'-----------------------------------------------------------
Function FiyatBul(Kod,Miktar,Liste)
	Set S48T=Doc.Select1("111asdasd","STOK48T","EVRAKNO='"&Liste&"'&KOD='"&Kod&"'&MIKTARB<='"&Miktar&"'&MIKTARE>='"&Miktar&"'","",-1)
	S48T.SafePack()
	If S48T.GetRecCount>0 Then
		For j= 1 To S48T.GetRecCount
			Fiyat=S48T.PRICE(j)
		Next
	Else
		Set S48T=Doc.Select1("222asdasd","STOK48T","EVRAKNO='"&Liste&"'&KOD='"&Kod&"'&MIKTARB<='"&Miktar&"'&MIKTARE='0'","",-1)
		For j= 1 To S48T.GetRecCount
			Fiyat=S48T.PRICE(j)
		Next
	End If
	FiyatBul=Fiyat	
End Function

Sub PromosyonFiyatListesiniKaydet()

	Set T40T=Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL40T")
	For i= 1 to T40T.GetReccount
		Index=Trim(T40T.EVRAKNO(i))&Trim(T40T.TRNUM(i))
		ProPriceList=Trim(T40T.FIYATLS(i))
		RblPriceList=Trim(T40T.ROADBL(i))
		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","TEKL40T",Index)
		Dim T_01
		set T_01=doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")
		If ProPriceList<>"" Then
			K=T_01.SetfieldValue("PRO",-1,ProPriceList)
		End If
		If RblPriceList<>"" Then
			K=T_01.SetfieldValue("RBLK",-1,RblPriceList)
		End If
			T_01.Exec "Save"
	Next
End Sub

Function SF_MIKTAR_AfterValidate()
	Set TK40T=Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL40T")
	Kod=Trim(TK40T.KOD)
	Miktar=TK40T.SF_MIKTAR
	ListeNo=Trim(TK40T.FIYATLS)
	RoadBl= Trim(TK40T.ROADBL)
	If ListeNo<>"" and Trim(TK40T.SGK_3)="PRO" Then
		PromosyonFiyat=FiyatBul(Kod,Miktar,ListeNo)
		TK40T.OR_FIYAT=PromosyonFiyat
		TK40T.OR_TUTAR=PromosyonFiyat*Miktar
		TK40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR=PromosyonFiyat*Miktar
	End If
	RBl= Trim(TK40T.ROADBL)
	If RBl<>"" Then
		RBlFiyat=FiyatBul(Kod,Miktar,RBl)
		TK40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_3=RBlFiyat
	End If
'	Doc.RunCommand("CMD_FIYATLANDIR_001")
End Function

Function FiyatBul(Kod,Miktar,Liste)
	Set S48T=Doc.Select1("111asdasd","STOK48T","EVRAKNO='"&Liste&"'&KOD='"&Kod&"'&MIKTARB<='"&Miktar&"'&MIKTARE>='"&Miktar&"'","",-1)
	S48T.SafePack()
	If S48T.GetRecCount>0 Then
		For j= 1 To S48T.GetRecCount
			Fiyat=S48T.PRICE(j)
		Next
	Else
		Set S48T=Doc.Select1("222asdasd","STOK48T","EVRAKNO='"&Liste&"'&KOD='"&Kod&"'&MIKTARB<='"&Miktar&"'&MIKTARE='0'","",-1)
		For j= 1 To S48T.GetRecCount
			Fiyat=S48T.PRICE(j)
		Next
	End If
	FiyatBul=Fiyat	
End Function

Function AfterSave()
	Deger=0
	Set UBL=Doc.GetRowsetObject("UBLDSP1T")
	For j= UBL.GetRecCount to 1 Step -1
		If Trim(UBL.ITM_SELLERS_ID(j))<>"" Then
			If Left(Trim(UBL.ITM_SELLERS_ID(j)),2)="58" Then
				UBL.DeleteRow_NOUI(j)
				Deger=1
			End If
		End If
	Next
	If Deger=1 Then
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	End If
End FunctionFunction AfterSave()
	Set UBL=Doc.GetRowsetObject("UBLDSP1T")
	Set UBLE=Doc.GetRowsetObject("UBLDSP1E")
	IrsNo=Trim(UBLE.SW_MODULE_VOUCHERNO)
	Set S60T=Doc.Select1("TEMP","STOK60T","EVRAKNO='"&IrsNo&"'","",-1)
	Deger=0
	If S60T.GetRecCount>0 Then
		For i= 1 To S60T.GetRecCount
			Skod=Trim(S60T.KOD(i))
			AmbalajKonsinye=Trim(S60T.FATURASIZMI(i))
			If AmbalajKonsinye="E" Then
				For j= UBL.GetRecCount To 1 Step -1
					IF Trim(UBL.ITM_SELLERS_ID(j))<>"" Then
						If Skod=Trim(UBL.ITM_SELLERS_ID(j)) Then
							If Left(UBL.ITM_SELLERS_ID(j),2)="58" Then
								UBL.DeleteRow_NOUI(j)
								Deger=1
							End If
						End If
					End If
				Next
			End If
		Next
	End If
	If Deger=1 Then
		Doc.Save_Voucher()
	End If
End Function


Function AfterSave()
	Set UBL=Doc.GetRowsetObject("UBLDSP1T")
	For j= UBL.GetRecCount to 1 Step -1
		'IF Trim(UBL.ITM_SELLERS_ID(j))<>"" Then
			If Left(Trim(UBL.ITM_SELLERS_ID(j)),2)="58" Then
				Deger=1
				UBL.DeleteRow_NOUI(j)
			End If
		'End If
	Next
	If Deger=1 Then
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	End If
End Function

Function BeforeSave()
	Set UBL=Doc.GetTableObject("UBLINV1E")
	Set UBL02=Doc.GetTableObject("UBLINV1E02")
	Set UBLV1TT=Doc.GetTableObject("UBLINV1TT")
	Set UBLV1T=Doc.GetTableObject("UBLINV1T")
	Set UBLV1T03=Doc.GetTableObject("UBLINV1T03")

	TaxTotalEvrak = UBL.TAXTOTAL

	Set SST1 = Doc.Select1("ssss", "SESS001T1", "UBLINV1E_VOUCHERNO = '"& UBL.VOUCHERNO &"' ", "", -1)
	
	'MSGBOX UBL.VOUCHERNO & VBCRLF & SST1.GetRecCount


	If SST1.GetRecCount > 0 Then

		If Trim(SST1.SOURCE_SWM_MODULE(1)) = "STOK70I" Then

'msgbox "1 : " & Trim(SST1.SOURCE_SWM_MODULE(1))

			Set S70EF = Doc.Select1("fsdEEfs", "STOK70EF", "EVRAKNO = '"& SST1.SOURCE_SWM_VOUCHER(1) &"' & EINV_TYPE = 'TEVKIFATIADE' ", "", -1)

'MSGBOX "2 : " &  SST1.SOURCE_SWM_VOUCHER(1) & VBCRLF & S70EF.GetRecCount

			If S70EF.GetRecCount > 0 Then

				Set S70E = Doc.Select1("fsdfs", "STOK70E", "EVRAKNO = '"& SST1.SOURCE_SWM_VOUCHER(1) &"' ", "", -1)
		
'MSGBOX  "3 : " &   SST1.SOURCE_SWM_VOUCHER(1) & VBCRLF& S70E.GetRecCount
			
				If S70E.GetRecCount > 0 Then
					'TevkifatOraniVal = S70E.TEVKIFAT_ORANI(1)
					'TLKdvlerToplamVal = S70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
					TevkifatOraniTutarVal = S70E.TL_KESINTI1(1)
					KDVliToplamTutarVal = S70E.CH_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
				End If

'MSGBOX  "4 : " &  vbcrlf & "TevkifatOraniTutarVal >0  = " & TevkifatOraniTutarVal & vbcrlf & "KDVliToplamTutarVal > 0 = " & KDVliToplamTutarVal  
				If  TevkifatOraniTutarVal >0 and KDVliToplamTutarVal > 0 Then

'MSGBOX  "5 : xxxx " 
					If TaxTotalEvrak > 0 Then
						TevkifatOraniTutarVal = TaxTotalEvrak
					End If

					UBLV1TT.TAX_AMOUNT = TevkifatOraniTutarVal
					UBLV1T.TAXTOTAL = TevkifatOraniTutarVal
					UBL.LMT_TAX_NCLUSIVE = KDVliToplamTutarVal
					UBLV1T03.TAX_AMOUNT = TevkifatOraniTutarVal
					'UBL.HTML_FILENAME_DSP = ""
					'UBL.PDF_FILENAME_DSP = ""
				End If
			End If
		End If
	End If

	If Trim(UBL.EINVOICE_10)="A" Then
		FtNo=Trim(UBL.SW_MODULE_VOUCHERNO)
		Set S70E= Doc.Select1("_aaa","STOK70E","EVRAKNO='"+FtNo+"'","",1)
		CariKod=Left(Trim(S70E.CH_KODU),6)
		Earsivmi=Trim(S70E.EINVOICE_10)
		If CariKod="120 02" and Earsivmi="A" Then
			Set S70EF = Doc.Select1("_VVVV","STOK70EF","EVRAKNO='"+FtNo+"'","",1)
	 		GonderimSekli=Trim(S70EF.EIS2_DELIVERY_METHOD)
			If GonderimSekli="KAGIT" Then
				MailAdresiVarmi=Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00","EMAIL",Trim(S70E.CH_KODU),"")
				If Trim(MailAdresiVarmi)<>"" Then
					UBL02.APCT_CNT_EMAIL="muhasebe@ozak-t.com"			
				End If
			End If
		End If				
	End If 
End Function
